    const tc = (Math.atan2(centroid[w] - cw, centroid[u] - cu) * 180) / Math.PI;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const r = Math.hypot(s[i * 6 + u] - cu, s[i * 6 + w] - cw);
      rmin = Math.min(rmin, r); rmax = Math.max(rmax, r);
      let dt = (Math.atan2(s[i * 6 + w] - cw, s[i * 6 + u] - cu) * 180) / Math.PI - tc;
      dt = ((((dt + 180) % 360) + 360) % 360) - 180;
      tmin = Math.min(tmin, dt); tmax = Math.max(tmax, dt);
    }
    d.center2 = [cu, cw]; d.rMin = rmin; d.rMax = rmax; d.radius = (rmin + rmax) / 2;
    d.thetaC = (tc + 360) % 360; d.thetaHalf = Math.max(Math.abs(tmin), Math.abs(tmax));
    let nStd = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) nStd += (s[i * 6 + 3 + k] - meanN[k]) ** 2 / n;
    if ((rmax - rmin) / d.radius < 0.01 && Math.abs(mN[k]) < 0.05) d.kind = "cylinder";
    else if (Math.sqrt(nStd) < 0.02) d.kind = "cone";
    else d.kind = "revolved";
  } else {
    // freeform: report radial/angular extents about the machine x-axis (useful for rotor parts)
    let rmin = Infinity, rmax = 0, tmin = Infinity, tmax = -Infinity;
    const tc = (Math.atan2(centroid[2], centroid[1]) * 180) / Math.PI;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const r = Math.hypot(s[i * 6 + 1], s[i * 6 + 2]);
      rmin = Math.min(rmin, r); rmax = Math.max(rmax, r);
      let dt = (Math.atan2(s[i * 6 + 2], s[i * 6 + 1]) * 180) / Math.PI - tc;
      dt = ((((dt + 180) % 360) + 360) % 360) - 180;
      tmin = Math.min(tmin, dt); tmax = Math.max(tmax, dt);
    }
    d.rMin = rmin; d.rMax = rmax; d.thetaC = (tc + 360) % 360; d.thetaHalf = Math.max(Math.abs(tmin), Math.abs(tmax));
  }
  return d;
}
function solve3(A: number[], b: number[]): number[] | null {
  const det = (m: number[]) => m[0] * (m[4] * m[8] - m[5] * m[7]) - m[1] * (m[3] * m[8] - m[5] * m[6]) + m[2] * (m[3] * m[7] - m[4] * m[6]);
  const D = det(A);
  if (Math.abs(D) < 1e-30) return null;
  return [0, 1, 2].map((c) => { const M = A.slice(); for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) M[r * 3 + c] = b[r]; return det(M) / D; });
}
const tolStr = (v: number) => (v <= 1.0001e-4 ? "1e-4" : lit(v, 5));
export function synthesize(d: PatchDesc, scope: Box, cad: Box, evalRule: (r: string) => number[]): Candidate[] {
  const out: Candidate[] = [];
  const push = (label: string, rule: string, robustness: "geometric" | "tag", note: string) => {
    if (out.some((c) => c.rule === rule)) return;
    let m: number[] = [];
    try { m = evalRule(rule); } catch { return; }
    const sib = new Set(d.siblings);
    out.push({ label, rule, matches: m, unique: m.length === 1 && m[0] === d.id, pattern: d.siblings.length > 1 && m.length === sib.size && m.every((x) => sib.has(x)), robustness, note });
  };
  const ext = (k: number) => Math.max(1e-4, (d.bbox.max[k] - d.bbox.min[k]) * 0.02);
  const range = (k: number) => `${AX[k]} in [${lit(d.bbox.min[k] - ext(k), 4)}, ${lit(d.bbox.max[k] + ext(k), 4)}]`;
  const isUnique = (r: string) => { try { const m = evalRule(r); return m.length === 1 && m[0] === d.id; } catch { return false; } };
  if (d.kind === "planar") {
    const k = [0, 1, 2].find((i) => Math.abs(d.meanN[i]) > 0.995);
    if (k !== undefined) {
      const ax = AX[k], sgn = d.meanN[k] < 0;
      const c1 = `normal.${ax} ${sgn ? "< -0.995" : "> 0.995"}`;
      const v = d.centroid[k];
      let c2: string;
      if (Math.abs(v - scope.min[k]) < 1e-6) c2 = `abs(${ax} - bbox.${ax}_min) < 1e-4`;
      else if (Math.abs(v - scope.max[k]) < 1e-6) c2 = `abs(${ax} - bbox.${ax}_max) < 1e-4`;
      else if (Math.abs(v - cad.min[k]) < 1e-6) c2 = `abs(${ax} - cad.${ax}_min) < 1e-4`;
      else if (Math.abs(v - cad.max[k]) < 1e-6) c2 = `abs(${ax} - cad.${ax}_max) < 1e-4`;
      else c2 = `abs(${ax} - ${lit(v, 5)}) < 1e-4`;
      let rule = `${c1} and ${c2}`;
      if (!isUnique(rule) && d.rMin !== undefined) rule += ` and r_yz in [${lit(d.rMin! * 0.98, 4)}, ${lit(d.rMax! * 1.02, 4)}]`;
      if (!isUnique(rule)) rule += ` and ${range((k + 1) % 3)} and ${range((k + 2) % 3)}`;
      push("Planar · axis-aligned", rule, "geometric", `Normal cone ${d.coneDeg.toFixed(2)}° → plane ⟂ ${ax}; anchored to ${c2.includes("bbox") ? "domain bbox" : c2.includes("cad") ? "CAD bbox" : "absolute offset"}.`);
      push("Normal only (loose)", c1, "geometric", "Drops the position term — expect overlaps with other coplanar-normal faces.");
    } else {
      const n = d.meanN.map((v) => lit(v, 4)).join(", ");
      const off = d.centroid[0] * d.meanN[0] + d.centroid[1] * d.meanN[1] + d.centroid[2] * d.meanN[2];
      push("Planar · oblique", `dot(normal, (${n})) > 0.995 and abs(dot(pos, (${n})) - ${lit(off, 5)}) < 1e-4`, "geometric", "Oblique plane: normal alignment + signed plane offset.");
    }
  } else if ((d.kind === "cylinder" || d.kind === "cone" || d.kind === "revolved") && d.axis) {
    const k = AX.indexOf(d.axis), u = AX[(k + 1) % 3], w = AX[(k + 2) % 3];
    const fn = `cyl_radius_${u}${w}`;
    const [cu, cw] = d.center2!;
    if (d.kind === "cylinder") {
      const r = d.radius!;
      let rule = "";
      for (const f of [0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01]) {
        rule = `${fn}(${lit(cu, 5)}, ${lit(cw, 5)}) in [${lit(r * (1 - f), 4)}, ${lit(r * (1 + f), 4)}]`;
        if (isUnique(rule)) break;
      }
      if (!isUnique(rule)) rule += ` and ${range(k)}`;
      push("Cylinder · radius band", rule, "geometric", `Normals ⟂ ${d.axis}-axis, Kasa circle fit r = ${(r * 1000).toFixed(2)} mm, centre (${lit(cu, 4)}, ${lit(cw, 4)}).`);
    } else {
      const nk = d.meanN[k];
      let rule = `abs(normal.${d.axis} - ${lit(nk, 3)}) < 0.03 and ${fn}(${lit(cu, 5)}, ${lit(cw, 5)}) in [${lit(d.rMin! * 0.98, 4)}, ${lit(d.rMax! * 1.02, 4)}]`;
      if (!isUnique(rule)) rule += ` and ${range(k)}`;
      push(d.kind === "cone" ? "Cone · normal angle + radius" : "Surface of revolution", rule, "geometric", d.kind === "cone" ? `Constant normal·${d.axis} = ${nk.toFixed(3)} → half-angle ${(Math.asin(Math.abs(nk)) / DEG).toFixed(1)}°.` : `Revolved about ${d.axis}; radius ${(d.rMin! * 1000).toFixed(1)}–${(d.rMax! * 1000).toFixed(1)} mm.`);
      if (!isUnique(rule)) push("Revolution · radius + axial window", `${fn}(${lit(cu, 5)}, ${lit(cw, 5)}) in [${lit(d.rMin! * 0.98, 4)}, ${lit(d.rMax! * 1.02, 4)}] and ${range(k)}`, "geometric", "Radius band and axial window only.");
    }
  } else {
    // freeform (blades, vanes, pylon skin)
    const region = `r_yz in [${lit(d.rMin! * 0.98, 4)}, ${lit(d.rMax! * 1.02, 4)}] and ${range(0)}`;
    if (d.siblings.length > 1) {
      push(`Pattern ×${d.siblings.length} · annular region`, region, "geometric", `Detected ${d.siblings.length} congruent siblings (rotational pattern, ${(360 / d.siblings.length).toFixed(1)}° pitch) — one rule covers all.`);
      push("This instance · angular sector", `${region} and ang_dist_yz(${lit(d.thetaC!, 2)}) < ${lit(Math.min(d.thetaHalf! + 1.5, 179), 2)}`, "geometric", `Sector about x-axis centred at θ = ${d.thetaC!.toFixed(1)}°.`);
    } else {
      let rule = `${range(0)} and ${range(1)} and ${range(2)}`;
      if (!isUnique(rule)) rule = `inside_box(${[...d.bbox.min.map((v, i) => v - ext(i)), ...d.bbox.max.map((v, i) => v + ext(i))].map((v) => lit(v, 4)).join(", ")})`;
      push("Freeform · bounding region", rule, "geometric", `Normal cone ${d.coneDeg.toFixed(0)}° (freeform) → enclosed by its padded bounding box.`);
    }
  }
  if (d.siblings.length > 1) push(`STEP tag pattern ×${d.siblings.length}`, `step_tag ~ "${tagPrefix(d.tag)}_*"`, "tag", "Survives re-meshing; breaks if CAD is re-exported with new tags.");
  push("STEP tag (exact)", `step_tag ~ "${d.tag}"`, "tag", "Fallback: binds to the AP242 face tag rather than a fragile integer id.");
  return out;
}
export { tolStr };

Next is the NGV-2 cooled-vane results case. It includes a real Hess–Smith panel solver, Karman–Tsien compressibility, BL/heat-transfer models, a structured 3D field grid, KPIs and the Pareto DOE.
Created src/cases/ngv.ts
Created src/cases/ngv.ts

// "NGV-2" cooled turbine vane section (2.5D) — Run A baseline vs Run B adjoint-optimized.
// Hess–Smith source+vortex panel method + Karman–Tsien + isentropic thermo + BL / cooling correlations.
// Produces CFDFLD03-like structured fields (i fastest) consumed by the GPU slice and the line probe.
import { nacaPt } from "../geom/edf90";
export interface Design { name: string; m: number; p: number; t: number; alpha: number; bumpU: number; bumpL: number; film: number }
export const RUN_A: Design = { name: "Run A · Baseline", m: 0.04, p: 0.4, t: 0.15, alpha: 3, bumpU: 0, bumpL: 0, film: 0.36 };
export const RUN_B: Design = { name: "Run B · Adjoint-opt (it. 37)", m: 0.047, p: 0.47, t: 0.138, alpha: 3, bumpU: -0.0045, bumpL: 0.0025, film: 0.46 };
const g = 1.4, R = 287.05, CP = 1250, PR = 0.71;
export const FS = (() => {
  const M = 0.3, T0 = 1700, p0 = 1.8e6, c = 0.05, span = 0.025, Tc = 850;
  const T = T0 / (1 + 0.5 * (g - 1) * M * M);
  const p = p0 / Math.pow(1 + 0.5 * (g - 1) * M * M, g / (g - 1));
  const a = Math.sqrt(g * R * T), U = M * a, rho = p / (R * T);
  const mu = sutherland(T);
  return { M, T0, p0, c, span, Tc, T, p, a, U, rho, mu, q: 0.5 * rho * U * U, Re: (rho * U * c) / mu, k: (mu * CP) / PR };
})();
export function sutherland(T: number) { return 1.716e-5 * Math.pow(T / 273.15, 1.5) * (273.15 + 110.4) / (T + 110.4); }
export function airfoil(d: Design, n = 110): { x: Float64Array; y: Float64Array } {
  const x = new Float64Array(n + 1), y = new Float64Array(n + 1);
  const hh = (xc: number, xh: number) => (xc <= 0 || xc >= 1 ? 0 : Math.pow(Math.sin(Math.PI * Math.pow(xc, Math.log(0.5) / Math.log(xh))), 3));
  for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) {
    const u = 1 - i / n; // clockwise: TE -> lower -> LE -> upper -> TE
    const [xc, yc] = nacaPt(u, d.m, d.p, d.t);
    const bump = u < 0.5 ? d.bumpU * hh(xc, 0.12) : -d.bumpL * hh(xc, 0.72);
    x[i] = xc; y[i] = yc + bump;
  }
  return { x, y };
}
export interface PanelSol {
  N: number; xs: Float64Array; ys: Float64Array; xm: Float64Array; ym: Float64Array; th: Float64Array; L: Float64Array;
  q: Float64Array; gam: number; vt: Float64Array; cp: Float64Array; cl: number; alpha: number;
}
function srcVel(xs: Float64Array, ys: Float64Array, th: Float64Array, L: Float64Array, j: number, px: number, py: number, self: boolean): [number, number] {
  const c = Math.cos(th[j]), s = Math.sin(th[j]);
  const dx = px - xs[j], dy = py - ys[j];
  const xl = dx * c + dy * s, yl = -dx * s + dy * c, l = L[j];
  const r1 = xl * xl + yl * yl, r2 = (xl - l) * (xl - l) + yl * yl;
  const ul = (0.25 / Math.PI) * Math.log(r1 / r2);
  const vl = self ? 0.5 : (Math.atan2(yl, xl - l) - Math.atan2(yl, xl)) / (2 * Math.PI);
  return [ul * c - vl * s, ul * s + vl * c];
}
export function solvePanels(geo: { x: Float64Array; y: Float64Array }, alphaDeg: number): PanelSol {
  const xs = geo.x, ys = geo.y, N = xs.length - 1;
  const xm = new Float64Array(N), ym = new Float64Array(N), th = new Float64Array(N), L = new Float64Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    xm[i] = 0.5 * (xs[i] + xs[i + 1]); ym[i] = 0.5 * (ys[i] + ys[i + 1]);
    th[i] = Math.atan2(ys[i + 1] - ys[i], xs[i + 1] - xs[i]); L[i] = Math.hypot(xs[i + 1] - xs[i], ys[i + 1] - ys[i]);
  }
  const al = (alphaDeg * Math.PI) / 180, M = N + 1;
  const A = new Float64Array(M * M), b = new Float64Array(M), At = new Float64Array(N * N), Bt = new Float64Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    const si = Math.sin(th[i]), ci = Math.cos(th[i]);
    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) {
      const [us, vs] = srcVel(xs, ys, th, L, j, xm[i], ym[i], i === j);
      A[i * M + j] = -us * si + vs * ci;
      A[i * M + N] += -vs * si - us * ci;
      At[i * N + j] = us * ci + vs * si;
      Bt[i] += vs * ci - us * si;
    }
    b[i] = -Math.sin(al - th[i]);
  }
  for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) A[N * M + j] = At[j] + At[(N - 1) * N + j];
  A[N * M + N] = Bt[0] + Bt[N - 1];
  b[N] = -(Math.cos(al - th[0]) + Math.cos(al - th[N - 1]));
  const x = gauss(A, b, M);
  const q = x.slice(0, N), gam = x[N];
  const vt = new Float64Array(N), cp = new Float64Array(N);
  let fx = 0, fy = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
    let v = Math.cos(al - th[i]) + gam * Bt[i];
    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) v += At[i * N + j] * q[j];
    vt[i] = v; cp[i] = 1 - v * v;
    fx += -cp[i] * -Math.sin(th[i]) * L[i];
    fy += -cp[i] * Math.cos(th[i]) * L[i];
  }
  return { N, xs, ys, xm, ym, th, L, q, gam, vt, cp, cl: fy * Math.cos(al) - fx * Math.sin(al), alpha: alphaDeg };
}
function gauss(A: Float64Array, b: Float64Array, n: number): Float64Array {
  for (let c = 0; c < n; c++) {
    let p = c;
    for (let r = c + 1; r < n; r++) if (Math.abs(A[r * n + c]) > Math.abs(A[p * n + c])) p = r;
    if (p !== c) { for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) { const t = A[c * n + k]; A[c * n + k] = A[p * n + k]; A[p * n + k] = t; } const t = b[c]; b[c] = b[p]; b[p] = t; }
    const d = A[c * n + c];
    for (let r = c + 1; r < n; r++) {
      const f = A[r * n + c] / d;
      if (f === 0) continue;
      for (let k = c; k < n; k++) A[r * n + k] -= f * A[c * n + k];
      b[r] -= f * b[c];
    }
  }
  const x = new Float64Array(n);
  for (let r = n - 1; r >= 0; r--) { let s = b[r]; for (let k = r + 1; k < n; k++) s -= A[r * n + k] * x[k]; x[r] = s / A[r * n + r]; }
  return x;
}
export function velocityAt(s: PanelSol, px: number, py: number): [number, number] {
  const al = (s.alpha * Math.PI) / 180;
  let u = Math.cos(al), v = Math.sin(al);
  for (let j = 0; j < s.N; j++) {
    const dx = px - s.xm[j], dy = py - s.ym[j], r2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    let us: number, vs: number;
    if (r2 > 16 * s.L[j] * s.L[j]) { const k = s.L[j] / (2 * Math.PI * r2); us = k * dx; vs = k * dy; }
    else [us, vs] = srcVel(s.xs, s.ys, s.th, s.L, j, px, py, false);
    u += s.q[j] * us + s.gam * vs;
    v += s.q[j] * vs - s.gam * us;
  }
  return [u, v];
}
const kt = (cp0: number) => { const b = Math.sqrt(1 - FS.M * FS.M); return cp0 / (b + ((FS.M * FS.M) / (1 + b)) * cp0 * 0.5); };
function thermoFromCp(cpc: number) {
  const p = FS.p + cpc * FS.q;
  const Me = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, (2 / (g - 1)) * (Math.pow(FS.p0 / p, (g - 1) / g) - 1)));
  const T = FS.T0 / (1 + 0.5 * (g - 1) * Me * Me);
  return { p, Me, T, U: Me * Math.sqrt(g * R * T), rho: p / (R * T) };
}
export interface SurfStation { xc: number; s: number; cp: number; Me: number; Ue: number; Tw: number; h: number; Nu: number; tau: number; cf: number; yplus: number; lam: number; rho: number; nu: number; utau: number }
export interface Surface { upper: SurfStation[]; lower: SurfStation[]; nodeVals: (key: keyof SurfStation) => Float32Array }
export function surfaceAnalysis(sol: PanelSol, d: Design, y1: number): Surface {
  const N = sol.N, c = FS.c;
  let st = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < N - 1; i++) if (sol.vt[i] < 0 && sol.vt[i + 1] >= 0) { st = i; break; }
  const rLE = 1.1019 * d.t * d.t * c, D = 2 * rLE;
  const ReD = (FS.rho * FS.U * D) / FS.mu;
  const hst = (1.14 * Math.sqrt(ReD) * Math.pow(PR, 0.4) * FS.k) / D;
  const Uc = 1 / (0.0012 / 22 + 1 / (2600 * Math.pow(d.t / 0.12, 0.7)));
  const build = (idx: number[]): SurfStation[] => {
    let s = 0;
    const out: SurfStation[] = [];
    idx.forEach((i, n) => {
      if (n > 0) s += 0.5 * (sol.L[i] + sol.L[idx[n - 1]]) * c;
      else s = 0.5 * sol.L[i] * c;
      const cpc = kt(sol.cp[i]);
      const th = thermoFromCp(cpc);
      const mu = sutherland(th.T), nu = mu / th.rho;
      out.push({ xc: sol.xm[i], s, cp: cpc, Me: th.Me, Ue: th.U, Tw: 0, h: 0, Nu: 0, tau: 0, cf: 0, yplus: 0, lam: 0, rho: th.rho, nu, utau: 0 });
    });
    for (let n = 0; n < out.length; n++) {
      const o = out[n], a = out[Math.max(0, n - 1)], b2 = out[Math.min(out.length - 1, n + 1)];
      const dUds = (b2.Ue - a.Ue) / Math.max(1e-9, b2.s - a.s);
      o.lam = Math.max(-2, Math.min(2, (o.s / Math.max(o.Ue, 1)) * dUds));
      const Res = Math.max(50, (o.Ue * o.s) / o.nu);
      const w = smooth(0.02 * c, 0.09 * c, o.s);
      const pg = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(1.5, 1 + 0.6 * o.lam));
      o.cf = (1 - w) * (0.664 / Math.sqrt(Res)) + w * 0.0592 * Math.pow(Res, -0.2) * pg;
      o.tau = o.cf * 0.5 * o.rho * o.Ue * o.Ue;
      o.utau = Math.sqrt(o.tau / o.rho);
      o.yplus = (o.utau * y1) / o.nu;
      const ht = 0.0296 * Math.pow(Res, -0.2) * Math.pow(PR, -2 / 3) * o.rho * o.Ue * CP;
      const hl = (0.332 * Math.sqrt(Res) * Math.cbrt(PR) * FS.k) / Math.max(o.s, 1e-6);
      const hs = hst * Math.exp(-Math.pow(o.s / (1.3 * rLE * 1.57), 2));
      o.h = Math.sqrt(hs * hs + Math.pow(w * ht + (1 - w) * hl, 2));
      const Taw = FS.T0 - (1 - Math.cbrt(PR)) * (FS.T0 - FS.T0 / (1 + 0.2 * o.Me * o.Me));
      const eta = d.film * Math.exp(-o.s / (0.12 * c));
      const Tae = Taw - eta * (Taw - FS.Tc);
      o.Tw = (o.h * Tae + Uc * FS.Tc) / (o.h + Uc);
      o.Nu = (o.h * c) / FS.k;
    }
    return out;
  };
  const upIdx: number[] = [], loIdx: number[] = [];
  for (let i = st + 1; i < N; i++) upIdx.push(i);
  for (let i = st; i >= 0; i--) loIdx.push(i);
  const upper = build(upIdx), lower = build(loIdx);
  const byPanel = new Map<number, SurfStation>();
  upIdx.forEach((i, n) => byPanel.set(i, upper[n]));
  loIdx.forEach((i, n) => byPanel.set(i, lower[n]));
  return {
    upper, lower,
    nodeVals: (key) => {
      const out = new Float32Array(N + 1);
      for (let k = 0; k <= N; k++) {
        const a = byPanel.get(Math.max(0, k - 1))!, b2 = byPanel.get(Math.min(N - 1, k))!;
        out[k] = 0.5 * ((a[key] as number) + (b2[key] as number));
      }
      return out;
    },
  };
}
const smooth = (a: number, b: number, x: number) => { const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (x - a) / (b - a))); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t); };
export interface KPI { cl: number; cd: number; ld: number; zeta: number; tmax: number; yplusMax: number; cpMin: number }
export function kpis(sol: PanelSol, surf: Surface, d: Design): KPI {
  const cfAvg = 0.074 * Math.pow(FS.Re, -0.2), FF = 1 + 2 * d.t + 60 * d.t ** 4;
  const cpMin = Math.min(...surf.upper.map((s) => s.cp));
  const rec = surf.upper[surf.upper.length - 1].cp - cpMin;
  const cd = 2 * cfAvg * FF * (1 + 0.1 * rec * rec);
  const cl = sol.cl * (1 / Math.sqrt(1 - FS.M * FS.M));
  const zeta = cd + 0.012 * cl * cl;
  const all = [...surf.upper, ...surf.lower];
  return { cl, cd, ld: cl / cd, zeta, tmax: Math.max(...all.map((s) => s.Tw)), yplusMax: Math.max(...all.map((s) => s.yplus)), cpMin };
}
/** Boundary-layer u+(y+) at a station: Reichardt inner law + Coles wake (Clauser-β from λ). */
export function blProfile(st: SurfStation, y1: number, growth = 1.18) {
  const kap = 0.41;
  const Res = Math.max(1e3, (st.Ue * st.s) / st.nu);
  const delta = 0.37 * st.s * Math.pow(Res, -0.2) + 1e-5;
  const beta = Math.max(-0.4, -1.6 * st.lam);
  const Pi = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, 0.8 * Math.pow(Math.max(0, beta + 0.5), 0.75)));
  const uplus = (yp: number) => {
    const y = (yp * st.nu) / Math.max(st.utau, 1e-6);
    const inner = Math.log(1 + kap * yp) / kap + 7.8 * (1 - Math.exp(-yp / 11) - (yp / 11) * Math.exp(-yp / 3));
    return inner + ((2 * Pi) / kap) * Math.pow(Math.sin((Math.PI / 2) * Math.min(1, y / delta)), 2);
  };
  const cells: [number, number][] = [];
  let y = y1 * 0.5, h = y1;
  for (let k = 0; k < 60 && y < delta * 1.6; k++) {
    const yp = (y * st.utau) / st.nu;
    const err = 1 + 0.035 * Math.sin(k * 1.7) * Math.exp(-k / 9);
    cells.push([yp, uplus(yp) * err]);
    y += 0.5 * h + 0.5 * h * growth;
    h *= growth;
  }
  return { cells, uplus, delta, Pi, deltaPlus: (delta * st.utau) / st.nu };
}
// ------------------------------------------------------------------ structured 3D fields
export const GRID = { nx: 132, ny: 76, nz: 8 };
export const DOMAIN = { min: [-0.5 * FS.c, -0.72 * FS.c, 0], max: [2.0 * FS.c, 0.72 * FS.c, FS.span] };
export const FIELD_NAMES = ["Cp", "Umag", "Ux", "Uy", "Uz", "p", "T", "Mach", "rho", "mu", "kappa", "p0loss", "k", "omega", "nut"] as const;
export type FieldName = (typeof FIELD_NAMES)[number];
export const MASK = 1e30;
export interface RunData { design: Design; sol: PanelSol; surf: Surface; kpi: KPI; fields: Record<FieldName, Float32Array>; geo: { x: Float64Array; y: Float64Array } }
function inside(px: number, py: number, x: Float64Array, y: Float64Array) {
  let c = false;
  for (let i = 0, j = x.length - 1; i < x.length; j = i++) if (y[i] > py !== y[j] > py && px < ((x[j] - x[i]) * (py - y[i])) / (y[j] - y[i]) + x[i]) c = !c;
  return c;
}
export function computeRun(d: Design, y1: number): RunData {
  const geo = airfoil(d);
  const sol = solvePanels(geo, d.alpha);
  const surf = surfaceAnalysis(sol, d, y1);
  const kpi = kpis(sol, surf, d);
  const { nx, ny, nz } = GRID, n = nx * ny * nz, c = FS.c;
  const F = Object.fromEntries(FIELD_NAMES.map((k) => [k, new Float32Array(n)])) as Record<FieldName, Float32Array>;
  const al = (d.alpha * Math.PI) / 180, yTE = geo.y[0];
  const dEw = 0.16 * FS.span;
  for (let j = 0; j < ny; j++)
    for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
      const X = DOMAIN.min[0] + ((DOMAIN.max[0] - DOMAIN.min[0]) * i) / (nx - 1);
      const Y = DOMAIN.min[1] + ((DOMAIN.max[1] - DOMAIN.min[1]) * j) / (ny - 1);
      const xc = X / c, yc = Y / c;
      const masked = xc > -0.01 && xc < 1.01 && inside(xc, yc, geo.x, geo.y);
      let u = 0, v = 0, wk = 0, sig = 0.02;
      if (!masked) {
        [u, v] = velocityAt(sol, xc, yc);
        const sp = Math.hypot(u, v);
        if (sp > 2.6) { u *= 2.6 / sp; v *= 2.6 / sp; }
        if (xc > 0.985) {
          const dx = xc - 1;
          sig = 0.012 + 0.07 * dx + 0.03 * Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, dx));
          const yw = yTE + dx * Math.tan(al);
          wk = ((kpi.cd * 0.5) / (Math.sqrt(2 * Math.PI) * sig)) * Math.exp(-0.5 * ((yc - yw) / sig) ** 2) * smooth(0.985, 1.01, xc);
        }
      }
      const V2 = u * u + v * v;
      const th = thermoFromCp(kt(1 - V2));
      for (let k = 0; k < nz; k++) {
        const id = i + nx * (j + ny * k);
        if (masked) { for (const f of FIELD_NAMES) F[f][id] = MASK; continue; }
        const Z = DOMAIN.min[2] + ((DOMAIN.max[2] - DOMAIN.min[2]) * k) / (nz - 1);
        const dz = Math.min(Z, FS.span - Z);
        const fz = Math.min(1, Math.pow(Math.max(dz, 0) / dEw, 1 / 7));
        const sp = Math.sqrt(V2) || 1;
        const Umag = th.U * (1 - wk) * fz;
        const T = FS.T0 - (Umag * Umag) / (2 * CP);
        const a = Math.sqrt(g * R * T), Mach = Umag / a;
        const p0 = th.p * Math.pow(1 + 0.5 * (g - 1) * Mach * Mach, g / (g - 1));
        const rho = th.p / (R * T), mu = sutherland(T);
        const kk = 1.5 * (0.04 * FS.U) ** 2 + (wk * 0.9 * FS.U) ** 2 + (1 - fz) * (0.12 * FS.U) ** 2;
        const lm = 0.07 * sig * c + 0.002 * c;
        const eps = Math.pow(kk, 1.5) / lm;
        F.Cp[id] = (th.p - FS.p) / FS.q; F.Umag[id] = Umag; F.Ux[id] = (Umag * u) / sp; F.Uy[id] = (Umag * v) / sp;
        F.Uz[id] = 0.015 * Umag * (1 - fz) * Math.sign(Z - FS.span / 2) * Math.sin(Math.PI * Math.min(1, Math.abs(yc) * 4));
        F.p[id] = th.p; F.T[id] = T; F.Mach[id] = Mach; F.rho[id] = rho; F.mu[id] = mu; F.kappa[id] = (mu * CP) / PR;
        F.p0loss[id] = (FS.p0 - p0) / (FS.p0 - FS.p); F.k[id] = kk; F.omega[id] = eps / (0.09 * kk); F.nut[id] = (0.09 * kk * kk) / eps;
      }
    }
  return { design: d, sol, surf, kpi, fields: F, geo };
}
/** CPU trilinear sampler (same math as the WGSL `sample_trilinear`). Returns NaN in masked cells. */
export function sampleTri(f: Float32Array, p: number[]): number {
  const { nx, ny, nz } = GRID;
  const gx = ((p[0] - DOMAIN.min[0]) / (DOMAIN.max[0] - DOMAIN.min[0])) * (nx - 1);
  const gy = ((p[1] - DOMAIN.min[1]) / (DOMAIN.max[1] - DOMAIN.min[1])) * (ny - 1);
  const gz = ((p[2] - DOMAIN.min[2]) / (DOMAIN.max[2] - DOMAIN.min[2])) * (nz - 1);
  if (gx < 0 || gy < 0 || gz < 0 || gx > nx - 1 || gy > ny - 1 || gz > nz - 1) return NaN;
  const i = Math.min(nx - 2, Math.floor(gx)), j = Math.min(ny - 2, Math.floor(gy)), k = Math.min(nz - 2, Math.floor(gz));
  const fx = gx - i, fy = gy - j, fz = gz - k;
  let s = 0;
  for (let c = 0; c < 8; c++) {
    const di = c & 1, dj = (c >> 1) & 1, dk = (c >> 2) & 1;
    const v = f[i + di + nx * (j + dj + ny * (k + dk))];
    if (v > 1e29) return NaN;
    s += v * (di ? fx : 1 - fx) * (dj ? fy : 1 - fy) * (dk ? fz : 1 - fz);
  }
  return s;
}
// ------------------------------------------------------------------ DOE / Pareto / polars
export interface DoePt { m: number; p: number; t: number; cd: number; tmax: number; cl: number; feasible: boolean; pareto: boolean }
export const CL_REQ = 0.78;
export function runDoe(n = 84): DoePt[] {
  let seed = 1234567;
  const rnd = () => ((seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647);
  const pts: DoePt[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const d: Design = { name: "doe", m: 0.015 + 0.06 * rnd(), p: 0.3 + 0.22 * rnd(), t: 0.095 + 0.1 * rnd(), alpha: 3, bumpU: 0, bumpL: 0, film: 0.36 + 0.12 * rnd() };
    const sol = solvePanels(airfoil(d, 70), 3);
    const k = kpis(sol, surfaceAnalysis(sol, d, 1.5e-6), d);
    pts.push({ m: d.m, p: d.p, t: d.t, cd: k.cd * 1e4, tmax: k.tmax, cl: k.cl, feasible: k.cl >= CL_REQ, pareto: false });
  }
  for (const a of pts) a.pareto = a.feasible && !pts.some((b) => b !== a && b.feasible && b.cd <= a.cd && b.tmax <= a.tmax && (b.cd < a.cd || b.tmax < a.tmax));
  return pts;
}
export function dragPolar(d: Design): { a: number; cl: number; cd: number }[] {
  const out: { a: number; cl: number; cd: number }[] = [];
  const geo = airfoil(d, 80);
  for (let a = -2; a <= 10; a += 1) {
    const dd = { ...d, alpha: a };
    const sol = solvePanels(geo, a);
    const k = kpis(sol, surfaceAnalysis(sol, dd, 1.5e-6), dd);
    out.push({ a, cl: k.cl, cd: k.cd * 1e4 });
  }
  return out;
}

Created src/calc/units.ts

// 7-element SI dimension vector [kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd] with rational (half-integer) exponents.
export type Dim = number[];
export const BASE = ["kg", "m", "s", "K", "mol", "A", "cd"];
export const D = (kg = 0, m = 0, s = 0, K = 0, mol = 0, A = 0, cd = 0): Dim => [kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd];
export const ONE = D();
const r = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 1e6) / 1e6;
export const dmul = (a: Dim, b: Dim): Dim => a.map((v, i) => r(v + b[i]));
export const ddiv = (a: Dim, b: Dim): Dim => a.map((v, i) => r(v - b[i]));
export const dpow = (a: Dim, e: number): Dim => a.map((v) => r(v * e));
export const deq = (a: Dim, b: Dim) => a.every((v, i) => Math.abs(v - b[i]) < 1e-9);
export const isOne = (a: Dim) => deq(a, ONE);
const SUP: Record<string, string> = { "0": "⁰", "1": "¹", "2": "²", "3": "³", "4": "⁴", "5": "⁵", "6": "⁶", "7": "⁷", "8": "⁸", "9": "⁹", "-": "⁻", "/": "ᐟ" };
function supExp(e: number): string {
  if (e === 1) return "";
  let s: string;
  if (Number.isInteger(e)) s = String(e);
  else {
    const den = [2, 3, 4, 6].find((d) => Math.abs(e * d - Math.round(e * d)) < 1e-6) ?? 0;
    s = den ? `${Math.round(e * den)}/${den}` : e.toFixed(2);
  }
  return s.split("").map((c) => SUP[c] ?? c).join("");
}
export function fmtDim(d: Dim): string {
  const parts = BASE.map((b, i) => (Math.abs(d[i]) < 1e-9 ? "" : b + supExp(d[i]))).filter(Boolean);
  return parts.length ? parts.join("·") : "1";
}
export const fmtVec = (d: Dim) => `[${d.map((v) => (Number.isInteger(v) ? String(v) : v.toFixed(1))).join(", ")}]`;
export const QUANTITIES: { name: string; dim: Dim }[] = [
  { name: "Dimensionless", dim: D() },
  { name: "Pressure", dim: D(1, -1, -2) },
  { name: "Density", dim: D(1, -3) },
  { name: "Velocity", dim: D(0, 1, -1) },
  { name: "Specific Kinetic Energy", dim: D(0, 2, -2) },
  { name: "Temperature", dim: D(0, 0, 0, 1) },
  { name: "Dynamic Viscosity", dim: D(1, -1, -1) },
  { name: "Kinematic Viscosity", dim: D(0, 2, -1) },
  { name: "Thermal Conductivity", dim: D(1, 1, -3, -1) },
  { name: "Specific Heat / Gas Constant", dim: D(0, 2, -2, -1) },
  { name: "Velocity Gradient (Frequency)", dim: D(0, 0, -1) },
  { name: "Q-criterion / Strain Rate²", dim: D(0, 0, -2) },
  { name: "Temperature Gradient", dim: D(0, -1, 0, 1) },
  { name: "Pressure Gradient", dim: D(1, -2, -2) },
  { name: "Entropy Generation Rate", dim: D(1, -1, -3, -1) },
  { name: "Power Density", dim: D(1, -1, -3) },
  { name: "Dissipation Rate", dim: D(0, 2, -3) },
  { name: "Acceleration", dim: D(0, 1, -2) },
  { name: "Mass Flux", dim: D(1, -2, -1) },
  { name: "Heat Flux", dim: D(1, 0, -3) },
  { name: "Force", dim: D(1, 1, -2) },
  { name: "Energy", dim: D(1, 2, -2) },
  { name: "Length", dim: D(0, 1) },
  { name: "Time", dim: D(0, 0, 1) },
  { name: "Mass", dim: D(1) },
  { name: "Area", dim: D(0, 2) },
  { name: "Volume", dim: D(0, 3) },
  { name: "Universal Gas Constant", dim: D(1, 2, -2, -1, -1) },
  { name: "Sqrt Temperature", dim: D(0, 0, 0, 0.5) },
];
export const qname = (d: Dim) => QUANTITIES.find((q) => deq(q.dim, d))?.name ?? null;
export const describeDim = (d: Dim) => `${qname(d) ?? "Quantity"} [${fmtDim(d)}]`;
/** Unit-annotation parser for literals like 287.05[J/(kg*K)] or 9.81[m/s^2]. */
const UNITS: Record<string, [number, Dim]> = {
  kg: [1, D(1)], g: [1e-3, D(1)], m: [1, D(0, 1)], mm: [1e-3, D(0, 1)], cm: [1e-2, D(0, 1)], km: [1e3, D(0, 1)], um: [1e-6, D(0, 1)],
  s: [1, D(0, 0, 1)], ms: [1e-3, D(0, 0, 1)], min: [60, D(0, 0, 1)], h: [3600, D(0, 0, 1)], K: [1, D(0, 0, 0, 1)], mol: [1, D(0, 0, 0, 0, 1)],
  A: [1, D(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)], cd: [1, D(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1)], N: [1, D(1, 1, -2)], Pa: [1, D(1, -1, -2)], kPa: [1e3, D(1, -1, -2)],
  MPa: [1e6, D(1, -1, -2)], bar: [1e5, D(1, -1, -2)], J: [1, D(1, 2, -2)], W: [1, D(1, 2, -3)], Hz: [1, D(0, 0, -1)], rpm: [1 / 60, D(0, 0, -1)], "1": [1, D()],
};
export function parseUnit(src: string): { scale: number; dim: Dim } {
  let i = 0;
  const s = src.replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(/·/g, "*");
  const factor = (): { scale: number; dim: Dim } => {
    if (s[i] === "(") { i++; const v = expr(); if (s[i] !== ")") throw new Error(`unit: expected ')' in [${src}]`); i++; return pw(v); }
    const m = /^[A-Za-z]+|^1/.exec(s.slice(i));
    if (!m) throw new Error(`unit: unexpected '${s[i] ?? "end"}' in [${src}]`);
    i += m[0].length;
    const u = UNITS[m[0]];
    if (!u) throw new Error(`unit: unknown unit '${m[0]}' in [${src}]`);
    return pw({ scale: u[0], dim: u[1] });
  };
  const pw = (v: { scale: number; dim: Dim }) => {
    if (s[i] === "^") { i++; const m = /^-?\d+(\.\d+)?/.exec(s.slice(i))!; i += m[0].length; const e = parseFloat(m[0]); return { scale: Math.pow(v.scale, e), dim: dpow(v.dim, e) }; }
    return v;
  };
  const expr = (): { scale: number; dim: Dim } => {
    let v = factor();
    while (s[i] === "*" || s[i] === "/") {
      const op = s[i++], w = factor();
      v = op === "*" ? { scale: v.scale * w.scale, dim: dmul(v.dim, w.dim) } : { scale: v.scale / w.scale, dim: ddiv(v.dim, w.dim) };
    }
    return v;
  };
  const v = expr();
  if (i < s.length) throw new Error(`unit: trailing '${s.slice(i)}' in [${src}]`);
  return v;
}

Created src/calc/expr.ts
Created src/calc/expr.ts

// SI-checked algebraic & vector field expression language (mirror of rust `calc::{expr, check, eval}`).
// Every AST node is annotated with {kind: scalar|vector|tensor, dim: [kg,m,s,K,mol,A,cd]} BEFORE execution.
import { D, ddiv, deq, describeDim, Dim, dmul, dpow, fmtDim, isOne, ONE, parseUnit, qname } from "./units";
export type Kind = "s" | "v" | "t";
export interface Ty { kind: Kind; dim: Dim }
export type Node = (
  | { t: "num"; v: number; unit?: string; recognized?: string }
  | { t: "var"; name: string }
  | { t: "bin"; op: string; a: Node; b: Node }
  | { t: "neg"; a: Node }
  | { t: "call"; name: string; args: Node[] }
  | { t: "comp"; a: Node; c: number }
) & { s: number; e: number; ty?: Ty };
export interface Diag { level: "error" | "info" | "warn"; msg: string; s?: number; e?: number }
export class DimError extends Error { constructor(msg: string, public s: number, public e: number) { super(msg); } }
export interface VarDef { kind: Kind; dim: Dim; desc: string; value?: number }
export const VARS: Record<string, VarDef> = {
  p: { kind: "s", dim: D(1, -1, -2), desc: "static pressure" },
  rho: { kind: "s", dim: D(1, -3), desc: "density" },
  U: { kind: "v", dim: D(0, 1, -1), desc: "velocity vector" },
  T: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 0, 0, 1), desc: "static temperature" },
  mu: { kind: "s", dim: D(1, -1, -1), desc: "dynamic viscosity (Sutherland)" },
  kappa: { kind: "s", dim: D(1, 1, -3, -1), desc: "thermal conductivity" },
  k: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 2, -2), desc: "turbulent kinetic energy" },
  omega: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 0, -1), desc: "specific dissipation rate" },
  nut: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 2, -1), desc: "turbulent kinematic viscosity" },
  Cp: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "pressure coefficient" },
  Mach: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "Mach number" },
  p0loss: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "total-pressure loss coefficient" },
  gamma: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "ratio of specific heats", value: 1.4 },
  R_air: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 2, -2, -1), desc: "specific gas constant of air", value: 287.05 },
  c_p: { kind: "s", dim: D(0, 2, -2, -1), desc: "specific heat at const. pressure", value: 1250 },
  Pr: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "Prandtl number", value: 0.71 },
  pi: { kind: "s", dim: ONE, desc: "π", value: Math.PI },
};
const CONSTS = [
  { v: 287.05, name: "R_air", dim: D(0, 2, -2, -1), what: "specific gas constant of air" },
  { v: 8.314462, name: "R_u", dim: D(1, 2, -2, -1, -1), what: "universal gas constant" },
  { v: 9.80665, name: "g", dim: D(0, 1, -2), what: "standard gravity" },
  { v: 5.670374e-8, name: "sigma_SB", dim: D(1, 0, -3, -4), what: "Stefan–Boltzmann constant" },
  { v: 101325, name: "p_atm", dim: D(1, -1, -2), what: "standard atmosphere" },
];
/** Registered target quantities: assigning to these names enforces the result dimension. */
export const TARGETS: Record<string, Dim> = {
  mach: ONE, p_total: D(1, -1, -2), p0: D(1, -1, -2), q_dyn: D(1, -1, -2), cp_total: ONE, entropy_gen: D(1, -1, -3, -1),
  q_crit: D(0, 0, -2), vorticity: D(0, 0, -1), T_total: D(0, 0, 0, 1), nu_eff: D(0, 2, -1), re_cell: ONE,
};
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- parser (Pratt)
interface Tok { t: "num" | "id" | "op"; v: string; s: number; e: number; unit?: string }
function lex(src: string): Tok[] {
  const out: Tok[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < src.length) {
    const c = src[i];
    if (/\s/.test(c)) { i++; continue; }
    if (/[0-9]/.test(c) || (c === "." && /[0-9]/.test(src[i + 1] ?? ""))) {
      const m = /^(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)([eE][-+]?\d+)?/.exec(src.slice(i))!;
      const tok: Tok = { t: "num", v: m[0], s: i, e: i + m[0].length };
      i += m[0].length;
      let j = i;
      while (src[j] === " ") j++;
      if (src[j] === "[") {
        const k = src.indexOf("]", j);
        if (k < 0) throw new DimError("unterminated unit annotation '['", j, j + 1);
        tok.unit = src.slice(j + 1, k);
        tok.e = k + 1;
        i = k + 1;
      }
      out.push(tok);
      continue;
    }
    if (/[A-Za-z_]/.test(c)) { const m = /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*/.exec(src.slice(i))!; out.push({ t: "id", v: m[0], s: i, e: i + m[0].length }); i += m[0].length; continue; }
    if ("+-*/^(),.".includes(c)) { out.push({ t: "op", v: c, s: i, e: i + 1 }); i++; continue; }
    if (c === "·") { out.push({ t: "op", v: "*", s: i, e: i + 1 }); i++; continue; }
    throw new DimError(`unexpected character '${c}'`, i, i + 1);
  }
  return out;
}
const BP: Record<string, number> = { "+": 10, "-": 10, "*": 20, "/": 20, "^": 30 };
export function parseExpr(src: string): Node {
  const toks = lex(src);
  let i = 0;
  const peek = () => toks[i];
  const expect = (v: string) => { const t = toks[i]; if (!t || t.v !== v) throw new DimError(`expected '${v}'`, t?.s ?? src.length, t?.e ?? src.length); i++; return t; };
  const nud = (): Node => {
    const t = toks[i++];
    if (!t) throw new DimError("unexpected end of expression", src.length, src.length);
    if (t.t === "num") return { t: "num", v: parseFloat(t.v), unit: t.unit, s: t.s, e: t.e };
    if (t.v === "-") { const a = parse(25); return { t: "neg", a, s: t.s, e: a.e }; }
    if (t.v === "(") { const a = parse(0); const r = expect(")"); return { ...a, s: t.s, e: r.e }; }
    if (t.t === "id") {
      if (peek()?.v === "(") {
        i++;
        const args: Node[] = [];
        if (peek()?.v !== ")") { args.push(parse(0)); while (peek()?.v === ",") { i++; args.push(parse(0)); } }
        const r = expect(")");
        return { t: "call", name: t.v, args, s: t.s, e: r.e };
      }
      return { t: "var", name: t.v, s: t.s, e: t.e };
    }
    throw new DimError(`unexpected '${t.v}'`, t.s, t.e);
  };
  const parse = (rbp: number): Node => {
    let left = nud();
    for (;;) {
      const t = peek();
      if (!t) break;
      if (t.v === ".") {
        i++;
        const f = toks[i++];
        const c = f ? "xyz".indexOf(f.v) : -1;
        if (c < 0) throw new DimError("component access must be .x, .y or .z", f?.s ?? src.length, f?.e ?? src.length);
        left = { t: "comp", a: left, c, s: left.s, e: f.e };
        continue;
      }
      const bp = BP[t.v];
      if (!bp || bp <= rbp) break;
      i++;
      const right = parse(t.v === "^" ? bp - 1 : bp);
      left = { t: "bin", op: t.v, a: left, b: right, s: left.s, e: right.e };
    }
    return left;
  };
  const n = parse(0);
  if (i < toks.length) throw new DimError(`unexpected '${toks[i].v}'`, toks[i].s, toks[i].e);
  return n;
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- dimension / type checker
const KN: Record<Kind, string> = { s: "scalar", v: "vector", t: "tensor" };
const label = (n: Node, src: string) => src.slice(n.s, n.e).trim();
function suggestion(dl: Dim, dr: Dim): string {
  const ratio = ddiv(dl, dr);
  const v = Object.entries(VARS).find(([, d]) => d.kind === "s" && deq(d.dim, ratio) && !isOne(ratio));
  if (v) return ` — multiply by ${qname(v[1].dim) ?? v[1].desc} ${v[0]} [${fmtDim(v[1].dim)}]`;
  const inv = Object.entries(VARS).find(([, d]) => d.kind === "s" && deq(d.dim, dpow(ratio, -1)) && !isOne(ratio));
  if (inv) return ` — multiply the left operand by ${qname(inv[1].dim) ?? inv[1].desc} ${inv[0]} [${fmtDim(inv[1].dim)}]`;
  return ` — the operands differ by a factor of ${describeDim(ratio)}`;
}
export function check(root: Node, src: string, diags: Diag[]): Ty {
  const visit = (n: Node): Ty => {
    const ty = inner(n);
    n.ty = ty;
    return ty;
  };
  const err = (n: Node, msg: string): never => { throw new DimError(msg, n.s, n.e); };
  const inner = (n: Node): Ty => {
    switch (n.t) {
      case "num": {
        if (n.unit) {
          try { const u = parseUnit(n.unit); n.v *= u.scale; return { kind: "s", dim: u.dim }; } catch (e) { return err(n, (e as Error).message); }
        }
        const c = CONSTS.find((k) => Math.abs(n.v - k.v) / k.v < 1e-3);
        if (c) {
          n.recognized = c.name;
          diags.push({ level: "info", msg: `Literal ${n.v} recognised as ${c.name} = ${c.v} [${fmtDim(c.dim)}] (${c.what}). Write ${n.v}[…] to make the unit explicit.`, s: n.s, e: n.e });
          return { kind: "s", dim: c.dim };
        }
        return { kind: "s", dim: ONE };
      }
      case "var": {
        const v = VARS[n.name];
        if (!v) return err(n, `Unknown field or constant '${n.name}'. Available: ${Object.keys(VARS).join(", ")}`);
        return { kind: v.kind, dim: v.dim };
      }
      case "neg": return visit(n.a);
      case "comp": {
        const a = visit(n.a);
        if (a.kind !== "v") return err(n, `Component access .${"xyz"[n.c]} requires a vector, got ${KN[a.kind]} '${label(n.a, src)}'`);
        return { kind: "s", dim: a.dim };
      }
      case "bin": {
        const a = visit(n.a), b = visit(n.b);
        if (n.op === "+" || n.op === "-") {
          if (a.kind !== b.kind) return err(n, `Cannot ${n.op === "+" ? "add" : "subtract"} ${KN[a.kind]} '${label(n.a, src)}' and ${KN[b.kind]} '${label(n.b, src)}' — operand ranks differ`);
          if (!deq(a.dim, b.dim)) return err(n, `Cannot ${n.op === "+" ? "add" : "subtract"} ${describeDim(a.dim)} and ${describeDim(b.dim)}${suggestion(a.dim, b.dim)}`);
          return a;
        }
        if (n.op === "*") {
          if (a.kind === "v" && b.kind === "v") return err(n, `Ambiguous vector product '${label(n, src)}' — use dot(a, b) or cross(a, b)`);
          if (a.kind === "v" && b.kind === "t") return err(n, "vector * tensor is undefined here — use tensor * vector (contraction on the 2nd index)");
          if (a.kind === "t" && b.kind === "t") return err(n, "tensor * tensor: use dot-product contraction functions (not supported in Stage 2)");
          const kind: Kind = a.kind === "t" && b.kind === "v" ? "v" : a.kind === "s" ? b.kind : a.kind;
          return { kind, dim: dmul(a.dim, b.dim) };
        }
        if (n.op === "/") {
          if (b.kind !== "s") return err(n, `Division by a ${KN[b.kind]} '${label(n.b, src)}' is undefined`);
          return { kind: a.kind, dim: ddiv(a.dim, b.dim) };
        }
        if (n.op === "^") {
          const ex = constValue(n.b);
          if (ex === null) return err(n.b, "Exponent must be a constant number so the dimension is known at parse time");
          if (!isOne(b.dim)) return err(n.b, `Exponent must be dimensionless, got ${describeDim(b.dim)}`);
          if (a.kind !== "s") return err(n, `Power of a ${KN[a.kind]} — use mag_sq() / dot()`);
          return { kind: "s", dim: dpow(a.dim, ex) };
        }
        return err(n, `unknown operator ${n.op}`);
      }
      case "call": {
        const F = FUNCS[n.name];
        if (!F) return err(n, `Unknown function '${n.name}()'. Available: ${Object.keys(FUNCS).join(", ")}`);
        if (F.n !== n.args.length) return err(n, `${n.name}() expects ${F.n} argument(s), got ${n.args.length}`);
        const at = n.args.map(visit);
        const r = F.check(at, n, src);
        if (typeof r === "string") return err(n, r);
        return r;
      }
    }
  };
  return visit(root);
}
function constValue(n: Node): number | null {
  if (n.t === "num") return n.v;
  if (n.t === "neg") { const v = constValue(n.a); return v === null ? null : -v; }
  if (n.t === "bin" && n.op === "/") { const a = constValue(n.a), b = constValue(n.b); return a === null || b === null ? null : a / b; }
  return null;
}
const L1 = D(0, 1);
const needDimless = (name: string) => (a: Ty[]): Ty | string =>
  a[0].kind !== "s" ? `${name}() requires a scalar` : !isOne(a[0].dim) ? `${name}() requires a dimensionless argument, got ${describeDim(a[0].dim)} — normalise it first (e.g. by a reference value)` : { kind: "s", dim: ONE };
const FUNCS: Record<string, { n: number; check: (a: Ty[], n: Node, src: string) => Ty | string; doc: string }> = {
  dot: { n: 2, doc: "dot(a,b): v·v → s", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "v" && a[1].kind === "v" ? { kind: "s", dim: dmul(a[0].dim, a[1].dim) } : `dot() needs two vectors, got ${KN[a[0].kind]}, ${KN[a[1].kind]}`) },
  cross: { n: 2, doc: "cross(a,b): v×v → v", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "v" && a[1].kind === "v" ? { kind: "v", dim: dmul(a[0].dim, a[1].dim) } : `cross() needs two vectors, got ${KN[a[0].kind]}, ${KN[a[1].kind]}`) },
  mag: { n: 1, doc: "mag(x): |x|", check: (a) => ({ kind: "s", dim: a[0].dim }) },
  mag_sq: { n: 1, doc: "mag_sq(x): |x|²", check: (a) => ({ kind: "s", dim: dpow(a[0].dim, 2) }) },
  sqrt: { n: 1, doc: "sqrt(s): dim^½", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "s" ? { kind: "s", dim: dpow(a[0].dim, 0.5) } : "sqrt() of a non-scalar — use mag()") },
  abs: { n: 1, doc: "abs(s)", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "s" ? a[0] : "abs() expects a scalar") },
  min: { n: 2, doc: "min(a,b)", check: (a) => (deq(a[0].dim, a[1].dim) ? a[0] : `min() operands differ: ${describeDim(a[0].dim)} vs ${describeDim(a[1].dim)}`) },
  max: { n: 2, doc: "max(a,b)", check: (a) => (deq(a[0].dim, a[1].dim) ? a[0] : `max() operands differ: ${describeDim(a[0].dim)} vs ${describeDim(a[1].dim)}`) },
  exp: { n: 1, doc: "exp(s) dimensionless", check: needDimless("exp") },
  log: { n: 1, doc: "log(s) dimensionless", check: needDimless("log") },
  tanh: { n: 1, doc: "tanh(s)", check: needDimless("tanh") },
  grad: { n: 1, doc: "grad(s)→v, grad(v)→t  [dim·m⁻¹]", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "t" ? "grad() of a tensor is not supported" : { kind: a[0].kind === "s" ? "v" : "t", dim: ddiv(a[0].dim, L1) }) },
  div: { n: 1, doc: "div(v)→s [dim·m⁻¹]", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "v" ? { kind: "s", dim: ddiv(a[0].dim, L1) } : `div() needs a vector, got ${KN[a[0].kind]}`) },
  curl: { n: 1, doc: "curl(v)→v [dim·m⁻¹]", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "v" ? { kind: "v", dim: ddiv(a[0].dim, L1) } : `curl() needs a vector, got ${KN[a[0].kind]}`) },
  trace: { n: 1, doc: "trace(t)→s", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "t" ? { kind: "s", dim: a[0].dim } : "trace() needs a tensor") },
  sym: { n: 1, doc: "sym(t)", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "t" ? a[0] : "sym() needs a tensor") },
  skew: { n: 1, doc: "skew(t)", check: (a) => (a[0].kind === "t" ? a[0] : "skew() needs a tensor") },
};
export const FUNC_DOCS = Object.values(FUNCS).map((f) => f.doc);
export interface Stmt { target: string; src: string; ast: Node; ty: Ty; diags: Diag[]; offset: number }
/** Parse + check "name = expr". Throws DimError with absolute positions. */
export function compileStmt(line: string): Stmt {
  const m = /^\s*([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*=(?!=)/.exec(line);
  const target = m ? m[1] : "result";
  const offset = m ? m[0].length : 0;
  const src = line.slice(offset);
  const diags: Diag[] = [];
  try {
    const ast = parseExpr(src);
    const ty = check(ast, src, diags);
    const want = TARGETS[target];
    if (want && !deq(want, ty.dim)) throw new DimError(`'${target}' is registered as ${describeDim(want)} but the expression yields ${describeDim(ty.dim)}`, 0, src.length);
    return { target, src, ast, ty, diags, offset };
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof DimError) throw new DimError(e.message, e.s + offset, e.e + offset);
    throw e;
  }
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- vectorised evaluator on the structured grid
export type FV = { k: "c"; v: number } | { k: Kind; d: Float32Array };
export interface Grid { nx: number; ny: number; nz: number; h: number[]; fields: Record<string, Float32Array>; mask: Uint8Array }
export function evaluate(n: Node, G: Grid): FV {
  const N = G.nx * G.ny * G.nz;
  const ev = (n: Node): FV => {
    switch (n.t) {
      case "num": return { k: "c", v: n.recognized ? CONSTS.find((c) => c.name === n.recognized)!.v : n.v };
      case "var": {
        const v = VARS[n.name];
        if (v.value !== undefined) return { k: "c", v: v.value };
        if (n.name === "U") {
          const d = new Float32Array(3 * N), ux = G.fields.Ux, uy = G.fields.Uy, uz = G.fields.Uz;
          for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { d[3 * i] = ux[i]; d[3 * i + 1] = uy[i]; d[3 * i + 2] = uz[i]; }
          return { k: "v", d };
        }
        return { k: "s", d: G.fields[n.name] };
      }
      case "neg": return map1(ev(n.a), (x) => -x);
      case "comp": { const a = ev(n.a) as { k: Kind; d: Float32Array }; const d = new Float32Array(N); for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) d[i] = a.d[3 * i + n.c]; return { k: "s", d }; }
      case "bin": return bin(n.op, ev(n.a), ev(n.b), N);
      case "call": return call(n.name, n.args.map(ev), G);
    }
  };
  return ev(n);
}
const width = (k: Kind) => (k === "s" ? 1 : k === "v" ? 3 : 9);
function map1(a: FV, f: (x: number) => number): FV {
  if (a.k === "c") return { k: "c", v: f(a.v) };
  const d = new Float32Array(a.d.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i++) d[i] = f(a.d[i]);
  return { k: a.k, d };
}
function bin(op: string, a: FV, b: FV, N: number): FV {
  const f = op === "+" ? (x: number, y: number) => x + y : op === "-" ? (x: number, y: number) => x - y : op === "*" ? (x: number, y: number) => x * y : op === "/" ? (x: number, y: number) => x / y : (x: number, y: number) => Math.pow(x, y);
  if (a.k === "c" && b.k === "c") return { k: "c", v: f(a.v, b.v) };
  if (a.k === "c") return map1(b, (y) => f(a.v, y));
  if (b.k === "c") return map1(a, (x) => f(x, b.v));
  if (op === "*" && a.k === "t" && b.k === "v") {
    const d = new Float32Array(3 * N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) d[3 * i + r] = a.d[9 * i + 3 * r] * b.d[3 * i] + a.d[9 * i + 3 * r + 1] * b.d[3 * i + 1] + a.d[9 * i + 3 * r + 2] * b.d[3 * i + 2];
    return { k: "v", d };
  }
  const wa = width(a.k), wb = width(b.k), w = Math.max(wa, wb), k = wa >= wb ? a.k : b.k;
  const d = new Float32Array(w * N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) for (let c = 0; c < w; c++) d[w * i + c] = f(a.d[wa * i + (wa === 1 ? 0 : c)], b.d[wb * i + (wb === 1 ? 0 : c)]);
  return { k, d };
}
function toArr(a: FV, N: number): { k: Kind; d: Float32Array } {
  if (a.k !== "c") return a as { k: Kind; d: Float32Array };
  return { k: "s", d: new Float32Array(N).fill(a.v) };
}
function gradScalar(f: Float32Array, G: Grid, out: Float32Array, stride: number, off: number) {
  const { nx, ny, nz, h, mask } = G;
  const st = [1, nx, nx * ny], n = [nx, ny, nz];
  for (let k = 0; k < nz; k++)
    for (let j = 0; j < ny; j++)
      for (let i = 0; i < nx; i++) {
        const id = i + nx * (j + ny * k), ijk = [i, j, k];
        for (let a = 0; a < 3; a++) {
          let lo = id, hi = id, dl = 0, dh = 0;
          if (ijk[a] > 0 && !mask[id - st[a]]) { lo = id - st[a]; dl = 1; }
          if (ijk[a] < n[a] - 1 && !mask[id + st[a]]) { hi = id + st[a]; dh = 1; }
          out[stride * id + off + a] = dl + dh ? (f[hi] - f[lo]) / ((dl + dh) * h[a]) : 0;
        }
      }
}
function call(name: string, A: FV[], G: Grid): FV {
  const N = G.nx * G.ny * G.nz;
  const s1 = (fn: (x: number) => number) => map1(A[0], fn);
  switch (name) {
    case "sqrt": return s1(Math.sqrt);
    case "abs": return s1(Math.abs);
    case "exp": return s1(Math.exp);
    case "log": return s1(Math.log);
    case "tanh": return s1(Math.tanh);
    case "min": return bin2(A[0], A[1], N, Math.min);
    case "max": return bin2(A[0], A[1], N, Math.max);
  }
  const a = toArr(A[0], N), w = width(a.k);
  if (name === "mag" || name === "mag_sq") {
    const d = new Float32Array(N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { let s = 0; for (let c = 0; c < w; c++) s += a.d[w * i + c] ** 2; d[i] = name === "mag" ? Math.sqrt(s) : s; }
    return { k: "s", d };
  }
  if (name === "dot" || name === "cross") {
    const b = toArr(A[1], N);
    if (name === "dot") { const d = new Float32Array(N); for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) d[i] = a.d[3 * i] * b.d[3 * i] + a.d[3 * i + 1] * b.d[3 * i + 1] + a.d[3 * i + 2] * b.d[3 * i + 2]; return { k: "s", d }; }
    const d = new Float32Array(3 * N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
      const [x1, y1, z1, x2, y2, z2] = [a.d[3 * i], a.d[3 * i + 1], a.d[3 * i + 2], b.d[3 * i], b.d[3 * i + 1], b.d[3 * i + 2]];
      d[3 * i] = y1 * z2 - z1 * y2; d[3 * i + 1] = z1 * x2 - x1 * z2; d[3 * i + 2] = x1 * y2 - y1 * x2;
    }
    return { k: "v", d };
  }
  if (name === "grad") {
    if (a.k === "s") { const d = new Float32Array(3 * N); gradScalar(a.d, G, d, 3, 0); return { k: "v", d }; }
    const d = new Float32Array(9 * N), comp = new Float32Array(N);
    for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) { for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) comp[i] = a.d[3 * i + c]; gradScalar(comp, G, d, 9, 3 * c); }
    return { k: "t", d };
  }
  const gradT = (): Float32Array => (call("grad", [a], G) as { d: Float32Array }).d;
  if (name === "div") { const t = gradT(); const d = new Float32Array(N); for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) d[i] = t[9 * i] + t[9 * i + 4] + t[9 * i + 8]; return { k: "s", d }; }
  if (name === "curl") {
    const t = gradT(), d = new Float32Array(3 * N);
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { const g = (r: number, c: number) => t[9 * i + 3 * r + c]; d[3 * i] = g(2, 1) - g(1, 2); d[3 * i + 1] = g(0, 2) - g(2, 0); d[3 * i + 2] = g(1, 0) - g(0, 1); }
    return { k: "v", d };
  }
  if (name === "trace") { const d = new Float32Array(N); for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) d[i] = a.d[9 * i] + a.d[9 * i + 4] + a.d[9 * i + 8]; return { k: "s", d }; }
  if (name === "sym" || name === "skew") {
    const d = new Float32Array(9 * N), sg = name === "sym" ? 1 : -1;
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) for (let r = 0; r < 3; r++) for (let c = 0; c < 3; c++) d[9 * i + 3 * r + c] = 0.5 * (a.d[9 * i + 3 * r + c] + sg * a.d[9 * i + 3 * c + r]);
    return { k: "t", d };
  }
  throw new Error("eval: " + name);
}
function bin2(a: FV, b: FV, N: number, f: (x: number, y: number) => number): FV {
  const A = toArr(a, N), B = toArr(b, N);
  const d = new Float32Array(N);
  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) d[i] = f(A.d[i], B.d[i]);
  return { k: "s", d };
}
// ---------------------------------------------------------------- CFDFLD03 encoder (64-byte header + f32 payload)
export function encodeCfdfld03(name: string, dim: Dim, data: Float32Array, nComp: number, dims: number[], mn: number, mx: number): ArrayBuffer {
  const buf = new ArrayBuffer(64 + data.byteLength);
  const dv = new DataView(buf);
  "CFDFLD03".split("").forEach((c, i) => dv.setUint8(i, c.charCodeAt(0)));
  dv.setUint32(8, 3, true);
  dv.setUint32(12, data.length / nComp, true);
  dv.setUint32(16, nComp, true);
  dv.setUint32(20, dims[0], true); dv.setUint32(24, dims[1], true); dv.setUint32(28, dims[2], true);
  dim.forEach((e, i) => dv.setInt8(32 + i, Math.round(e * 2)));
  dv.setFloat32(40, mn, true); dv.setFloat32(44, mx, true);
  name.slice(0, 16).split("").forEach((c, i) => dv.setUint8(48 + i, c.charCodeAt(0) & 0x7f));
  new Float32Array(buf, 64).set(data);
  return buf;
}
export { fmtDim };

Created src/calc/rhai.ts

// Tiny Rhai-subset interpreter for the post-processing console (the native app embeds the real `rhai` crate).
// Supports: let, assignments, numbers/strings/bools/arrays, + - * / % comparisons && ||, ranges a..b,
// for x in <array|range> { }, if/else, function calls into the host API.
export type RVal = number | string | boolean | RVal[] | null;
export interface RhaiHost { [fn: string]: (...args: RVal[]) => RVal }
interface Tok { t: "num" | "str" | "id" | "op"; v: string }
function lex(src: string): Tok[] {
  const out: Tok[] = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < src.length) {
    const c = src[i];
    if (/\s/.test(c)) { i++; continue; }
    if (c === "/" && src[i + 1] === "/") { while (i < src.length && src[i] !== "\n") i++; continue; }
    if (/[0-9]/.test(c)) { const m = /^\d+(\.\d+)?([eE][-+]?\d+)?/.exec(src.slice(i))!; out.push({ t: "num", v: m[0] }); i += m[0].length; continue; }
    if (c === '"' || c === "`") { const j = src.indexOf(c, i + 1); if (j < 0) throw new Error("unterminated string"); out.push({ t: "str", v: src.slice(i + 1, j) }); i = j + 1; continue; }
    if (/[A-Za-z_]/.test(c)) { const m = /^[A-Za-z_]\w*/.exec(src.slice(i))!; out.push({ t: "id", v: m[0] }); i += m[0].length; continue; }
    const two = src.slice(i, i + 2);
    if (["==", "!=", "<=", ">=", "&&", "||", "..", "+="].includes(two)) { out.push({ t: "op", v: two }); i += 2; continue; }
    if ("+-*/%<>=(){}[],;!".includes(c)) { out.push({ t: "op", v: c }); i++; continue; }
    throw new Error(`unexpected '${c}'`);
  }
  return out;
}
export function runRhai(src: string, host: RhaiHost, scope: Map<string, RVal>): RVal {
  const T = lex(src);
  let i = 0;
  const peek = (v?: string) => (T[i] && (v === undefined || T[i].v === v) ? T[i] : undefined);
  const eat = (v: string) => { if (!peek(v)) throw new Error(`expected '${v}'${T[i] ? ` near '${T[i].v}'` : ""}`); i++; };
  type Thunk = () => RVal;
  const block = (): Thunk[] => { eat("{"); const st: Thunk[] = []; while (!peek("}")) st.push(stmt()); eat("}"); return st; };
  const runAll = (st: Thunk[]) => { let r: RVal = null; for (const s of st) r = s(); return r; };
  const stmt = (): Thunk => {
    if (peek("let")) {
      i++;
      const name = T[i++].v;
      eat("=");
      const e = expr();
      if (peek(";")) i++;
      return () => { scope.set(name, e()); return null; };
    }
    if (peek("for")) {
      i++;
      const name = T[i++].v;
      eat("in");
      const it = expr();
      const body = block();
      return () => { const arr = it(); if (!Array.isArray(arr)) throw new Error("for: expected array or range"); for (const v of arr) { scope.set(name, v); runAll(body); } return null; };
    }
    if (peek("if")) {
      i++;
      const c = expr();
      const a = block();
      let b: Thunk[] = [];
      if (peek("else")) { i++; b = peek("if") ? [stmt()] : block(); }
      return () => (c() ? runAll(a) : runAll(b));
    }
    if (T[i]?.t === "id" && (T[i + 1]?.v === "=" || T[i + 1]?.v === "+=")) {
      const name = T[i].v, op = T[i + 1].v;
      i += 2;
      const e = expr();
      if (peek(";")) i++;
      return () => { if (!scope.has(name)) throw new Error(`variable '${name}' not declared (use let)`); const v = e(); scope.set(name, op === "+=" ? (scope.get(name) as any) + (v as any) : v); return null; };
    }
    const e = expr();
    if (peek(";")) { i++; return () => { e(); return null; }; }
    return e;
  };
  const BIN: [string[], (a: any, b: any, op: string) => RVal][] = [
    [["||"], (a, b) => a || b],
    [["&&"], (a, b) => a && b],
    [["==", "!="], (a, b, o) => (o === "==" ? a === b : a !== b)],
    [["<", ">", "<=", ">="], (a, b, o) => (o === "<" ? a < b : o === ">" ? a > b : o === "<=" ? a <= b : a >= b)],
    [[".."], (a, b) => Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, b - a) }, (_, k) => a + k)],
    [["+", "-"], (a, b, o) => (o === "+" ? (Array.isArray(a) ? [...a, ...(Array.isArray(b) ? b : [b])] : a + b) : a - b)],
    [["*", "/", "%"], (a, b, o) => (o === "*" ? a * b : o === "/" ? a / b : a % b)],
  ];
  const level = (lv: number): Thunk => {
    if (lv >= BIN.length) return unary();
    let l = level(lv + 1);
    while (T[i] && T[i].t === "op" && BIN[lv][0].includes(T[i].v)) {
      const op = T[i++].v, a = l, b = level(lv + 1), f = BIN[lv][1];
      l = () => f(a(), b(), op);
    }
    return l;
  };
  const expr = (): Thunk => level(0);
  const unary = (): Thunk => {
    if (peek("-")) { i++; const a = unary(); return () => -(a() as number); }
    if (peek("!")) { i++; const a = unary(); return () => !a(); }
    return primary();
  };
  const primary = (): Thunk => {
    const t = T[i++];
    if (!t) throw new Error("unexpected end of input");
    if (t.t === "num") { const v = parseFloat(t.v); return () => v; }
    if (t.t === "str") return () => t.v;
    if (t.v === "(") { const e = expr(); eat(")"); return e; }
    if (t.v === "[") {
      const items: Thunk[] = [];
      if (!peek("]")) { items.push(expr()); while (peek(",")) { i++; items.push(expr()); } }
      eat("]");
      return () => items.map((f) => f());
    }
    if (t.t === "id") {
      if (t.v === "true") return () => true;
      if (t.v === "false") return () => false;
      if (peek("(")) {
        i++;
        const args: Thunk[] = [];
        if (!peek(")")) { args.push(expr()); while (peek(",")) { i++; args.push(expr()); } }
        eat(")");
        return () => { const f = host[t.v]; if (!f) throw new Error(`function not found: ${t.v}(…) — try help()`); return f(...args.map((a) => a())); };
      }
      return () => { if (!scope.has(t.v)) throw new Error(`variable not found: ${t.v}`); return scope.get(t.v)!; };
    }
    throw new Error(`unexpected '${t.v}'`);
  };
  let last: RVal = null;
  while (i < T.length) last = stmt()();
  return last;
}
export const fmtR = (v: RVal): string => (v === null ? "()" : Array.isArray(v) ? `[${v.map(fmtR).join(", ")}]` : typeof v === "number" ? String(+v.toPrecision(6)) : typeof v === "string" ? v : String(v));

Created src/plots/Plot.tsx

import { ReactNode, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
export interface Axis { label: ReactNode; min: number; max: number; log?: boolean; invert?: boolean; fmt?: (v: number) => string }
export interface Series { name: string; pts: [number, number][]; color: string; dash?: string; width?: number; markers?: boolean; markersOnly?: boolean; r?: number; hollow?: boolean; opacity?: number; step?: boolean }
export interface Band { y0: number; y1: number; color: string; label?: string; hatch?: boolean }
export interface Ref { v: number; color: string; label?: string; dash?: string }
export interface PlotProps {
  x: Axis; y: Axis; series: Series[]; bands?: Band[]; hlines?: Ref[]; vlines?: Ref[]; height?: number; title?: ReactNode; right?: ReactNode;
  cursorX?: number | null; onHoverX?: (x: number | null) => void; onPointClick?: (si: number, pi: number) => void; legend?: boolean; marks?: { x: number; y: number; color: string; label: string }[];
}
export function niceTicks(a: number, b: number, n = 6): number[] {
  const span = Math.abs(b - a) || 1;
  const raw = span / n, p = Math.pow(10, Math.floor(Math.log10(raw)));
  const step = [1, 2, 2.5, 5, 10].map((m) => m * p).find((s) => span / s <= n) ?? 10 * p;
  const lo = Math.ceil(Math.min(a, b) / step - 1e-9) * step;
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let v = lo; v <= Math.max(a, b) + step * 1e-9; v += step) out.push(Math.abs(v) < step * 1e-9 ? 0 : v);
  return out;
}
export const tickFmt = (v: number) => {
  const a = Math.abs(v);
  if (a === 0) return "0";
  if (a >= 1e4 || a < 1e-3) return v.toExponential(0).replace("e+", "e");
  return String(+v.toPrecision(4));
};
const SUPS = "⁰¹²³⁴⁵⁶⁷⁸⁹";
const pow10 = (k: number) => "10" + (k < 0 ? "⁻" : "") + String(Math.abs(k)).split("").map((d) => SUPS[+d]).join("");
function useWidth(): [React.RefObject<HTMLDivElement | null>, number] {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [w, setW] = useState(400);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ref.current) return;
    const ro = new ResizeObserver((e) => setW(Math.max(160, e[0].contentRect.width)));
    ro.observe(ref.current);
    return () => ro.disconnect();
  }, []);
  return [ref, w];
}
export function Plot(p: PlotProps) {
  const [ref, W] = useWidth();
  const H = p.height ?? 220;
  const m = { l: 50, r: 14, t: 10, b: 34 };
  const iw = W - m.l - m.r, ih = H - m.t - m.b;
  const tx = (v: number) => {
    const t = p.x.log ? (Math.log10(Math.max(v, 1e-30)) - Math.log10(p.x.min)) / (Math.log10(p.x.max) - Math.log10(p.x.min)) : (v - p.x.min) / (p.x.max - p.x.min);
    return m.l + (p.x.invert ? 1 - t : t) * iw;
  };
  const ty = (v: number) => {
    const t = p.y.log ? (Math.log10(Math.max(v, 1e-30)) - Math.log10(p.y.min)) / (Math.log10(p.y.max) - Math.log10(p.y.min)) : (v - p.y.min) / (p.y.max - p.y.min);
    return m.t + (p.y.invert ? t : 1 - t) * ih;
  };
  const inv = (px: number) => {
    let t = (px - m.l) / iw;
    if (p.x.invert) t = 1 - t;
    return p.x.log ? Math.pow(10, Math.log10(p.x.min) + t * (Math.log10(p.x.max) - Math.log10(p.x.min))) : p.x.min + t * (p.x.max - p.x.min);
  };
  const logTicks = (a: number, b: number) => {
    const maj: number[] = [], min: number[] = [];
    for (let k = Math.floor(Math.log10(a)); k <= Math.ceil(Math.log10(b)); k++) {
      const v = Math.pow(10, k);
      if (v >= a * 0.999 && v <= b * 1.001) maj.push(v);
      for (let j = 2; j < 10; j++) if (v * j > a && v * j < b) min.push(v * j);
    }
    return { maj, min };
  };
  const xt = p.x.log ? logTicks(p.x.min, p.x.max) : { maj: niceTicks(p.x.min, p.x.max, Math.max(3, Math.floor(iw / 70))), min: [] };
  const yt = p.y.log ? logTicks(p.y.min, p.y.max) : { maj: niceTicks(p.y.min, p.y.max, Math.max(3, Math.floor(ih / 34))), min: [] };
  const clipId = useRef("c" + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)).current;
  const path = (s: Series) => {
    let d = "";
    let pen = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < s.pts.length; i++) {
      const [x, y] = s.pts[i];
      if (!isFinite(x) || !isFinite(y)) { pen = false; continue; }
      if (s.step && pen && i > 0) d += `H${tx(x).toFixed(1)}`;
      d += `${pen ? "L" : "M"}${tx(x).toFixed(1)},${ty(y).toFixed(1)}`;
      pen = true;
    }
    return d;
  };
  const interp = (s: Series, x: number) => {
    let best: [number, number] | null = null, bd = Infinity;
    for (const pt of s.pts) { const d = Math.abs(pt[0] - x); if (d < bd && isFinite(pt[1])) { bd = d; best = pt; } }
    return best;
  };
  const cx = p.cursorX;
  return (
    <div ref={ref} className="relative w-full select-none">
      {(p.title || p.right) && (
        <div className="mb-1 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-1">
          <div className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-300">{p.title}</div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1">{p.right}</div>
        </div>
      )}
      <svg width={W} height={H} className="block rounded-md bg-[#111217]"
        onMouseMove={(e) => { const r = (e.currentTarget as SVGSVGElement).getBoundingClientRect(); const px = e.clientX - r.left; p.onHoverX?.(px >= m.l && px <= m.l + iw ? inv(px) : null); }}
        onMouseLeave={() => p.onHoverX?.(null)}>
        <defs>
          <clipPath id={clipId}><rect x={m.l} y={m.t} width={iw} height={ih} /></clipPath>
          <pattern id={clipId + "h"} width="6" height="6" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" patternTransform="rotate(45)"><line x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="6" stroke="#EF233C" strokeOpacity="0.35" strokeWidth="2" /></pattern>
        </defs>
        {(p.bands ?? []).map((b, i) => {
          const y0 = ty(b.y0), y1 = ty(b.y1);
          return (
            <g key={i}>
              <rect x={m.l} y={Math.min(y0, y1)} width={iw} height={Math.abs(y1 - y0)} fill={b.hatch ? `url(#${clipId}h)` : b.color} clipPath={`url(#${clipId})`} />
              {b.label && <text x={m.l + iw - 4} y={Math.min(y0, y1) + 10} textAnchor="end" fontSize="9" fill={b.hatch ? "#ff8a96" : "#cbd5e1"} opacity="0.85">{b.label}</text>}
            </g>
          );
        })}
        {yt.min.map((v) => <line key={"ym" + v} x1={m.l} x2={m.l + iw} y1={ty(v)} y2={ty(v)} stroke="#1d1e25" />)}
        {xt.min.map((v) => <line key={"xm" + v} y1={m.t} y2={m.t + ih} x1={tx(v)} x2={tx(v)} stroke="#1d1e25" />)}
        {yt.maj.map((v) => (
          <g key={"y" + v}>
            <line x1={m.l} x2={m.l + iw} y1={ty(v)} y2={ty(v)} stroke="#262832" />
            <text x={m.l - 5} y={ty(v) + 3} textAnchor="end" fontSize="9.5" fill="#8b8b96" fontFamily="ui-monospace,monospace">{p.y.log ? pow10(Math.round(Math.log10(v))) : (p.y.fmt ?? tickFmt)(v)}</text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {xt.maj.map((v) => (
          <g key={"x" + v}>
            <line y1={m.t} y2={m.t + ih} x1={tx(v)} x2={tx(v)} stroke="#262832" />
            <text x={tx(v)} y={m.t + ih + 12} textAnchor="middle" fontSize="9.5" fill="#8b8b96" fontFamily="ui-monospace,monospace">{p.x.log ? pow10(Math.round(Math.log10(v))) : (p.x.fmt ?? tickFmt)(v)}</text>
          </g>
        ))}
        {(p.hlines ?? []).map((h, i) => (
          <g key={"h" + i}>
            <line x1={m.l} x2={m.l + iw} y1={ty(h.v)} y2={ty(h.v)} stroke={h.color} strokeDasharray={h.dash ?? "4 3"} strokeWidth="1" />
            {h.label && <text x={m.l + 4} y={ty(h.v) - 3} fontSize="9" fill={h.color}>{h.label}</text>}
          </g>
        ))}
        {(p.vlines ?? []).map((h, i) => (
          <g key={"v" + i}>
            <line y1={m.t} y2={m.t + ih} x1={tx(h.v)} x2={tx(h.v)} stroke={h.color} strokeDasharray={h.dash ?? "4 3"} />
            {h.label && <text x={tx(h.v) + 3} y={m.t + 10} fontSize="9" fill={h.color}>{h.label}</text>}
          </g>
        ))}
        <g clipPath={`url(#${clipId})`}>
          {p.series.map((s, si) => (
            <g key={si} opacity={s.opacity ?? 1}>
              {!s.markersOnly && <path d={path(s)} fill="none" stroke={s.color} strokeWidth={s.width ?? 1.6} strokeDasharray={s.dash} strokeLinejoin="round" />}
              {(s.markers || s.markersOnly) &&
                s.pts.map((pt, pi) => isFinite(pt[1]) && (
                  <circle key={pi} cx={tx(pt[0])} cy={ty(pt[1])} r={s.r ?? 2.6} fill={s.hollow ? "#111217" : s.color} stroke={s.color} strokeWidth="1.2"
                    className={p.onPointClick ? "cursor-pointer hover:opacity-70" : ""} onClick={p.onPointClick ? () => p.onPointClick!(si, pi) : undefined} />
                ))}
            </g>
          ))}
          {(p.marks ?? []).map((mk, i) => (
            <g key={"mk" + i} transform={`translate(${tx(mk.x)},${ty(mk.y)})`}>
              <path d="M0,-7 L6,4 L-6,4 Z" fill={mk.color} stroke="#111217" strokeWidth="1" />
              <text x="8" y="-4" fontSize="10" fill={mk.color} fontWeight="600">{mk.label}</text>
            </g>
          ))}
        </g>
        {cx !== null && cx !== undefined && cx >= Math.min(p.x.min, p.x.max) && cx <= Math.max(p.x.min, p.x.max) && (
          <g>
            <line y1={m.t} y2={m.t + ih} x1={tx(cx)} x2={tx(cx)} stroke="#e4e4e7" strokeOpacity="0.55" />
            {p.series.filter((s) => !s.markersOnly).slice(0, 4).map((s, i) => {
              const pt = interp(s, cx);
              if (!pt) return null;
              return (
                <g key={i}>
                  <circle cx={tx(pt[0])} cy={ty(pt[1])} r="3.2" fill={s.color} stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="1" />
                  <text x={tx(pt[0]) + 6} y={ty(pt[1]) - 5 - i * 0} fontSize="9.5" fill={s.color} fontFamily="ui-monospace,monospace" stroke="#111217" strokeWidth="3" paintOrder="stroke">{tickFmt(pt[1])}</text>
                </g>
              );
            })}
          </g>
        )}
        <rect x={m.l} y={m.t} width={iw} height={ih} fill="none" stroke="#3a3b46" />
        <text x={m.l + iw / 2} y={H - 4} textAnchor="middle" fontSize="10.5" fill="#b4b4be">{p.x.label}</text>
        <text transform={`translate(12,${m.t + ih / 2}) rotate(-90)`} textAnchor="middle" fontSize="10.5" fill="#b4b4be">{p.y.label}</text>
      </svg>
      {p.legend !== false && (
        <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 px-1">
          {p.series.filter((s) => s.name).map((s, i) => (
            <span key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
              <svg width="16" height="6"><line x1="0" y1="3" x2="16" y2="3" stroke={s.color} strokeWidth="2" strokeDasharray={s.markersOnly ? "1 3" : s.dash} /></svg>
              {s.name}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
/** Polar directivity chart (0° = upstream on the rotor axis), radial axis in dB. */
export function PolarPlot({ curve, points, rMin, rMax, size = 220, title }: { curve: [number, number][]; points: { th: number; v: number; color: string; label: string }[]; rMin: number; rMax: number; size?: number; title?: string }) {
  const c = size / 2, R = size / 2 - 22;
  const rad = (v: number) => (Math.max(rMin, Math.min(rMax, v)) - rMin) / (rMax - rMin) * R;
  const pt = (thDeg: number, v: number) => { const a = (thDeg * Math.PI) / 180; return [c - Math.cos(a) * rad(v), c - Math.sin(a) * rad(v)]; };
  const ring = niceTicks(rMin, rMax, 4).filter((v) => v > rMin);
  const d = curve.map(([th, v], i) => { const [x, y] = pt(th, v); return `${i ? "L" : "M"}${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`; }).join("");
  const dm = curve.map(([th, v], i) => { const [x, y] = pt(-th, v); return `${i ? "L" : "M"}${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`; }).join("");
  return (
    <div>
      {title && <div className="mb-1 px-1 text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-300">{title}</div>}
      <svg width={size} height={size} className="mx-auto block rounded-md bg-[#111217]">
        {ring.map((v) => <circle key={v} cx={c} cy={c} r={rad(v)} fill="none" stroke="#262832" />)}
        {ring.map((v) => <text key={"t" + v} x={c + 2} y={c - rad(v) - 2} fontSize="8.5" fill="#6b6b76" fontFamily="ui-monospace,monospace">{v} dB</text>)}
        {[0, 30, 60, 90, 120, 150, 180, 210, 240, 270, 300, 330].map((a) => {
          const [x, y] = pt(a, rMax);
          return (
            <g key={a}>
              <line x1={c} y1={c} x2={x} y2={y} stroke="#1f2028" />
              {a <= 180 && <text x={c - Math.cos((a * Math.PI) / 180) * (R + 11)} y={c - Math.sin((a * Math.PI) / 180) * (R + 11) + 3} fontSize="8.5" fill="#7b7b86" textAnchor="middle">{a}°</text>}
            </g>
          );
        })}
        <line x1={c - R - 4} y1={c} x2={c + R + 4} y2={c} stroke="#3a3b46" />
        <text x={c - R} y={c + 12} fontSize="8.5" fill="#7b7b86">upstream</text>
        <path d={dm} fill="none" stroke="#004AFD" strokeOpacity="0.35" strokeDasharray="3 3" />
        <path d={d} fill="#004AFD22" stroke="#4d8bff" strokeWidth="1.6" />
        {points.map((p, i) => { const [x, y] = pt(p.th, p.v); return <g key={i}><circle cx={x} cy={y} r="4" fill={p.color} stroke="#fff" strokeWidth="1" /><text x={x + 6} y={y - 4} fontSize="9" fill={p.color}>{p.label}</text></g>; })}
      </svg>
    </div>
  );
}

Plotting is done. Now the shared workspace module (EDF-90 singleton, domain sizing, NGV field metadata, vane mesh builder), followed by Workspace 1, the Boundary Rule Builder.
