import { buildEdf90, CadModel, Box } from "../geom/edf90";
import { MeshData } from "../gl/primitives";
import { FS, MASK, RunData, SurfStation } from "../cases/ngv";
import { V3 } from "../lib/math";
export const C = { obsidian: "#111217", carbon: "#18181C", blue: "#004AFD", red: "#EF233C", amber: "#f5a524", green: "#22c55e" };
let edf: CadModel | null = null;
export const getEdf = () => (edf ??= buildEdf90());
export interface Domain { L: number; up: number; down: number; lat: number; vert: number }
export const defaultDomain = (): Domain => ({ L: +getEdf().length.toFixed(4), up: 5, down: 15, lat: 5, vert: 5 });
export function domainBox(d: Domain): Box {
  const m = getEdf();
  return { min: [m.bbox.min[0] - d.up * d.L, -d.vert * d.L, -d.lat * d.L], max: [m.bbox.max[0] + d.down * d.L, d.vert * d.L, d.lat * d.L] };
}
export const BC_TYPES: Record<string, { label: string; color: string }> = {
  "velocity-inlet": { label: "Velocity inlet", color: "#22c55e" },
  "pressure-outlet": { label: "Pressure outlet", color: "#f59e0b" },
  wall: { label: "No-slip wall", color: "#9aa0ad" },
  "mrf-wall": { label: "Wall (MRF rotating)", color: "#38bdf8" },
  symmetry: { label: "Slip / symmetry", color: "#a78bfa" },
  interface: { label: "Interface", color: "#f472b6" },
};
// ----------------------------------------------------------------- NGV field metadata
export interface FieldMeta { label: string; unit: string; range: [number, number]; cmap: string }
export const FIELD_META: Record<string, FieldMeta> = {
  Cp: { label: "Cₚ", unit: "–", range: [-2.0, 1.0], cmap: "turbo" },
  Umag: { label: "|U|", unit: "m/s", range: [0, 1.75 * FS.U], cmap: "viridis" },
  Ux: { label: "Uₓ", unit: "m/s", range: [-0.2 * FS.U, 1.7 * FS.U], cmap: "viridis" },
  p: { label: "p", unit: "Pa", range: [FS.p - 2 * FS.q, FS.p + FS.q], cmap: "turbo" },
  T: { label: "T", unit: "K", range: [FS.T0 - 85, FS.T0], cmap: "inferno" },
  Mach: { label: "Mach", unit: "–", range: [0, 0.56], cmap: "viridis" },
  p0loss: { label: "Yₚ₀ loss", unit: "–", range: [0, 0.25], cmap: "inferno" },
  k: { label: "k", unit: "m²/s²", range: [0, 0.012 * FS.U * FS.U], cmap: "inferno" },
  rho: { label: "ρ", unit: "kg/m³", range: [FS.rho * 0.85, FS.rho * 1.05], cmap: "viridis" },
};
export const SURF_META: Record<string, { label: string; unit: string; key: keyof SurfStation; range: [number, number]; cmap: string }> = {
  cp: { label: "Cₚ", unit: "–", key: "cp", range: [-2.0, 1.0], cmap: "turbo" },
  Tw: { label: "T_w", unit: "K", key: "Tw", range: [1050, 1420], cmap: "inferno" },
  yplus: { label: "y⁺", unit: "–", key: "yplus", range: [0, 2.5], cmap: "viridis" },
  Nu: { label: "Nu_c", unit: "–", key: "Nu", range: [0, 6000], cmap: "inferno" },
  tau: { label: "τ_w", unit: "Pa", key: "tau", range: [0, 900], cmap: "viridis" },
};
/** Extruded vane (2.5D) with per-vertex surface scalar. */
export function vaneMesh(run: RunData, key: keyof SurfStation, nzs = 10): MeshData {
  const { x, y } = run.geo, n = x.length, c = FS.c;
  const vals = run.surf.nodeVals(key);
  const P: number[] = [], N: number[] = [], S: number[] = [], I: number[] = [];
  for (let k = 0; k <= nzs; k++) {
    const z = (FS.span * k) / nzs;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const a = Math.max(0, i - 1), b = Math.min(n - 1, i + 1);
      const tx = x[b] - x[a], ty = y[b] - y[a], l = Math.hypot(tx, ty) || 1;
      P.push(x[i] * c, y[i] * c, z);
      N.push(-ty / l, tx / l, 0);
      S.push(vals[i]);
    }
  }
  for (let k = 0; k < nzs; k++)
    for (let i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {
      const a = k * n + i, b = a + n;
      I.push(a, b, a + 1, a + 1, b, b + 1);
    }
  return { pos: new Float32Array(P), nrm: new Float32Array(N), scalar: new Float32Array(S), idx: new Uint32Array(I) };
}
/** Surface point (meters) at x/c on the upper/lower side for synced plot cursors. */
export function surfacePoint(run: RunData, xc: number, upper: boolean, z: number): V3 {
  const { x, y } = run.geo;
  const n = x.length;
  const half = Math.floor(n / 2);
  let best = 0, bd = Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const isUp = i >= half;
    if (isUp !== upper) continue;
    const d = Math.abs(x[i] - xc);
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = i; }
  }
  return [x[best] * FS.c, y[best] * FS.c, z];
}
export function fieldRange(f: Float32Array): [number, number] {
  let a = Infinity, b = -Infinity;
  for (let i = 0; i < f.length; i++) { const v = f[i]; if (v < MASK * 0.1) { if (v < a) a = v; if (v > b) b = v; } }
  return [a, b];
}
export function absPercentile(a: Float32Array, b: Float32Array, q = 0.985): number {
  const d: number[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i += 3) if (a[i] < 1e29 && b[i] < 1e29) d.push(Math.abs(b[i] - a[i]));
  d.sort((x, y) => x - y);
  return Math.max(1e-12, d[Math.floor(d.length * q)] ?? 1);
}

Created src/workspaces/RuleBuilder.tsx
Created src/workspaces/RuleBuilder.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Viewport, Scene, pickKind, Label } from "../gl/Viewport";
import { OrbitCamera } from "../gl/camera";
import { GpuMesh, Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { LineBatch } from "../gl/primitives";
import { buildFarfield, patchSamples, Patch, Box } from "../geom/edf90";
import { compileRule, matchRule, PatchCtx, RuleError, RULE_FUNCS, RULE_VARS } from "../rules/dsl";
import { describe, synthesize, Candidate, PatchDesc } from "../rules/synth";
import { BC_TYPES, C, Domain, domainBox, getEdf } from "./shared";
import { Badge, Btn, KV, Section, Select, Toggle } from "../ui/controls";
import { dot, fmt, sub, V3 } from "../lib/math";
interface BC { id: number; name: string; type: string; rule: string }
const DEFAULT_BCS: BC[] = [
  { id: 1, name: "farfield_inlet", type: "velocity-inlet", rule: "normal.x < -0.995 and abs(x - bbox.x_min) < 1e-4" },
  { id: 2, name: "farfield_outlet", type: "pressure-outlet", rule: "normal.x > 0.995 and abs(x - bbox.x_max) < 1e-4" },
  { id: 3, name: "farfield_slip", type: "symmetry", rule: 'step_tag ~ "FARFIELD_SIDE_*"' },
  { id: 4, name: "duct_walls", type: "wall", rule: 'step_tag ~ "DUCT_*"' },
  { id: 5, name: "rotor_blades", type: "mrf-wall", rule: "r_yz in [0.019, 0.0446] and x in [0.012, 0.04]" },
  { id: 6, name: "stator_vanes", type: "wall", rule: 'step_tag ~ "STATOR_*"' },
];
const hexRgb = (h: string) => [parseInt(h.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(h.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(h.slice(5, 7), 16)];
export default function RuleBuilder({ active, domain }: { active: boolean; domain: Domain }) {
  const model = getEdf();
  const [farfield, setFarfield] = useState(true);
  const [bcs, setBcs] = useState<BC[]>(DEFAULT_BCS);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState("cyl_radius_yz(0, 0) in [0.018, 0.022]");
  const [draftName, setDraftName] = useState("hub_wall");
  const [draftType, setDraftType] = useState("wall");
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(-1);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState(-1);
  const [xray, setXray] = useState(false);
  const ff = useMemo(() => (farfield ? buildFarfield(domainBox(domain), model.patches.length) : null), [farfield, domain, model]);
  const patches: Patch[] = useMemo(() => [...model.patches, ...(ff?.patches ?? [])], [model, ff]);
  const ctxs: PatchCtx[] = useMemo(() => [
    ...model.patches.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, tag: p.tag, name: p.name, area: p.area, samples: patchSamples(model.mesh, p) })),
    ...(ff ? ff.patches.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, tag: p.tag, name: p.name, area: p.area, samples: patchSamples(ff.mesh, { ...p, v0: p.v0, v1: p.v1 }) })) : []),
  ], [model, ff]);
  const scope: Box = useMemo(() => (ff ? domainBox(domain) : model.bbox), [ff, domain, model]);
  const evalRule = (r: string) => matchRule(compileRule(r), ctxs, scope, model.bbox);
  const bcMatches = useMemo(() => bcs.map((b) => { try { return { ids: evalRule(b.rule), err: "" }; } catch (e) { return { ids: [] as number[], err: (e as Error).message }; } }), [bcs, ctxs, scope]);
  const claimed = useMemo(() => {
    const m = new Map<number, number[]>();
    bcs.forEach((b, i) => { if (b.id === editing) return; bcMatches[i].ids.forEach((f) => m.set(f, [...(m.get(f) ?? []), i])); });
    return m;
  }, [bcs, bcMatches, editing]);
  const draftRes = useMemo(() => {
    try { return { ids: evalRule(draft), err: null as RuleError | null }; } catch (e) { return { ids: [] as number[], err: e as RuleError }; }
  }, [draft, ctxs, scope]);
  const overlaps = useMemo(() => {
    const o = new Map<number, number[]>();
    draftRes.ids.forEach((f) => (claimed.get(f) ?? []).forEach((bi) => o.set(bi, [...(o.get(bi) ?? []), f])));
    return o;
  }, [draftRes, claimed]);
  const desc: PatchDesc | null = useMemo(() => {
    if (selected < 0) return null;
    const p = patches.find((q) => q.id === selected);
    const c = ctxs.find((q) => q.id === selected);
    if (!p || !c) return null;
    return describe(p.id, p.name, p.tag, p.area, p.centroid, c.samples, patches.map((q) => ({ id: q.id, tag: q.tag, area: q.area })));
  }, [selected, patches, ctxs]);
  const cands: Candidate[] = useMemo(() => (desc ? synthesize(desc, scope, model.bbox, evalRule) : []), [desc, scope, ctxs]);
  // ------------------------------------------------ scene (imperative, reads S.current)
  const cam = useMemo(() => { const c = new OrbitCamera(); c.frame([0.045, 0.02, 0], 0.11); c.yaw = 0.75; c.pitch = 0.3; return c; }, []);
  const S = useRef({ colors: new Uint8Array(64 * 4), dirty: true, hover: -1, xray: false, selEdges: [] as number[], ff: null as null | typeof ff, ffMesh: null as GpuMesh | null, ffDirty: true, L: domain.L, labels: [] as Label[], camV: -1 });
  const scene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    let r: Renderer, cad: GpuMesh, tex: WebGLTexture;
    const edges = new Float32Array(model.patches.flatMap((p) => p.edges));
    const floor = new LineBatch().grid(0.16, 0.01, model.bbox.min[1] - 0.004, [1, 1, 1, 0.05]).data;
    return {
      init(rr) { r = rr; cad = r.createMesh(model.mesh); tex = r.createFaceTable(64); S.current.dirty = true; S.current.ffDirty = true; },
      draw(ctx) {
        const s = S.current;
        if (s.ffDirty) { if (s.ffMesh) r.deleteMesh(s.ffMesh); s.ffMesh = s.ff ? r.createMesh(s.ff.mesh) : null; s.ffDirty = false; s.dirty = true; }
        if (s.ff && cam.version !== s.camV) {
          s.camV = cam.version;
          const eye = cam.eye();
          s.ff.patches.forEach((p) => { const n: V3 = [p.sign * (model.mesh.nrm[0] * 0), 0, 0]; void n; const nn = ffNormal(p.tag); s.colors[p.id * 4 + 3] = dot(nn, sub(p.centroid, eye)) > 0 ? 255 : 0; });
          s.dirty = true;
        }
        if (s.dirty && !ctx.picking) { r.setFaceColors(tex, s.colors); s.dirty = false; }
        if (s.ffMesh) r.drawSurface(s.ffMesh, { faceTex: tex, mode: 0, hover: s.hover, grid: s.L * 0.5 });
        r.drawLines(floor, 1);
        r.drawSurface(cad, { faceTex: tex, mode: 0, hover: s.hover, xray: s.xray ? 0.28 : 0 });
        r.drawLines(edges, 1.1);
        if (s.selEdges.length) r.drawLines(s.selEdges, 2.4, { xray: 0.35 });
      },
      onHover(v) { const k = pickKind(v); S.current.hover = k.face; setHover(k.face); },
      onClick(v) { const k = pickKind(v); setSelected(k.face); },
      labels: () => S.current.labels,
      frame: () => ({ c: [0.045, 0.02, 0], r: 0.11 }),
      onKey(k) { if (k === "z") setXray((x) => !x); },
      hud: () => "W1 · Spatial BC rules",
    };
  }, [model]);
  const ffNormal = (tag: string): V3 => (({ FARFIELD_XMIN: [-1, 0, 0], FARFIELD_XMAX: [1, 0, 0], FARFIELD_SIDE_1: [0, -1, 0], FARFIELD_SIDE_2: [0, 1, 0], FARFIELD_SIDE_3: [0, 0, -1], FARFIELD_SIDE_4: [0, 0, 1] } as Record<string, V3>)[tag]);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.ff = ff; S.current.ffDirty = true; S.current.L = domain.L; S.current.camV = -1; }, [ff, domain.L]);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.xray = xray; }, [xray]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const s = S.current;
    const draftSet = new Set(draftRes.ids);
    for (const p of patches) {
      const owners = claimed.get(p.id) ?? [];
      let col = p.kind === "farfield" ? [70, 74, 84] : [141, 145, 156];
      if (draftSet.has(p.id)) col = owners.length ? hexRgb(C.red) : hexRgb(C.blue);
      else if (owners.length > 1) col = hexRgb(C.red);
      else if (owners.length === 1) {
        const c = hexRgb(BC_TYPES[bcs[owners[0]].type].color);
        col = p.kind === "farfield" ? c.map((v) => v * 0.45) : c.map((v) => v * 0.55 + 141 * 0.45);
      }
      s.colors.set([col[0], col[1], col[2], p.kind === "farfield" ? s.colors[p.id * 4 + 3] : 255], p.id * 4);
    }
    s.dirty = true;
    s.camV = -1;
    const sp = patches.find((p) => p.id === selected);
    s.selEdges = sp ? (sp.edges.length ? sp.edges.map((v, i) => (i % 10 >= 6 ? [1, 1, 1, 1][i % 10 - 6] : v)) : []) : [];
    const hp = patches.find((p) => p.id === hover);
    s.labels = hp ? [{ key: "hov", pos: hp.centroid, text: `${hp.tag} · ${hp.name}${(claimed.get(hp.id) ?? []).length ? " → " + (claimed.get(hp.id) ?? []).map((i) => bcs[i].name).join(", ") : " · unassigned"}`, color: draftSet.has(hp.id) ? C.blue : undefined }] : [];
  }, [patches, claimed, draftRes, selected, hover, bcs]);
  const commit = () => {
    if (draftRes.err) return;
    if (editing !== null) { setBcs((b) => b.map((x) => (x.id === editing ? { ...x, rule: draft, name: draftName, type: draftType } : x))); setEditing(null); }
    else setBcs((b) => [...b, { id: Date.now(), name: draftName || "bc_" + b.length, type: draftType, rule: draft }]);
  };
  const subtractFrom = (bi: number) => setBcs((b) => b.map((x, i) => (i === bi ? { ...x, rule: `(${x.rule}) and not (${draft})` } : x)));
  const unassigned = patches.filter((p) => !(claimed.get(p.id) ?? []).length && !draftRes.ids.includes(p.id));
  const conflicts = [...claimed.entries()].filter(([, o]) => o.length > 1);
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0">
      <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Viewport scene={scene} camera={cam} active={active} />
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-3 space-y-1.5">
          <div className="rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 backdrop-blur">
            <b className="text-white">Click</b> a CAD patch → rule synthesis · <b className="text-white">drag</b> orbit · <b className="text-white">RMB</b> pan · <b className="text-white">Z</b> x-ray · <b className="text-white">F</b> frame
          </div>
          <div className="flex gap-2 text-[10px]">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-black/50 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-zinc-300"><i className="h-2 w-2 rounded-sm" style={{ background: C.blue }} />draft match</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-black/50 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-zinc-300"><i className="h-2 w-2 rounded-sm" style={{ background: C.red }} />overlap with other BC</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-black/50 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-zinc-300"><i className="h-2 w-2 rounded-sm bg-[#8d919c]" />unassigned</span>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-6 right-3 flex gap-1.5">
          <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setXray(!xray)}>{xray ? "◉" : "○"} X-ray</Btn>
          <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setFarfield(!farfield)}>{farfield ? "Hide" : "Show"} farfield patches</Btn>
        </div>
      </div>
      <aside className="w-[420px] shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-[#26262d] bg-[#18181C]">
        <Section title="Rule draft" accent={draftRes.err ? C.red : overlaps.size ? C.red : C.blue} right={<Badge color={draftRes.err ? C.red : overlaps.size ? C.red : C.blue}>{draftRes.err ? "error" : `${draftRes.ids.length} face${draftRes.ids.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`}</Badge>}>
          <div className="flex gap-1.5">
            <input value={draftName} onChange={(e) => setDraftName(e.target.value)} className="w-32 rounded border border-[#2c2c34] bg-[#121216] px-1.5 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-100 outline-none focus:border-[#004AFD]" />
            <Select value={draftType} onChange={setDraftType} options={Object.entries(BC_TYPES).map(([v, t]) => ({ v, label: t.label }))} className="flex-1" />
          </div>
          <textarea value={draft} onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value)} spellCheck={false} rows={3}
            className="w-full resize-y rounded border bg-[#0e0e12] p-2 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-relaxed text-[#cfe0ff] outline-none"
            style={{ borderColor: draftRes.err ? C.red : overlaps.size ? C.red + "aa" : "#2c2c34" }} />
          {draftRes.err && (
            <div className="rounded border border-[#EF233C]/40 bg-[#EF233C]/10 p-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-[#ff8a96]">
              <div>{draft.slice(0, Math.max(0, draftRes.err.pos))}<span className="bg-[#EF233C]/40 text-white">{draft.slice(Math.max(0, draftRes.err.pos), Math.max(0, draftRes.err.pos) + 1) || " "}</span></div>
              <div className="mt-1">✕ {draftRes.err.message}</div>
            </div>
          )}
          {!draftRes.err && overlaps.size > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-1.5 rounded border border-[#EF233C]/50 bg-[#EF233C]/10 p-2 text-[11px] text-[#ffb3bb]">
              {[...overlaps.entries()].map(([bi, faces]) => (
                <div key={bi}>
                  <div>⚠ Overlaps BC <b className="text-white">{bcs[bi].name}</b> on {faces.length} face{faces.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}: <span className="font-mono">{faces.map((f) => patches.find((p) => p.id === f)?.tag).join(", ")}</span></div>
                  <div className="mt-1 flex gap-1"><Btn variant="danger" onClick={() => subtractFrom(bi)}>Subtract from '{bcs[bi].name}'</Btn></div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <Btn variant="primary" onClick={commit} disabled={!!draftRes.err || draftRes.ids.length === 0}>{editing !== null ? "Update BC" : "Commit as BC"}</Btn>
            {editing !== null && <Btn onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel edit</Btn>}
            <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] text-zinc-500">n̂ = fluid-outward normal</span>
          </div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="Smart Spatial Rule Synthesizer" right={desc ? <Badge color="#9aa0ad">{desc.kind}</Badge> : undefined}>
          {!desc && <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">Pick a face in the viewport. The synthesizer inspects normal cone, centroid, bbox, cylindrical radius and STEP tag and proposes topology-independent rules instead of a brittle face_group_id.</div>}
          {desc && (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 rounded bg-[#121216] p-2">
                <KV k="STEP tag" v={desc.tag} />
                <KV k="face_group_id" v={<span className="text-zinc-500 line-through">{desc.id}</span>} />
                <KV k="area" v={`${fmt(desc.area * 1e6, 4)} mm²`} />
                <KV k="normal cone" v={`${desc.coneDeg.toFixed(2)}°`} />
                <KV k="n̄" v={desc.meanN.map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")} />
                <KV k="centroid" v={desc.centroid.map((v) => (v * 1e3).toFixed(1)).join(", ") + " mm"} />
                {desc.radius !== undefined && <KV k={`r (${desc.axis}-axis)`} v={`${(desc.radius * 1e3).toFixed(3)} mm`} />}
                {desc.rMin !== undefined && desc.radius === undefined && <KV k="r_yz range" v={`${(desc.rMin * 1e3).toFixed(1)}–${(desc.rMax! * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm`} />}
                <KV k="siblings" v={desc.siblings.length > 1 ? `×${desc.siblings.length} pattern` : "none"} />
                <KV k="bbox x" v={`${(desc.bbox.min[0] * 1e3).toFixed(1)}…${(desc.bbox.max[0] * 1e3).toFixed(1)}`} />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {cands.map((c, i) => (
                  <button key={i} onClick={() => setDraft(c.rule)} className={"w-full rounded border p-2 text-left transition-colors " + (draft === c.rule ? "border-[#004AFD] bg-[#004AFD]/10" : "border-[#2a2a32] bg-[#141418] hover:border-[#3d3d48]")}>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-200">{c.label}</span>
                      <span className="ml-auto flex gap-1">
                        {c.unique && <Badge color={C.green}>unique</Badge>}
                        {c.pattern && <Badge color="#38bdf8">pattern</Badge>}
                        {!c.unique && !c.pattern && <Badge color={C.amber}>{c.matches.length} faces</Badge>}
                        <Badge color={c.robustness === "geometric" ? "#a3e635" : "#9aa0ad"}>{c.robustness}</Badge>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 break-all font-mono text-[11px] text-[#9ec1ff]">{c.rule}</div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-zinc-500">{c.note}</div>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </Section>
        <Section title={`Boundary conditions (${bcs.length})`} right={conflicts.length ? <Badge color={C.red}>{conflicts.length} conflict</Badge> : <Badge color={C.green}>consistent</Badge>}>
          {bcs.map((b, i) => {
            const m = bcMatches[i];
            return (
              <div key={b.id} className={"rounded border p-2 " + (editing === b.id ? "border-[#004AFD]" : "border-[#2a2a32]") + " bg-[#141418]"}>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                  <i className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-sm" style={{ background: BC_TYPES[b.type].color }} />
                  <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100">{b.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-500">{BC_TYPES[b.type].label}</span>
                  <span className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Badge color={m.err ? C.red : m.ids.length ? "#9aa0ad" : C.amber}>{m.err ? "error" : `${m.ids.length}`}</Badge>
                    <Btn onClick={() => { setEditing(b.id); setDraft(b.rule); setDraftName(b.name); setDraftType(b.type); }}>edit</Btn>
                    <Btn onClick={() => setBcs((x) => x.filter((y) => y.id !== b.id))}>✕</Btn>
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1 break-all font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-400">{b.rule}</div>
                {m.err && <div className="mt-1 text-[10px] text-[#ff8a96]">{m.err}</div>}
              </div>
            );
          })}
          <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            Unassigned: {unassigned.length === 0 ? <span className="text-[#22c55e]">none — every patch has exactly one BC ✓</span> : <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">{unassigned.map((p) => p.tag).join(", ")}</span>}
          </div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="Rule language reference" defaultOpen={false}>
          <div className="text-[10.5px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">
            <div className="mb-1 text-zinc-300">Variables</div>
            <div className="font-mono">{RULE_VARS.join(" · ")}</div>
            <div className="mb-1 mt-2 text-zinc-300">Functions</div>
            <div className="font-mono">{RULE_FUNCS.join(" · ")}</div>
            <div className="mb-1 mt-2 text-zinc-300">Operators</div>
            <div className="font-mono">and or not · &lt; &lt;= &gt; &gt;= == != · x in [a, b] · step_tag ~ "GLOB_*" · (x, y, z) vectors</div>
            <div className="mt-2">A face group matches when the predicate holds on ≥ 99.5 % of its sample vertices. Rules are re-evaluated after every re-mesh / CAD update, so ids never leak into the case setup.</div>
          </div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="Scope" defaultOpen={false}>
          <Toggle label="Include farfield patches (from W3 domain)" value={farfield} onChange={setFarfield} />
          <KV k="bbox (scope)" v={`${scope.min.map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")} → ${scope.max.map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")}`} />
          <KV k="cad bbox" v={`${model.bbox.min.map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")} → ${model.bbox.max.map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")}`} />
          <KV k="patches" v={`${patches.length} (${model.patches.length} CAD + ${ff?.patches.length ?? 0} farfield)`} />
        </Section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

Next is Workspace 3: interactive domain/MRF gizmos and the physics actors (DPM spray cone with Rosin–Rammler, FWH mics with a Gutin directivity polar, anisotropic porous axes).
Created src/workspaces/DomainActors.tsx
Created src/workspaces/DomainActors.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Viewport, Scene, pickKind, DragSession, ViewCtx } from "../gl/Viewport";
import { OrbitCamera } from "../gl/camera";
import { GpuMesh, HANDLE_BASE, Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { coneMesh, cubeMesh, LineBatch, rgba, sphereMesh, tubeMesh } from "../gl/primitives";
import { add, basisMatrix, cross, DEG, dist, dot, frameMatrix, identity, len, m3mul, m3T, madd, norm, Ray, rayAxisT, rayPlane, rotEuler, rotX, scl, sub, V3 } from "../lib/math";
import { C, Domain, domainBox, getEdf } from "./shared";
import { Badge, Btn, KV, Section, Seg, Slider } from "../ui/controls";
import { Plot, PolarPlot } from "../plots/Plot";
import { lutColor } from "../lib/colormap";
interface Mrf { radius: number; x0: number; x1: number; rpm: number; vinf: number }
interface Inj { pos: V3; yaw: number; pitch: number; half: number; len: number; d32: number; n: number; mdot: number; U: number }
interface Mic { id: number; pos: V3 }
interface Por { pos: V3; size: V3; yaw: number; pitch: number; roll: number; K: number[] }
type Focus = "domain" | "mrf" | "inj" | "mic" | "por";
const A0 = 340.3;
const dirOf = (yaw: number, pitch: number): V3 => [Math.cos(yaw * DEG) * Math.cos(pitch * DEG), Math.sin(pitch * DEG), -Math.sin(yaw * DEG) * Math.cos(pitch * DEG)];
const yawPitch = (d: V3) => ({ yaw: Math.atan2(-d[2], d[0]) / DEG, pitch: Math.asin(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, d[1]))) / DEG });
function gammaFn(z: number): number {
  if (z < 0.5) return Math.PI / (Math.sin(Math.PI * z) * gammaFn(1 - z));
  const g = [676.5203681218851, -1259.1392167224028, 771.32342877765313, -176.61502916214059, 12.507343278686905, -0.13857109526572012, 9.9843695780195716e-6, 1.5056327351493116e-7];
  z -= 1;
  let x = 0.99999999999980993;
  g.forEach((c, i) => (x += c / (z + i + 1)));
  const t = z + 7.5;
  return Math.sqrt(2 * Math.PI) * Math.pow(t, z + 0.5) * Math.exp(-t) * x;
}
function besselJ(n: number, x: number): number {
  let term = Math.pow(x / 2, n) / gammaFn(n + 1), s = term;
  for (let k = 1; k < 40; k++) { term *= -(x * x) / (4 * k * (k + n)); s += term; if (Math.abs(term) < 1e-16 * Math.abs(s)) break; }
  return s;
}
/** Gutin rotor loading-noise harmonics (unducted estimate) + broadband floor. */
export function gutin(rpm: number, B: number, rTip: number, S: number, thDeg: number) {
  const Om = (rpm / 60) * 2 * Math.PI, Re = 0.8 * rTip, th = thDeg * DEG;
  const T = 35 * (rpm / 35000) ** 2, P = 2200 * (rpm / 35000) ** 3, Q = P / Math.max(Om, 1e-6);
  let e = 0;
  for (let m = 1; m <= 3; m++) {
    const pm = ((m * B * Om) / (2 * Math.SQRT2 * Math.PI * A0 * S)) * Math.abs(-T * Math.cos(th) + (Q * A0) / (Om * Re * Re)) * Math.abs(besselJ(m * B, (m * B * Om * Re * Math.sin(th)) / A0));
    e += (pm / 2e-5) ** 2;
  }
  const Mt = (Om * rTip) / A0;
  const bb = 58 + 50 * Math.log10(Math.max(Mt, 1e-3) / 0.4) - 20 * Math.log10(S) + 10 * Math.log10(0.3 + Math.sin(th) ** 2);
  return 10 * Math.log10(e + Math.pow(10, bb / 10));
}
export default function DomainActors({ active, domain, setDomain }: { active: boolean; domain: Domain; setDomain: (d: Domain) => void }) {
  const model = getEdf();
  const [mrf, setMrf] = useState<Mrf>({ radius: 0.0448, x0: 0.012, x1: 0.04, rpm: 32000, vinf: 30 });
  const [inj, setInj] = useState<Inj>({ pos: [-0.12, 0.03, 0.0], yaw: 0, pitch: -9, half: 14, len: 0.09, d32: 40, n: 2.6, mdot: 2.0, U: 25 });
  const [mics, setMics] = useState<Mic[]>([{ id: 1, pos: [model.xRotor - 0.7071, 0.7071, 0] }, { id: 2, pos: [model.xRotor, 1.0, 0] }, { id: 3, pos: [model.xRotor + 0.7071, 0.7071, 0] }]);
  const [por, setPor] = useState<Por>({ pos: [0.06, -0.095, 0], size: [0.04, 0.025, 0.06], yaw: 0, pitch: 0, roll: 0, K: [1e-7, 1e-10, 1e-10] });
  const [focus, setFocus] = useState<Focus>("mrf");
  const [spin, setSpin] = useState(true);
  const [hoverH, setHoverH] = useState(-1);
  const src: V3 = [model.xRotor, 0, 0];
  // derived engineering numbers
  const Om = (mrf.rpm / 60) * 2 * Math.PI, Utip = Om * model.rTip, Mtip = Utip / A0, Mhel = Math.hypot(Utip, mrf.vinf) / A0;
  const bpf = (model.blades * mrf.rpm) / 60, lam = A0 / bpf;
  const box = domainBox(domain);
  const blockage = (Math.PI * 0.056 ** 2) / ((box.max[1] - box.min[1]) * (box.max[2] - box.min[2]));
  const bladeX: [number, number] = [model.xRotor - 0.0085, model.xRotor + 0.0085];
  const mrfIssues: { lvl: "ok" | "warn" | "err"; msg: string }[] = [];
  if (mrf.radius <= model.rTip) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "err", msg: `Interface r = ${(mrf.radius * 1e3).toFixed(2)} mm cuts the rotor blades (r_tip = ${(model.rTip * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm)` });
  else if (mrf.radius < model.rDuct) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "ok", msg: `Interface sits in the ${((model.rDuct - model.rTip) * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm tip gap ✓` });
  else mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "warn", msg: "Zone encloses the duct shroud — set DUCT_SHROUD as an absolute-frame (stationary) wall" });
  if (mrf.x0 > bladeX[0] || mrf.x1 < bladeX[1]) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "err", msg: "Zone does not enclose the blades axially" });
  if (mrf.x1 > 0.0475) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "warn", msg: "Zone intersects the stator row (x > 47.5 mm) — rotor/stator interface must lie between rows" });
  if (Mtip > 0.85) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "err", msg: `Tip Mach ${Mtip.toFixed(2)} — transonic, use a density-based solver` });
  else if (Mtip > 0.7) mrfIssues.push({ lvl: "warn", msg: `Tip Mach ${Mtip.toFixed(2)} — compressibility significant` });
  const X = inj.d32 * gammaFn(1 - 1 / inj.n);
  const Rp = rotEuler(por.yaw, por.pitch, por.roll);
  const Dg = m3mul(m3mul(Rp, [1 / por.K[0], 0, 0, 0, 1 / por.K[1], 0, 0, 0, 1 / por.K[2]]), m3T(Rp));
  const micData = mics.map((m) => {
    const d = sub(m.pos, src), S = len(d);
    const th = Math.acos(Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, -d[0] / S))) / DEG;
    return { ...m, S, th, spl: gutin(mrf.rpm, model.blades, model.rTip, S, th) };
  });
  const meanS = micData.reduce((a, m) => a + m.S, 0) / Math.max(1, micData.length);
  // ---------------------------------------------- scene
  const cam = useMemo(() => { const c = new OrbitCamera(); c.frame([0.03, 0.0, 0], 0.22); c.yaw = 0.95; c.pitch = 0.32; return c; }, []);
  const P = useRef({ mrf, inj, mics, por, domain, focus, spin, hoverH, box });
  P.current = { mrf, inj, mics, por, domain, focus, spin, hoverH, box };
  const setters = useRef({ setMrf, setInj, setMics, setPor, setDomain });
  setters.current = { setMrf, setInj, setMics, setPor, setDomain };
  const scene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    let r: Renderer, cad: GpuMesh, sph: GpuMesh, cone: GpuMesh, tube: GpuMesh, cube: GpuMesh;
    const staticEdges = new Float32Array(model.patches.filter((p) => !p.tag.startsWith("ROTOR")).flatMap((p) => p.edges));
    const rotorEdges = new Float32Array(model.patches.filter((p) => p.tag.startsWith("ROTOR")).flatMap((p) => p.edges));
    const floor = new LineBatch().grid(0.3, 0.02, -0.12, [1, 1, 1, 0.045]).data;
    const drops = Array.from({ length: 160 }, (_, i) => ({ u: Math.random(), a: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, rr: Math.sqrt(Math.random()), ph: Math.random(), seed: (i * 0.618) % 1 }));
    const lb = new LineBatch();
    const handlePos = () => {
      const s = P.current, b = s.box, cy = 0;
      const h: { id: number; p: V3; c: string }[] = [];
      if (s.focus === "domain") {
        const cx = (b.min[0] + b.max[0]) / 2;
        h.push({ id: 0, p: [b.min[0], cy, 0], c: "#22c55e" }, { id: 1, p: [b.max[0], cy, 0], c: "#f59e0b" }, { id: 2, p: [cx, b.min[1], 0], c: "#a78bfa" }, { id: 3, p: [cx, b.max[1], 0], c: "#a78bfa" }, { id: 4, p: [cx, cy, b.min[2]], c: "#a78bfa" }, { id: 5, p: [cx, cy, b.max[2]], c: "#a78bfa" });
      }
      if (s.focus === "mrf") h.push({ id: 10, p: [(s.mrf.x0 + s.mrf.x1) / 2, s.mrf.radius, 0], c: "#38bdf8" }, { id: 11, p: [s.mrf.x0, 0, 0], c: "#38bdf8" }, { id: 12, p: [s.mrf.x1, 0, 0], c: "#38bdf8" });
      if (s.focus === "inj") {
        const d = dirOf(s.inj.yaw, s.inj.pitch), tip = madd(s.inj.pos, d, s.inj.len);
        const perp = norm(cross(d, Math.abs(d[1]) < 0.9 ? [0, 1, 0] : [1, 0, 0]));
        h.push({ id: 20, p: s.inj.pos, c: "#f97316" }, { id: 21, p: tip, c: "#fdba74" }, { id: 22, p: madd(tip, perp, s.inj.len * Math.tan(s.inj.half * DEG)), c: "#fde68a" });
      }
      s.mics.forEach((m, i) => h.push({ id: 100 + i, p: m.pos, c: "#e879f9" }));
      if (s.focus === "por") {
        const R = rotEuler(s.por.yaw, s.por.pitch, s.por.roll);
        h.push({ id: 30, p: s.por.pos, c: "#facc15" }, { id: 31, p: madd(s.por.pos, [R[0], R[3], R[6]], 0.045), c: "#ef4444" });
      }
      return h;
    };
    return {
      init(rr) { r = rr; cad = r.createMesh(model.mesh); sph = r.createMesh(sphereMesh()); cone = r.createMesh(coneMesh()); tube = r.createMesh(tubeMesh()); cube = r.createMesh(cubeMesh()); },
      draw(ctx: ViewCtx) {
        const s = P.current, t = ctx.t / 1000;
        const ang = s.spin ? t * (s.mrf.rpm / 32000) * 2.2 : 0;
        const rot = rotX(ang);
        const wpp = (p: V3) => ctx.cam.worldPerPx(dist(p, ctx.eye), ctx.hPx);
        // CAD
        r.drawSurface(cad, { color: [0.62, 0.64, 0.68, 1], count: model.rotor.first, noPick: false, pickId: 0 });
        r.drawSurface(cad, { color: [0.55, 0.62, 0.72, 1], first: model.rotor.first, count: model.rotor.count, model: rot, pickId: 0 });
        r.drawSurface(cad, { color: [0.62, 0.64, 0.68, 1], first: model.rotor.first + model.rotor.count, count: model.mesh.idx.length - model.rotor.first - model.rotor.count, pickId: 0 });
        r.drawLines(floor, 1);
        r.drawLines(staticEdges, 1);
        if (!s.spin) r.drawLines(rotorEdges, 1);
        // handles (pickable)
        for (const h of handlePos()) {
          const sz = wpp(h.p) * (s.hoverH === h.id ? 8 : 6);
          const c = rgba(h.c);
          r.drawSurface(sph, { color: s.hoverH === h.id ? [1, 1, 1, 1] : c, model: basisMatrix(h.p, [0, 0, 1], sz, sz, sz), pickId: HANDLE_BASE + h.id, depthTest: false });
        }
        if (ctx.picking) return;
        lb.clear();
        // domain box
        const b = s.box;
        lb.box(b.min, b.max, s.focus === "domain" ? [0.6, 0.75, 1, 0.9] : [0.6, 0.7, 0.9, 0.35]);
        if (s.focus === "domain") {
          const yb = b.min[1];
          lb.seg([b.min[0], yb, 0], [model.bbox.min[0], yb, 0], [0.13, 0.77, 0.37, 1]).seg([model.bbox.max[0], yb, 0], [b.max[0], yb, 0], [0.96, 0.62, 0.04, 1]);
          lb.seg([model.bbox.min[0], b.min[1], 0], [model.bbox.min[0], b.max[1], 0], [1, 1, 1, 0.15]);
          lb.seg([model.bbox.max[0], b.min[1], 0], [model.bbox.max[0], b.max[1], 0], [1, 1, 1, 0.15]);
        }
        // MRF
        const mc = s.focus === "mrf" ? [0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 1] : [0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.45];
        for (const x of [s.mrf.x0, s.mrf.x1]) lb.circle([x, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0], s.mrf.radius, mc, 72);
        for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const a = (k / 8) * Math.PI * 2; lb.seg([s.mrf.x0, s.mrf.radius * Math.cos(a), s.mrf.radius * Math.sin(a)], [s.mrf.x1, s.mrf.radius * Math.cos(a), s.mrf.radius * Math.sin(a)], [mc[0], mc[1], mc[2], 0.3]); }
        const xa = s.mrf.x0 - 0.012, ra = s.mrf.radius * 0.75, a0 = ang % (Math.PI * 2);
        lb.circle([xa, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0], ra, [0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 0.9], 40, a0, a0 + 4.2);
        // injector cone + droplets
        const d = dirOf(s.inj.yaw, s.inj.pitch), R = s.inj.len * Math.tan(s.inj.half * DEG);
        lb.seg(s.inj.pos, madd(s.inj.pos, d, s.inj.len * 1.15), [0.98, 0.45, 0.09, 0.9]);
        lb.circle(madd(s.inj.pos, d, s.inj.len), d, R, [0.98, 0.45, 0.09, 0.9], 48);
        const ex = norm(cross(d, Math.abs(d[1]) < 0.9 ? [0, 1, 0] : [1, 0, 0])), ey = cross(d, ex);
        const Xd = s.inj.d32 * gammaFn(1 - 1 / s.inj.n);
        for (const q of drops) {
          const f = (t * (s.inj.U / 25) * 0.6 + q.ph) % 1;
          const dd = Xd * Math.pow(-Math.log(1 - Math.min(0.999, q.seed)), 1 / s.inj.n);
          const dirq = norm(add(d, add(scl(ex, Math.cos(q.a) * q.rr * Math.tan(s.inj.half * DEG)), scl(ey, Math.sin(q.a) * q.rr * Math.tan(s.inj.half * DEG)))));
          const p0 = madd(s.inj.pos, dirq, f * s.inj.len * 1.25), p1 = madd(p0, dirq, -0.004 - 0.002 * (dd / 60));
          const col = lutColor("inferno", 0.25 + Math.min(1, dd / (2.2 * s.inj.d32)) * 0.7).match(/\d+/g)!.map((v) => +v / 255);
          lb.seg(p1, p0, [col[0], col[1], col[2], 0.85 * (1 - f * 0.6)]);
        }
        // mics
        s.mics.forEach((m) => {
          const rr = dist(m.pos, src);
          lb.seg(src, m.pos, [0.91, 0.47, 0.98, 0.5]);
          if (s.focus === "mic") { lb.circle(src, [0, 0, 1], rr, [0.91, 0.47, 0.98, 0.35], 96); lb.circle(src, [0, 1, 0], rr, [0.91, 0.47, 0.98, 0.2], 96); lb.circle(src, [1, 0, 0], rr, [0.91, 0.47, 0.98, 0.2], 96); }
        });
        // porous axes
        const Rp = rotEuler(s.por.yaw, s.por.pitch, s.por.roll);
        const axes: V3[] = [[Rp[0], Rp[3], Rp[6]], [Rp[1], Rp[4], Rp[7]], [Rp[2], Rp[5], Rp[8]]];
        const acol = [[0.94, 0.27, 0.27, 1], [0.13, 0.77, 0.37, 1], [0.23, 0.51, 0.96, 1]];
        axes.forEach((e, i) => lb.seg(s.por.pos, madd(s.por.pos, e, 0.04), acol[i]));
        r.drawLines(lb.data, 1.6, { xray: 0.3 });
        // translucent volumes
        const bc: V3 = scl(add(b.min, b.max), 0.5), bh: V3 = scl(sub(b.max, b.min), 0.5);
        r.drawSurface(cube, { model: frameMatrix(bc, [1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1], bh), color: [0.4, 0.55, 0.9, 1], alpha: s.focus === "domain" ? 0.06 : 0.025 });
        r.drawSurface(tube, { model: basisMatrix([s.mrf.x0, 0, 0], [1, 0, 0], s.mrf.radius, s.mrf.radius, s.mrf.x1 - s.mrf.x0), color: [0.22, 0.74, 0.97, 1], alpha: s.focus === "mrf" ? 0.22 : 0.1 });
        r.drawSurface(cone, { model: basisMatrix(s.inj.pos, d, R, R, s.inj.len), color: [0.98, 0.5, 0.15, 1], alpha: 0.16 });
        r.drawSurface(cube, { model: frameMatrix(s.por.pos, Rp, scl(s.por.size, 0.5)), color: [0.98, 0.8, 0.08, 1], alpha: s.focus === "por" ? 0.28 : 0.16 });
        axes.forEach((e, i) => { const tip = madd(s.por.pos, e, 0.04); const h = 0.008; r.drawSurface(cone, { model: basisMatrix(tip, scl(e, -1), h * 0.35, h * 0.35, h), color: acol[i] }); });
        void identity;
      },
      onHover(v) { const k = pickKind(v); P.current.hoverH = k.handle; setHoverH(k.handle); },
      onDragStart(h: number, _ray: Ray, ctx: ViewCtx): DragSession | null {
        const s = P.current, st = setters.current, f = ctx.cam.axes().f;
        const hp = handlePos().find((x) => x.id === h);
        if (!hp) return null;
        const o = hp.p;
        const snap = (v: number) => Math.max(1, Math.round(v * 4) / 4);
        if (h <= 5) {
          const k = h >> 1, ax: V3 = [0, 0, 0];
          ax[k] = 1;
          return { move(ray) {
            const v = o[k] + rayAxisT(ray, o, ax), d = { ...P.current.domain }, cad = model.bbox;
            if (h === 0) d.up = snap((cad.min[0] - v) / d.L); else if (h === 1) d.down = snap((v - cad.max[0]) / d.L);
            else if (k === 1) d.vert = snap(Math.abs(v) / d.L); else d.lat = snap(Math.abs(v) / d.L);
            st.setDomain(d);
          } };
        }
        if (h === 10) return { move(ray) { st.setMrf({ ...P.current.mrf, radius: Math.max(0.025, Math.min(0.09, o[1] + rayAxisT(ray, o, [0, 1, 0]))) }); } };
        if (h === 11 || h === 12) return { move(ray) { const v = o[0] + rayAxisT(ray, o, [1, 0, 0]); const m = { ...P.current.mrf }; if (h === 11) m.x0 = Math.min(v, m.x1 - 0.004); else m.x1 = Math.max(v, m.x0 + 0.004); st.setMrf(m); } };
        const plane = (fn: (p: V3) => void): DragSession => ({ move(ray) { const p = rayPlane(ray, o, f); if (p) fn(p); } });
        if (h === 20) return plane((p) => st.setInj({ ...P.current.inj, pos: p }));
        if (h === 21) return plane((p) => st.setInj({ ...P.current.inj, ...yawPitch(norm(sub(p, P.current.inj.pos))) }));
        if (h === 22) return plane((p) => { const i = P.current.inj, d = dirOf(i.yaw, i.pitch), v = sub(p, i.pos), ax = dot(v, d), pr = len(sub(v, scl(d, ax))); st.setInj({ ...i, half: Math.max(2, Math.min(45, Math.atan2(pr, Math.max(ax, 1e-4)) / DEG)) }); });
        if (h >= 100) return plane((p) => st.setMics(P.current.mics.map((m, i) => (i === h - 100 ? { ...m, pos: p } : m))));
        if (h === 30) return plane((p) => st.setPor({ ...P.current.por, pos: p }));
        if (h === 31) return plane((p) => st.setPor({ ...P.current.por, ...yawPitch(norm(sub(p, P.current.por.pos))) }));
        void s;
        return null;
      },
      labels() {
        const s = P.current, b = s.box, L = s.domain.L;
        const out = [] as { key: string; pos: V3; text: string; color?: string }[];
        if (s.focus === "domain") {
          out.push({ key: "up", pos: [(b.min[0] + model.bbox.min[0]) / 2, b.min[1], 0], text: `upstream ${s.domain.up.toFixed(2)} L = ${(s.domain.up * L).toFixed(2)} m`, color: "#22c55e" });
          out.push({ key: "dn", pos: [(b.max[0] + model.bbox.max[0]) / 2, b.min[1], 0], text: `downstream ${s.domain.down.toFixed(2)} L = ${(s.domain.down * L).toFixed(2)} m`, color: "#f59e0b" });
          out.push({ key: "lat", pos: [(b.min[0] + b.max[0]) / 2, b.max[1], 0], text: `±${s.domain.vert} L (y) · ±${s.domain.lat} L (z)`, color: "#a78bfa" });
        }
        const M = ((s.mrf.rpm / 60) * 2 * Math.PI * model.rTip) / A0;
        out.push({ key: "mrf", pos: [(s.mrf.x0 + s.mrf.x1) / 2, s.mrf.radius + 0.012, 0], text: `MRF ${(s.mrf.rpm / 1000).toFixed(1)}k rpm · M_tip ${M.toFixed(3)}`, color: M > 0.85 ? C.red : M > 0.7 ? C.amber : "#38bdf8" });
        out.push({ key: "inj", pos: madd(s.inj.pos, [0, 1, 0], 0.012), text: `DPM θ ${s.inj.half.toFixed(1)}° · d₃₂ ${s.inj.d32.toFixed(0)} µm`, color: "#f97316" });
        s.mics.forEach((m, i) => { const d = sub(m.pos, src), S = len(d), th = Math.acos(-d[0] / S) / DEG; out.push({ key: "mic" + i, pos: madd(m.pos, [0, 1, 0], 0.03), text: `MIC-${i + 1} r ${S.toFixed(2)} m · θ ${th.toFixed(0)}° · ${gutin(s.mrf.rpm, model.blades, model.rTip, S, th).toFixed(1)} dB`, color: "#e879f9" }); });
        out.push({ key: "por", pos: madd(s.por.pos, [0, -1, 0], 0.025), text: "HX-1 porous · e₁ e₂ e₃", color: "#facc15" });
        return out;
      },
      frame: () => (P.current.focus === "domain" ? { c: scl(add(P.current.box.min, P.current.box.max), 0.5), r: len(sub(P.current.box.max, P.current.box.min)) * 0.5 } : { c: [0.03, 0, 0], r: 0.2 }),
      hud: () => "W3 · Domain & physics actors",
    };
  }, [model]);
  useEffect(() => { if (focus === "domain") { const f = scene.frame!(); cam.frame(f.c, f.r * 0.85); } else if (focus === "mic") cam.frame([0.03, 0.3, 0], 1.0); else if (cam.dist > 1.2) cam.frame([0.03, 0, 0], 0.2); }, [focus]);
  const ddist = (d: number[]) => { const xs: [number, number][] = []; for (let i = 1; i <= 120; i++) { const dd = (i / 120) * 3 * inj.d32; xs.push([dd, (inj.n / X) * Math.pow(dd / X, inj.n - 1) * Math.exp(-Math.pow(dd / X, inj.n))]); } void d; return xs; };
  const curve: [number, number][] = Array.from({ length: 91 }, (_, i) => [i * 2, gutin(mrf.rpm, model.blades, model.rTip, meanS || 1, Math.max(0.5, Math.min(179.5, i * 2)))]);
  const sev = (l: string) => (l === "err" ? C.red : l === "warn" ? C.amber : C.green);
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0">
      <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Viewport scene={scene} camera={cam} active={active} />
        <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <Seg value={focus} onChange={setFocus} options={[{ v: "domain", label: "Farfield box" }, { v: "mrf", label: "MRF zone" }, { v: "inj", label: "DPM injector" }, { v: "mic", label: "FWH mics" }, { v: "por", label: "Porous axes" }]} />
          <div className="pointer-events-none rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 backdrop-blur">Drag the coloured <b className="text-white">handles</b> · domain snaps to ¼ L · <b className="text-white">F</b> frame</div>
        </div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-6 right-3"><Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setSpin(!spin)}>{spin ? "❚❚ Pause" : "▶ Spin"} MRF preview</Btn></div>
      </div>
      <aside className="w-[400px] shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-[#26262d] bg-[#18181C]">
        <Section title="Farfield wind-tunnel box" accent="#a78bfa">
          <Slider label="Characteristic length L" value={domain.L} min={0.05} max={0.5} step={0.005} unit=" m" onChange={(v) => setDomain({ ...domain, L: v })} />
          <Slider label="Upstream" value={domain.up} min={1} max={20} step={0.25} unit=" L" onChange={(v) => setDomain({ ...domain, up: v })} />
          <Slider label="Downstream" value={domain.down} min={2} max={40} step={0.25} unit=" L" onChange={(v) => setDomain({ ...domain, down: v })} />
          <Slider label="Lateral ±z / vertical ±y" value={domain.lat} min={1} max={20} step={0.25} unit=" L" onChange={(v) => setDomain({ ...domain, lat: v, vert: v })} />
          <KV k="box size" v={`${(box.max[0] - box.min[0]).toFixed(2)} × ${(box.max[1] - box.min[1]).toFixed(2)} × ${(box.max[2] - box.min[2]).toFixed(2)} m`} />
          <KV k="blockage ratio" v={`${(blockage * 100).toFixed(3)} %`} color={blockage > 0.02 ? C.red : C.green} />
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">Faces export as FARFIELD_* patches → available to rules in W1 (bbox.x_min = {box.min[0].toFixed(3)} m).</div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="MRF / sliding-mesh rotor zone" accent="#38bdf8">
          <Slider label="RPM" value={mrf.rpm} min={2000} max={60000} step={500} onChange={(v) => setMrf({ ...mrf, rpm: v })} fmt={(v) => v.toLocaleString()} />
          <Slider label="Flight speed V∞" value={mrf.vinf} min={0} max={120} step={1} unit=" m/s" onChange={(v) => setMrf({ ...mrf, vinf: v })} />
          <Slider label="Interface radius" value={mrf.radius * 1e3} min={25} max={90} step={0.1} unit=" mm" onChange={(v) => setMrf({ ...mrf, radius: v / 1e3 })} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 rounded bg-[#121216] p-2">
            <KV k="Ω" v={`${Om.toFixed(0)} rad/s`} />
            <KV k="U_tip = Ω·r_tip" v={`${Utip.toFixed(1)} m/s`} />
            <KV k="M_tip" v={Mtip.toFixed(3)} color={Mtip > 0.85 ? C.red : Mtip > 0.7 ? C.amber : C.green} />
            <KV k="M_helical" v={Mhel.toFixed(3)} />
            <KV k="BPF (B=7)" v={`${bpf.toFixed(0)} Hz`} />
            <KV k="λ_BPF" v={`${(lam * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm`} />
          </div>
          {mrfIssues.map((m, i) => <div key={i} className="text-[11px]" style={{ color: sev(m.lvl) }}>{m.lvl === "ok" ? "✓" : m.lvl === "warn" ? "⚠" : "✕"} {m.msg}</div>)}
        </Section>
        <Section title="DPM spray injector" accent="#f97316">
          <Slider label="Cone half-angle θ_cone" value={inj.half} min={2} max={45} step={0.5} unit="°" onChange={(v) => setInj({ ...inj, half: v })} />
          <Slider label="Rosin–Rammler d₃₂ (SMD)" value={inj.d32} min={5} max={200} step={1} unit=" µm" onChange={(v) => setInj({ ...inj, d32: v })} />
          <Slider label="Spread n" value={inj.n} min={1.5} max={6} step={0.1} onChange={(v) => setInj({ ...inj, n: v })} />
          <Slider label="Injection velocity" value={inj.U} min={2} max={80} step={1} unit=" m/s" onChange={(v) => setInj({ ...inj, U: v })} />
          <Slider label="Mass flow ṁ" value={inj.mdot} min={0.1} max={10} step={0.1} unit=" g/s" onChange={(v) => setInj({ ...inj, mdot: v })} />
          <KV k="axis â" v={dirOf(inj.yaw, inj.pitch).map((v) => v.toFixed(3)).join(", ")} />
          <KV k="X̄ = d₃₂·Γ(1−1/n)" v={`${X.toFixed(1)} µm`} />
          <Plot height={120} x={{ label: "d [µm]", min: 0, max: 3 * inj.d32 }} y={{ label: "pdf", min: 0, max: Math.max(...ddist([]).map((p) => p[1])) * 1.1 }} series={[{ name: "Rosin–Rammler (volume)", pts: ddist([]), color: "#f97316" }]} vlines={[{ v: inj.d32, color: "#fde68a", label: "d₃₂" }]} legend={false} />
        </Section>
        <Section title={`FWH acoustic microphones (${mics.length})`} accent="#e879f9">
          {micData.map((m, i) => (
            <div key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px]">
              <Badge color="#e879f9">MIC-{i + 1}</Badge>
              <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">r {m.S.toFixed(2)} m · θ {m.th.toFixed(0)}° · {(m.S / lam).toFixed(0)} λ</span>
              <span className="ml-auto font-mono text-zinc-100">{m.spl.toFixed(1)} dB</span>
              <Btn onClick={() => setMics(mics.filter((x) => x.id !== m.id))}>✕</Btn>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div className="flex gap-1.5">
            <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setMics([...mics, { id: Date.now(), pos: [model.xRotor, -0.8, 0.4] }])}>+ Mic</Btn>
            <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setMics(Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => { const th = (15 + i * 25) * DEG; return { id: Date.now() + i, pos: [model.xRotor - Math.cos(th), Math.sin(th), 0] as V3 }; }))}>Polar arc 15°…165° @ 1 m</Btn>
          </div>
          <PolarPlot title="Directivity — Gutin loading tones + broadband (OASPL)" curve={curve} rMin={Math.max(20, Math.min(...curve.map((c) => c[1])) - 5)} rMax={Math.max(...curve.map((c) => c[1])) + 5} points={micData.map((m, i) => ({ th: m.th, v: m.spl + 20 * Math.log10(m.S / (meanS || 1)), color: "#e879f9", label: `${i + 1}` }))} />
          <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">Observers may lie outside the CFD domain — the permeable FWH integral propagates analytically. Far-field criterion r &gt; 2λ & r &gt; 2D.</div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="Anisotropic porous media — principal axes" accent="#facc15">
          <Slider label="yaw (about y)" value={por.yaw} min={-180} max={180} step={1} unit="°" onChange={(v) => setPor({ ...por, yaw: v })} />
          <Slider label="pitch (about z)" value={por.pitch} min={-90} max={90} step={1} unit="°" onChange={(v) => setPor({ ...por, pitch: v })} />
          <Slider label="roll (about e₁)" value={por.roll} min={-180} max={180} step={1} unit="°" onChange={(v) => setPor({ ...por, roll: v })} />
          <Slider label="log₁₀ K₁ (along e₁)" value={Math.log10(por.K[0])} min={-12} max={-5} step={0.1} unit=" m²" onChange={(v) => setPor({ ...por, K: [10 ** v, por.K[1], por.K[2]] })} />
          <Slider label="log₁₀ K₂ = K₃ (transverse)" value={Math.log10(por.K[1])} min={-12} max={-5} step={0.1} unit=" m²" onChange={(v) => setPor({ ...por, K: [por.K[0], 10 ** v, 10 ** v] })} />
          <div className="rounded bg-[#121216] p-2 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-300">
            <div className="mb-1 text-zinc-500">D_global = R·diag(1/K)·Rᵀ [m⁻²]</div>
            {[0, 1, 2].map((r) => <div key={r} className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">{[0, 1, 2].map((c) => <span key={c} className="text-right">{Dg[r * 3 + c].toExponential(2)}</span>)}</div>)}
            <div className="mt-1 text-zinc-500">e₁ = [{Rp[0].toFixed(3)}, {Rp[3].toFixed(3)}, {Rp[6].toFixed(3)}] · anisotropy {(por.K[0] / por.K[1]).toExponential(1)}</div>
          </div>
        </Section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

Next is Workspaces 4 & 5: the 3D line-probe/rake tool and chord slicer, with synchronized Cp / Tw / y⁺ / u⁺ plots.
Created src/workspaces/ngvGpu.ts
Created src/workspaces/ngvGpu.ts

import { Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { MeshData } from "../gl/primitives";
import { buildLut } from "../lib/colormap";
import { DOMAIN, FS, GRID } from "../cases/ngv";
const cache = new WeakMap<Renderer, Map<string, WebGLTexture>>();
export function lut(r: Renderer, name: string): WebGLTexture {
  let m = cache.get(r);
  if (!m) cache.set(r, (m = new Map()));
  let t = m.get(name);
  if (!t) m.set(name, (t = r.createLut(buildLut(name))));
  return t;
}
export const DIMS = [GRID.nx, GRID.ny, GRID.nz];
export const BMIN = DOMAIN.min, BMAX = DOMAIN.max;
export const sliceAtZ = (z: number) => ({ o: [BMIN[0], BMIN[1], z], eu: [BMAX[0] - BMIN[0], 0, 0], ev: [0, BMAX[1] - BMIN[1], 0] });
export function endwalls(): MeshData {
  const P: number[] = [], N: number[] = [], I: number[] = [];
  [0, FS.span].forEach((z, k) => {
    const b = P.length / 3;
    for (const [x, y] of [[BMIN[0], BMIN[1]], [BMAX[0], BMIN[1]], [BMAX[0], BMAX[1]], [BMIN[0], BMAX[1]]]) { P.push(x, y, z); N.push(0, 0, k ? -1 : 1); }
    I.push(b, b + 1, b + 2, b, b + 2, b + 3);
  });
  return { pos: new Float32Array(P), nrm: new Float32Array(N), idx: new Uint32Array(I) };
}

Created src/workspaces/ProbePlots.tsx
Created src/workspaces/ProbePlots.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Viewport, Scene, pickKind, ViewCtx, DragSession } from "../gl/Viewport";
import { OrbitCamera } from "../gl/camera";
import { GpuMesh, HANDLE_BASE, Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { LineBatch, sphereMesh } from "../gl/primitives";
import { basisMatrix, dist, lerp3, rayPlane, V3 } from "../lib/math";
import { blProfile, DOMAIN, FS, RunData, sampleTri } from "../cases/ngv";
import { C, FIELD_META, SURF_META, surfacePoint, vaneMesh } from "./shared";
import { BMAX, BMIN, DIMS, endwalls, lut, sliceAtZ } from "./ngvGpu";
import { Badge, Btn, Colorbar, KV, Section, Select, Seg, Slider, Toggle } from "../ui/controls";
import { Plot, Series } from "../plots/Plot";
import { lutCss } from "../lib/colormap";
const PROBE_FIELDS = ["Umag", "Cp", "p", "T", "Mach", "p0loss", "k", "rho"] as const;
const clampDom = (p: V3): V3 => [0, 1, 2].map((k) => Math.max(DOMAIN.min[k], Math.min(DOMAIN.max[k], p[k]))) as V3;
export default function ProbePlots({ active, runA, runB, y1, setY1 }: { active: boolean; runA: RunData; runB: RunData; y1: number; setY1: (v: number) => void }) {
  const c = FS.c, zMid = FS.span / 2;
  const [field, setField] = useState<(typeof PROBE_FIELDS)[number]>("Umag");
  const [pA, setPA] = useState<V3>([1.25 * c, -0.45 * c, zMid]);
  const [pB, setPB] = useState<V3>([1.25 * c, 0.45 * c, zMid]);
  const [surfKey, setSurfKey] = useState<"cp" | "Tw" | "yplus" | "Nu" | "tau">("cp");
  const [chordKey, setChordKey] = useState<"Tw" | "yplus" | "Nu" | "tau">("yplus");
  const [invert, setInvert] = useState(true);
  const [overlay, setOverlay] = useState(true);
  const [station, setStation] = useState(0.6);
  const [zc, setZc] = useState(zMid);
  const [curS, setCurS] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [curXc, setCurXc] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [hoverH, setHoverH] = useState(-1);
  const meta = FIELD_META[field];
  const L = dist(pA, pB);
  const samples = useMemo(() => {
    const n = 220;
    const mk = (run: RunData): [number, number][] => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => { const t = i / (n - 1); return [t * L * 1e3, sampleTri(run.fields[field], lerp3(pA, pB, t))]; });
    return { A: mk(runA), B: mk(runB) };
  }, [runA, runB, field, pA, pB, L]);
  // ---------------- scene
  const cam = useMemo(() => { const k = new OrbitCamera(); k.frame([0.03, 0, zMid], 0.06); k.yaw = 0.55; k.pitch = 0.42; return k; }, []);
  const S = useRef({ runA, field, surfKey, pA, pB, zc, curS, curXc, hoverH, dirtyVol: true, dirtyVane: true });
  Object.assign(S.current, { pA, pB, zc, curS, curXc, hoverH });
  const set = useRef({ setPA, setPB });
  set.current = { setPA, setPB };
  const scene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    let r: Renderer, vol: WebGLTexture, vane: GpuMesh | null = null, walls: GpuMesh, sph: GpuMesh;
    const lb = new LineBatch();
    return {
      init(rr) { r = rr; vol = r.createVolume(DIMS, S.current.runA.fields[S.current.field]); walls = r.createMesh(endwalls()); sph = r.createMesh(sphereMesh()); S.current.dirtyVane = true; },
      draw(ctx: ViewCtx) {
        const s = S.current;
        if (s.dirtyVol && !ctx.picking) { r.updateVolume(vol, DIMS, s.runA.fields[s.field]); s.dirtyVol = false; }
        if (s.dirtyVane && !ctx.picking) { if (vane) r.deleteMesh(vane); vane = r.createMesh(vaneMesh(s.runA, SURF_META[s.surfKey].key)); s.dirtyVane = false; }
        const wpp = (p: V3) => ctx.cam.worldPerPx(dist(p, ctx.eye), ctx.hPx);
        const sm = SURF_META[s.surfKey];
        if (vane) r.drawSurface(vane, { mode: 1, lut: lut(r, sm.cmap), range: sm.range, pickId: 0 });
        const fm = FIELD_META[s.field];
        r.drawSlice({ ...sliceAtZ(zMid - 1e-5), va: vol, dims: DIMS, bmin: BMIN, bmax: BMAX, mode: 0, lut: lut(r, fm.cmap), range: fm.range, iso: 16 });
        // handles
        [s.pA, s.pB].forEach((p, i) => { const sz = wpp(p) * (s.hoverH === i ? 8 : 6); r.drawSurface(sph, { model: basisMatrix(p, [0, 0, 1], sz, sz, sz), color: s.hoverH === i ? [1, 1, 1, 1] : [1, 0.82, 0.2, 1], pickId: HANDLE_BASE + i, depthTest: false }); });
        if (ctx.picking) return;
        lb.clear();
        lb.seg(s.pA, s.pB, [1, 0.82, 0.2, 1]);
        for (let i = 0; i <= 20; i++) { const p = lerp3(s.pA, s.pB, i / 20); lb.seg(p, [p[0], p[1], p[2] + 0.0012], [1, 0.82, 0.2, 0.7]); }
        // chord slicer: plane-wall intersection curve at z = zc
        const { x, y } = s.runA.geo;
        const ring: V3[] = Array.from({ length: x.length }, (_, i) => [x[i] * c, y[i] * c, s.zc]);
        lb.poly(ring, [1, 1, 1, 0.95]);
        lb.box([BMIN[0], BMIN[1], 0], [BMAX[0], BMAX[1], FS.span], [0.5, 0.6, 0.8, 0.35]);
        r.drawLines(lb.data, 2, { xray: 0.35 });
        r.drawSurface(walls, { color: [0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 1], alpha: 0.12, grid: 0.005 });
        const mk = (p: V3, col: number[]) => { const sz = wpp(p) * 5; r.drawSurface(sph, { model: basisMatrix(p, [0, 0, 1], sz, sz, sz), color: col, depthTest: false }); };
        if (s.curS !== null) mk(lerp3(s.pA, s.pB, Math.max(0, Math.min(1, s.curS / (dist(s.pA, s.pB) * 1e3)))), [0, 0.29, 0.99, 1]);
        if (s.curXc !== null) { mk(surfacePoint(s.runA, s.curXc, true, s.zc), [0, 0.29, 0.99, 1]); mk(surfacePoint(s.runA, s.curXc, false, s.zc), [0.94, 0.14, 0.24, 1]); }
      },
      onHover(v) { const k = pickKind(v); S.current.hoverH = k.handle; setHoverH(k.handle); },
      onDragStart(h, _ray, ctx): DragSession | null {
        if (h > 1) return null;
        const f = ctx.cam.axes().f, o = h === 0 ? S.current.pA : S.current.pB;
        return { move(ray) { const p = rayPlane(ray, o, f); if (p) (h === 0 ? set.current.setPA : set.current.setPB)(clampDom(p)); } };
      },
      labels() {
        const s = S.current;
        return [
          { key: "a", pos: s.pA, text: "p_A", color: "#facc15" },
          { key: "b", pos: s.pB, text: `p_B · |AB| ${(dist(s.pA, s.pB) * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm`, color: "#facc15" },
          { key: "z", pos: [c * 1.02, 0.02 * c, s.zc] as V3, text: `chord slice z = ${(s.zc * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm`, color: "#ffffff" },
        ];
      },
      frame: () => ({ c: [0.03, 0, zMid], r: 0.06 }),
      hud: () => "W4 · Line probe & chord slicer",
    };
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.runA = runA; S.current.dirtyVol = true; S.current.dirtyVane = true; }, [runA]);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.field = field; S.current.dirtyVol = true; }, [field]);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.surfKey = surfKey; S.current.dirtyVane = true; }, [surfKey]);
  // ---------------- chordwise series
  const chord = (run: RunData, key: keyof (typeof run.surf.upper)[0], side: "upper" | "lower") => run.surf[side].map((s) => [s.xc, s[key] as number] as [number, number]).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
  const both = (key: any, colA: string, colB: string): Series[] => [
    { name: "A suction", pts: chord(runA, key, "upper"), color: colA, dash: "5 3", width: 1.3 },
    { name: "A pressure", pts: chord(runA, key, "lower"), color: colA, dash: "1.5 2.5", width: 1.3 },
    ...(overlay ? [{ name: "B suction", pts: chord(runB, key, "upper"), color: colB, width: 1.8 }, { name: "B pressure", pts: chord(runB, key, "lower"), color: colB, dash: "6 2 1 2", width: 1.5 }] : []),
  ];
  const st = useMemo(() => { const arr = runB.surf.upper; let best = arr[0]; for (const s of arr) if (Math.abs(s.xc - station) < Math.abs(best.xc - station)) best = s; return best; }, [runB, station]);
  const bl = useMemo(() => blProfile(st, y1), [st, y1]);
  const yps = Array.from({ length: 120 }, (_, i) => Math.pow(10, -1 + (i / 119) * 4.3));
  const cm = SURF_META[chordKey];
  const yplusSeries = both("yplus", "#7c8aa5", C.blue);
  const yMax = Math.max(...yplusSeries.flatMap((s) => s.pts.map((p) => p[1])));
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0">
      <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-[1.05]">
        <Viewport scene={scene} camera={cam} active={active} />
        <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 flex flex-col gap-2">
          <div className="flex gap-1.5">
            <Select value={field} onChange={setField} options={PROBE_FIELDS.map((f) => ({ v: f, label: `Slice + probe: ${FIELD_META[f].label}` }))} />
            <Select value={surfKey} onChange={setSurfKey} options={Object.entries(SURF_META).map(([v, m]) => ({ v: v as any, label: `Wall: ${m.label}` }))} />
          </div>
          <div className="pointer-events-none rounded-md border border-white/10 bg-black/50 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300 backdrop-blur">Drag the yellow <b className="text-white">p_A / p_B</b> handles · hover a plot → 3D cursor</div>
        </div>
        <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-3 flex flex-col gap-1.5">
          <Colorbar css={lutCss(meta.cmap)} min={meta.range[0].toPrecision(3)} max={meta.range[1].toPrecision(3)} label={`slice z=${(zMid * 1e3).toFixed(1)} mm · ${meta.label} [${meta.unit}] · Run A`} />
          <Colorbar css={lutCss(SURF_META[surfKey].cmap)} min={String(SURF_META[surfKey].range[0])} max={String(SURF_META[surfKey].range[1])} label={`wall ${SURF_META[surfKey].label} [${SURF_META[surfKey].unit}]`} />
        </div>
        <div className="absolute right-3 top-3 flex gap-1">
          <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => { setPA([1.25 * c, -0.45 * c, zMid]); setPB([1.25 * c, 0.45 * c, zMid]); }}>Wake rake x/c=1.25</Btn>
          <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => { setPA([-0.4 * c, 0.04 * c, zMid]); setPB([1.9 * c, 0.04 * c, zMid]); }}>Axial line</Btn>
          <Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => { setPA([0.5 * c, 0.3 * c, 0]); setPB([0.5 * c, 0.3 * c, FS.span]); }}>Spanwise</Btn>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto border-l border-[#26262d] bg-[#18181C] p-3">
        <div className="mb-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <Toggle label={<span className="mr-2">Overlay Run B</span>} value={overlay} onChange={setOverlay} />
          <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">A = {runA.design.name} · B = {runB.design.name}</span>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <Plot title={<>Line probe p_A→p_B · {meta.label} [{meta.unit}] · trilinear (CFDFLD03 structured block)</>} height={190}
            x={{ label: "s [mm]", min: 0, max: Math.max(L * 1e3, 1e-3) }}
            y={{ label: `${meta.label} [${meta.unit}]`, min: Math.min(...[...samples.A, ...samples.B].map((p) => p[1]).filter(isFinite)) * 0.98, max: Math.max(...[...samples.A, ...samples.B].map((p) => p[1]).filter(isFinite)) * 1.02 + 1e-9 }}
            series={[{ name: "Run A", pts: samples.A, color: "#7c8aa5", dash: "5 3" }, ...(overlay ? [{ name: "Run B", pts: samples.B, color: C.blue, width: 2 }] : [])]}
            cursorX={curS} onHoverX={setCurS} />
          <Plot title={<>Chordwise pressure coefficient C<sub>p</sub>(x/c)</>} right={<Btn variant="outline" onClick={() => setInvert(!invert)}>{invert ? "−Cₚ up (aero)" : "Cₚ up"}</Btn>} height={210}
            x={{ label: "x/c", min: 0, max: 1 }} y={{ label: "Cₚ", min: -2.2, max: 1.15, invert }}
            series={both("cp", "#7c8aa5", C.blue)} hlines={[{ v: 0, color: "#52525b" }, { v: 1 - 1 / (1 - FS.M * FS.M) * 0, color: "#3f3f46" }]} cursorX={curXc} onHoverX={setCurXc} />
          <Plot title={<>Wall temperature T<sub>w</sub>(x/c) · film-cooled vane</>} height={180}
            x={{ label: "x/c", min: 0, max: 1 }} y={{ label: "T_w [K]", min: 1000, max: 1450 }}
            series={both("Tw", "#a1a1aa", "#fb923c")} hlines={[{ v: 1350, color: C.red, label: "material limit 1350 K" }]} cursorX={curXc} onHoverX={setCurXc} />
          <Plot title={<>y⁺(x/c) · first-cell height y₁ = {(y1 * 1e6).toFixed(2)} µm</>} height={200}
            right={<Select value={chordKey} onChange={setChordKey} options={Object.entries(SURF_META).filter(([k]) => k !== "cp").map(([v, m]) => ({ v: v as any, label: m.label }))} />}
            x={{ label: "x/c", min: 0, max: 1 }}
            y={chordKey === "yplus" ? { label: "y⁺", min: 0.05, max: Math.max(400, yMax * 2), log: true } : { label: `${cm.label} [${cm.unit}]`, min: 0, max: Math.max(...both(cm.key, "", "").flatMap((s) => s.pts.map((p) => p[1]))) * 1.1 }}
            bands={chordKey === "yplus" ? [{ y0: 0.05, y1: 1, color: "#22c55e22", label: "viscous sublayer target y⁺ ≤ 1" }, { y0: 5, y1: 30, color: "", hatch: true, label: "buffer layer — avoid" }, { y0: 30, y1: 300, color: "#004AFD22", label: "log-layer (wall functions)" }] : []}
            hlines={chordKey === "yplus" ? [{ v: 1, color: "#22c55e", label: "y⁺ = 1" }, { v: 30, color: "#4d8bff", label: "y⁺ = 30" }] : []}
            series={chordKey === "yplus" ? yplusSeries : both(cm.key, "#7c8aa5", C.blue)} cursorX={curXc} onHoverX={setCurXc} />
          <Slider label="First-cell height y₁ (mesh)" value={Math.log10(y1)} min={-6.5} max={-4} step={0.01} onChange={(v) => setY1(Math.pow(10, v))} fmt={(v) => `${(Math.pow(10, v) * 1e6).toFixed(2)} µm`} />
          <Plot title={<>Boundary-layer profile u⁺(y⁺) · Run B suction side x/c = {st.xc.toFixed(3)}</>} height={230}
            right={<Badge color={bl.Pi > 0.6 ? C.amber : "#9aa0ad"}>Π = {bl.Pi.toFixed(2)} · δ⁺ = {bl.deltaPlus.toFixed(0)}</Badge>}
            x={{ label: "y⁺", min: 0.1, max: 2e4, log: true }} y={{ label: "u⁺", min: 0, max: 35 }}
            series={[
              { name: "u⁺ = y⁺", pts: yps.filter((v) => v < 15).map((v) => [v, v]), color: "#22c55e", dash: "4 3" },
              { name: "log law (1/κ) ln(E y⁺), κ=0.41, E=9.793", pts: yps.filter((v) => v > 3).map((v) => [v, Math.log(9.793 * v) / 0.41]), color: "#f59e0b", dash: "4 3" },
              { name: "Reichardt + Coles wake", pts: yps.filter((v) => v < bl.deltaPlus * 1.5).map((v) => [v, bl.uplus(v)]), color: "#7c8aa5" },
              { name: "CFD cell centres", pts: bl.cells, color: C.blue, markersOnly: true, r: 2.8 },
            ]}
            bands={[]} vlines={[{ v: bl.cells[0]?.[0] ?? 1, color: C.blue, label: "1st cell" }, { v: bl.deltaPlus, color: "#71717a", label: "δ⁺" }]} />
          <Slider label="Profile station x/c (suction side)" value={station} min={0.05} max={0.97} step={0.01} onChange={setStation} />
          <Slider label="Chord slicer span station z" value={zc * 1e3} min={0} max={FS.span * 1e3} step={0.1} unit=" mm" onChange={(v) => setZc(v / 1e3)} />
          <Section title="Probe definition" defaultOpen={false}>
            <KV k="p_A [mm]" v={pA.map((v) => (v * 1e3).toFixed(2)).join(", ")} />
            <KV k="p_B [mm]" v={pB.map((v) => (v * 1e3).toFixed(2)).join(", ")} />
            <KV k="samples / interpolation" v="220 · trilinear (structured) / barycentric (tets, native)" />
            <Seg value={field} onChange={setField} small options={PROBE_FIELDS.map((f) => ({ v: f, label: FIELD_META[f].label }))} />
          </Section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

Created src/workspaces/Compare.tsx
Created src/workspaces/Compare.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Viewport, Scene, Slot, ViewCtx } from "../gl/Viewport";
import { OrbitCamera } from "../gl/camera";
import { GpuMesh, Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { LineBatch } from "../gl/primitives";
import { V3 } from "../lib/math";
import { CL_REQ, Design, dragPolar, FS, RUN_A, RunData, runDoe } from "../cases/ngv";
import { absPercentile, C, FIELD_META, vaneMesh } from "./shared";
import { BMAX, BMIN, DIMS, endwalls, lut, sliceAtZ } from "./ngvGpu";
import { Badge, Btn, Colorbar, Section, Seg, Select, Slider } from "../ui/controls";
import { Plot } from "../plots/Plot";
import { lutCss } from "../lib/colormap";
type Mode = "dual" | "wipe" | "diff" | "triple";
const FIELDS = ["Cp", "Umag", "p0loss", "T", "Mach", "k"] as const;
function deltaVane(A: RunData, B: RunData) {
  const m = vaneMesh(B, "cp");
  const nB = B.geo.x.length, half = Math.floor(nB / 2);
  const cpA = A.surf.nodeVals("cp"), cpB = B.surf.nodeVals("cp");
  const nA = A.geo.x.length, halfA = Math.floor(nA / 2);
  const d = new Float32Array(nB);
  for (let i = 0; i < nB; i++) {
    const up = i >= half;
    let best = 0, bd = Infinity;
    for (let j = 0; j < nA; j++) if ((j >= halfA) === up) { const e = Math.abs(A.geo.x[j] - B.geo.x[i]); if (e < bd) { bd = e; best = j; } }
    d[i] = cpB[i] - cpA[best];
  }
  const s = m.scalar!;
  for (let k = 0; k < s.length; k++) s[k] = d[k % nB];
  return m;
}
export default function Compare({ active, runA, runB, designB, setDesignB }: { active: boolean; runA: RunData; runB: RunData; designB: Design; setDesignB: (d: Design) => void }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("wipe");
  const [field, setField] = useState<(typeof FIELDS)[number]>("Cp");
  const [wipe, setWipe] = useState(0.5);
  const [db, setDb] = useState<Design>(designB);
  const [zs, setZs] = useState(FS.span / 2);
  useEffect(() => setDb(designB), [designB]);
  useEffect(() => { const t = setTimeout(() => { if (JSON.stringify(db) !== JSON.stringify(designB)) setDesignB(db); }, 220); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, [db]);
  const meta = FIELD_META[field];
  const dmax = useMemo(() => absPercentile(runA.fields[field], runB.fields[field]), [runA, runB, field]);
  const cam = useMemo(() => { const k = new OrbitCamera(); k.frame([0.032, 0.0, FS.span / 2], 0.042); k.yaw = 0.0; k.pitch = 0.0; k.setView(0.18, 0.12); return k; }, []);
  const S = useRef({ runA, runB, field, mode, wipe, dmax, zs, dirty: true });
  Object.assign(S.current, { mode, wipe, dmax, zs });
  const scene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    let r: Renderer, vA: WebGLTexture, vB: WebGLTexture, mA: GpuMesh | null = null, mB: GpuMesh | null = null, mD: GpuMesh | null = null, walls: GpuMesh;
    const lb = new LineBatch();
    return {
      init(rr) { r = rr; const s = S.current; vA = r.createVolume(DIMS, s.runA.fields[s.field]); vB = r.createVolume(DIMS, s.runB.fields[s.field]); walls = r.createMesh(endwalls()); s.dirty = true; },
      slots(W, H): Slot[] {
        const s = S.current, full = { x: 0, y: 0, w: W, h: H };
        if (s.mode === "dual") { const w = Math.floor(W / 2); return [{ key: "A", rect: { x: 0, y: 0, w, h: H }, scissor: { x: 0, y: 0, w: w - 1, h: H } }, { key: "B", rect: { x: w, y: 0, w: W - w, h: H }, scissor: { x: w + 1, y: 0, w: W - w, h: H } }]; }
        if (s.mode === "triple") { const w = Math.floor(W / 3); return ["A", "B", "D"].map((k, i) => ({ key: k, rect: { x: i * w, y: 0, w: i === 2 ? W - 2 * w : w, h: H }, scissor: { x: i * w + (i ? 1 : 0), y: 0, w: (i === 2 ? W - 2 * w : w) - 1, h: H } })); }
        if (s.mode === "diff") return [{ key: "D", rect: full, scissor: full }];
        const x = Math.round(W * s.wipe);
        return [{ key: "A", rect: full, scissor: { x: 0, y: 0, w: x, h: H } }, { key: "B", rect: full, scissor: { x, y: 0, w: W - x, h: H } }];
      },
      draw(ctx: ViewCtx) {
        const s = S.current;
        if (ctx.picking) return;
        if (s.dirty) {
          r.updateVolume(vA, DIMS, s.runA.fields[s.field]); r.updateVolume(vB, DIMS, s.runB.fields[s.field]);
          [mA, mB, mD].forEach((m) => m && r.deleteMesh(m));
          mA = r.createMesh(vaneMesh(s.runA, "cp")); mB = r.createMesh(vaneMesh(s.runB, "cp")); mD = r.createMesh(deltaVane(s.runA, s.runB));
          s.dirty = false;
        }
        const k = ctx.slot.key, fm = FIELD_META[s.field];
        if (k === "D") {
          r.drawSurface(mD!, { mode: 1, lut: lut(r, "coolwarm"), range: [-0.6, 0.6] });
          r.drawSlice({ ...sliceAtZ(s.zs), va: vA, vb: vB, dims: DIMS, bmin: BMIN, bmax: BMAX, mode: 2, lut: lut(r, "coolwarm"), range: [-s.dmax, s.dmax], iso: 12 });
        } else {
          r.drawSurface(k === "A" ? mA! : mB!, { mode: 1, lut: lut(r, "turbo"), range: [-2, 1] });
          r.drawSlice({ ...sliceAtZ(s.zs), va: vA, vb: vB, dims: DIMS, bmin: BMIN, bmax: BMAX, mode: k === "A" ? 0 : 1, lut: lut(r, fm.cmap), range: fm.range, iso: 16 });
        }
        lb.clear();
        const ring = (run: RunData, col: number[]) => lb.poly(Array.from({ length: run.geo.x.length }, (_, i) => [run.geo.x[i] * FS.c, run.geo.y[i] * FS.c, s.zs + 1e-5] as V3), col);
        if (k !== "B") ring(s.runA, [0.75, 0.75, 0.8, k === "D" ? 1 : 0.6]);
        if (k !== "A") ring(s.runB, [0.1, 0.45, 1, 1]);
        lb.box([BMIN[0], BMIN[1], 0], [BMAX[0], BMAX[1], FS.span], [0.5, 0.6, 0.8, 0.3]);
        r.drawLines(lb.data, 1.8);
        r.drawSurface(walls, { color: [0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 1], alpha: 0.1, grid: 0.005 });
      },
      frame: () => ({ c: [0.032, 0, FS.span / 2], r: 0.042 }),
      hud: () => `A/B · shared camera (sync)`,
    };
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { Object.assign(S.current, { runA, runB, field, dirty: true }); }, [runA, runB, field]);
  const doe = useMemo(() => runDoe(), []);
  const polarA = useMemo(() => dragPolar(RUN_A), []);
  const polarB = useMemo(() => dragPolar(designB), [designB]);
  const front = doe.filter((p) => p.pareto).sort((a, b) => a.cd - b.cd);
  const ka = runA.kpi, kb = runB.kpi;
  const rows: { k: React.ReactNode; a: string; b: string; d: string; good: boolean | null }[] = [
    { k: <>C<sub>l</sub></>, a: ka.cl.toFixed(4), b: kb.cl.toFixed(4), d: `${kb.cl >= ka.cl ? "+" : ""}${(kb.cl - ka.cl).toFixed(4)}`, good: kb.cl >= CL_REQ },
    { k: <>C<sub>d</sub></>, a: ka.cd.toFixed(5), b: kb.cd.toFixed(5), d: `${((kb.cd - ka.cd) * 1e4).toFixed(1)} cts`, good: kb.cd < ka.cd },
    { k: "L/D", a: ka.ld.toFixed(1), b: kb.ld.toFixed(1), d: `${((kb.ld / ka.ld - 1) * 100).toFixed(1)} %`, good: kb.ld > ka.ld },
    { k: <>Δp<sub>0</sub> loss ζ</>, a: (ka.zeta * 100).toFixed(3) + " %", b: (kb.zeta * 100).toFixed(3) + " %", d: `${((kb.zeta / ka.zeta - 1) * 100).toFixed(1)} %`, good: kb.zeta < ka.zeta },
    { k: <>T<sub>max</sub> wall</>, a: ka.tmax.toFixed(1) + " K", b: kb.tmax.toFixed(1) + " K", d: `${kb.tmax - ka.tmax >= 0 ? "+" : ""}${(kb.tmax - ka.tmax).toFixed(1)} K`, good: kb.tmax < ka.tmax },
    { k: <>C<sub>p,min</sub></>, a: ka.cpMin.toFixed(3), b: kb.cpMin.toFixed(3), d: (kb.cpMin - ka.cpMin).toFixed(3), good: kb.cpMin > ka.cpMin },
    { k: <>y⁺<sub>max</sub></>, a: ka.yplusMax.toFixed(2), b: kb.yplusMax.toFixed(2), d: (kb.yplusMax - ka.yplusMax).toFixed(2), good: null },
  ];
  const tag = (k: string) => <span className="rounded bg-black/60 px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-white ring-1 ring-white/15">{k}</span>;
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0">
      <div className="relative min-w-0 flex-1">
        <Viewport scene={scene} camera={cam} active={active} />
        {/* slot tags */}
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0">
          {mode === "wipe" && (<><div className="absolute left-3 top-12">{tag("RUN A · " + RUN_A.name.split("·")[1])}</div><div className="absolute right-3 top-12">{tag("RUN B · adjoint")}</div></>)}
          {mode === "dual" && (<><div className="absolute left-3 top-12">{tag("RUN A")}</div><div className="absolute left-[calc(50%+12px)] top-12">{tag("RUN B")}</div><div className="absolute bottom-0 left-1/2 top-0 w-px bg-white/20" /></>)}
          {mode === "triple" && (<><div className="absolute left-3 top-12">{tag("RUN A")}</div><div className="absolute left-[calc(33.3%+12px)] top-12">{tag("RUN B")}</div><div className="absolute left-[calc(66.6%+12px)] top-12">{tag("Δφ = φB − φA")}</div><div className="absolute bottom-0 left-1/3 top-0 w-px bg-white/20" /><div className="absolute bottom-0 left-2/3 top-0 w-px bg-white/20" /></>)}
          {mode === "diff" && <div className="absolute left-3 top-12">{tag("Δφ = φ_B − φ_A  (GPU, per-fragment)")}</div>}
        </div>
        {mode === "wipe" && (
          <div className="absolute bottom-0 top-0 z-10 w-0" style={{ left: `${wipe * 100}%` }}>
            <div className="absolute bottom-0 top-0 -ml-px w-0.5 bg-white/80 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(0,74,253,0.8)]" />
            <div className="absolute top-1/2 -ml-3.5 flex h-7 w-7 cursor-ew-resize items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/70 bg-[#004AFD] text-[11px] text-white shadow-lg"
              onPointerDown={(e) => {

                const up = () => { window.removeEventListener("pointermove", move); window.removeEventListener("pointerup", up); };
                window.addEventListener("pointermove", move); window.addEventListener("pointerup", up);
              }}>⇔</div>
          </div>
