        </Section>
        <Section title="Drag polar C_l(C_d)">
          <Plot height={190} x={{ label: "C_d [counts]", min: Math.min(...polarA.map((p) => p.cd), ...polarB.map((p) => p.cd)) * 0.95, max: Math.max(...polarA.map((p) => p.cd), ...polarB.map((p) => p.cd)) * 1.03 }} y={{ label: "C_l", min: Math.min(...polarA.map((p) => p.cl), ...polarB.map((p) => p.cl)) - 0.05, max: Math.max(...polarA.map((p) => p.cl), ...polarB.map((p) => p.cl)) + 0.05 }}
            series={[{ name: "Run A", pts: polarA.map((p) => [p.cd, p.cl]), color: "#a1a1aa", dash: "5 3", markers: true, r: 2 }, { name: "Run B", pts: polarB.map((p) => [p.cd, p.cl]), color: C.blue, markers: true, r: 2.4, width: 2 }]}
            hlines={[{ v: CL_REQ, color: C.amber, label: "C_l,req" }]} />
          <div className="flex gap-1"><Badge color="#9aa0ad">α = −2° … 10°</Badge><Badge color="#9aa0ad">Karman–Tsien M∞ = {FS.M}</Badge><Badge color="#9aa0ad">Re_c = {(FS.Re / 1e3).toFixed(0)}k</Badge></div>
        </Section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

Now Workspace 7: the SI-checked field calculator with a live AST dimension tree, CFDFLD03 GPU upload and the Rhai console.
Created src/workspaces/Calculator.tsx
Created src/workspaces/Calculator.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Viewport, Scene, ViewCtx } from "../gl/Viewport";
import { OrbitCamera } from "../gl/camera";
import { GpuMesh, Renderer } from "../gl/renderer";
import { LineBatch } from "../gl/primitives";
import { V3 } from "../lib/math";
import { DOMAIN, FS, GRID, MASK, RunData, sampleTri } from "../cases/ngv";
import { compileStmt, DimError, encodeCfdfld03, evaluate, FUNC_DOCS, Grid, Node, Stmt, VARS } from "../calc/expr";
import { describeDim, fmtDim, fmtVec, qname } from "../calc/units";
import { fmtR, runRhai, RhaiHost, RVal } from "../calc/rhai";
import { C, vaneMesh } from "./shared";
import { BMAX, BMIN, DIMS, endwalls, lut, sliceAtZ } from "./ngvGpu";
import { Badge, Btn, Colorbar, Section, Seg } from "../ui/controls";
import { lutCss } from "../lib/colormap";
const DEFAULT = `p_total = p + 0.5 * rho * dot(U, U)
mach = mag(U) / sqrt(1.4 * 287.05 * T)
entropy_gen = mu / T * mag_sq(grad(U)) + kappa / (T*T) * mag_sq(grad(T))
q_crit = 0.5 * (mag_sq(skew(grad(U))) - mag_sq(sym(grad(U))))
T_total = T + dot(U, U) / (2 * c_p)
bad = p + 0.5 * dot(U, U)`;
const PRESETS: [string, string][] = [
  ["vorticity", "vorticity = mag(curl(U))"],
  ["nu_eff", "nu_eff = mu / rho + nut"],
  ["re_cell", "re_cell = mag(U) * 0.00038[m] / (mu / rho)"],
  ["✗ exp(T)", "bad_exp = exp(T)"],
  ["✗ U + p", "bad_rank = U + p"],
  ["✗ cross(U,p)", "bad_cross = cross(U, p)"],
  ["✗ mach w/o R", "mach = mag(U) / sqrt(1.4 * T)"],
];
interface Derived { name: string; data: Float32Array; dim: number[]; kind: string; min: number; max: number; mean: number; buf: ArrayBuffer; evalMs: number; upMs: number }
interface Line { text: string; kind: "in" | "out" | "err" | "info" }
function AstTree({ n, src, depth = 0 }: { n: Node; src: string; depth?: number }) {
  const lbl = n.t === "num" ? (n.recognized ? `${n.v} ⇒ ${n.recognized}` : n.unit ? `${n.v}[${n.unit}]` : String(n.v)) : n.t === "var" ? n.name : n.t === "bin" ? n.op : n.t === "neg" ? "neg" : n.t === "call" ? n.name + "()" : "." + "xyz"[n.c];
  const kids = n.t === "bin" ? [n.a, n.b] : n.t === "neg" || n.t === "comp" ? [n.a] : n.t === "call" ? n.args : [];
  const kc = n.ty?.kind === "v" ? "#38bdf8" : n.ty?.kind === "t" ? "#e879f9" : "#a3e635";
  return (
    <div style={{ paddingLeft: depth ? 12 : 0 }} className={depth ? "border-l border-[#2a2a33]" : ""}>
      <div className="flex items-baseline gap-1.5 py-[1px] font-mono text-[10.5px]">
        <span className={n.t === "var" ? "text-[#9ec1ff]" : n.t === "num" ? "text-[#fbbf24]" : "text-zinc-200"}>{lbl}</span>
        {n.ty && <span className="rounded px-1 text-[9px]" style={{ color: kc, background: kc + "1f" }}>{n.ty.kind === "s" ? "scalar" : n.ty.kind === "v" ? "vector" : "tensor"}</span>}
        {n.ty && <span className="text-zinc-500">{fmtVec(n.ty.dim)}</span>}
        {n.ty && <span className="text-zinc-300">{fmtDim(n.ty.dim)}</span>}
        {n.ty && qname(n.ty.dim) && <span className="text-[9.5px] text-zinc-500">· {qname(n.ty.dim)}</span>}
      </div>
      {kids.map((k, i) => <AstTree key={i} n={k} src={src} depth={depth + 1} />)}
    </div>
  );
}
export default function Calculator({ active, runA, runB }: { active: boolean; runA: RunData; runB: RunData }) {
  const [script, setScript] = useState(DEFAULT);
  const [which, setWhich] = useState<"A" | "B">("A");
  const [sel, setSel] = useState(0);
  const [derived, setDerived] = useState<Record<string, Derived>>({});
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<string>("p_total");
  const [cmap, setCmap] = useState("viridis");
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<Line[]>([{ text: "Rhai 1.x post-processing console (subset preview) — type help()", kind: "info" }]);
  const [inp, setInp] = useState('derive("q_dyn", "0.5 * rho * mag_sq(U)"); stats("q_dyn")');
  const [hist, setHist] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const run = which === "A" ? runA : runB;
  const scope = useRef(new Map<string, RVal>());
  const grid: Grid = useMemo(() => {
    const f = run.fields as unknown as Record<string, Float32Array>;
    const mask = new Uint8Array(f.Cp.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < mask.length; i++) mask[i] = f.Cp[i] > 1e29 ? 1 : 0;
    const h = [0, 1, 2].map((k) => (DOMAIN.max[k] - DOMAIN.min[k]) / ([GRID.nx, GRID.ny, GRID.nz][k] - 1));
    return { nx: GRID.nx, ny: GRID.ny, nz: GRID.nz, h, fields: f, mask };
  }, [run]);
  const compiled = useMemo(() => script.split("\n").map((text, i) => {
    if (!text.trim() || text.trim().startsWith("//")) return { i, text, stmt: null as Stmt | null, err: null as DimError | null };
    try { return { i, text, stmt: compileStmt(text), err: null }; } catch (e) { return { i, text, stmt: null, err: e instanceof DimError ? e : new DimError(String(e), 0, text.length) }; }
  }), [script]);
  const derive = (st: Stmt): Derived => {
    const t0 = performance.now();
    const v = evaluate(st.ast, grid);
    const N = GRID.nx * GRID.ny * GRID.nz;
    let data: Float32Array, nComp = 1;
    if (v.k === "c") data = new Float32Array(N).fill(v.v);
    else if (v.k === "s") data = new Float32Array(v.d);
    else { nComp = v.k === "v" ? 3 : 9; data = new Float32Array(N); for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { let s = 0; for (let c = 0; c < nComp; c++) s += v.d[nComp * i + c] ** 2; data[i] = Math.sqrt(s); } }
    let mn = Infinity, mx = -Infinity, sum = 0, cnt = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) { if (grid.mask[i] || !isFinite(data[i])) { data[i] = MASK; continue; } mn = Math.min(mn, data[i]); mx = Math.max(mx, data[i]); sum += data[i]; cnt++; }
    const evalMs = performance.now() - t0;
    const buf = encodeCfdfld03(st.target, st.ty.dim, data, 1, DIMS, mn, mx);
    return { name: st.target, data, dim: st.ty.dim, kind: st.ty.kind, min: mn, max: mx, mean: sum / Math.max(cnt, 1), buf, evalMs, upMs: 0 };
  };
  // evaluate valid statements (debounced)
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      const out: Record<string, Derived> = {};
      for (const c of compiled) if (c.stmt) { try { out[c.stmt.target] = derive(c.stmt); } catch { /* runtime */ } }
      setDerived((d) => ({ ...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(d).filter(([, v]) => !compiled.some((c) => c.stmt?.target === v.name))), ...out }));
    }, 300);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [compiled, grid]);
  useEffect(() => { const c = compiled[sel]; if (c?.stmt) setShown(c.stmt.target); }, [sel, compiled]);
  const cur = derived[shown];
  const robust = useMemo(() => {
    if (!cur) return [0, 1] as [number, number];
    const v: number[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < cur.data.length; i += 7) if (cur.data[i] < 1e29) v.push(cur.data[i]);
    v.sort((a, b) => a - b);
    const lo = v[Math.floor(v.length * 0.02)] ?? 0, hi = v[Math.floor(v.length * 0.98)] ?? 1;
    if (lo < 0 && hi > 0) { const m = Math.max(-lo, hi); return [-m, m] as [number, number]; }
    return [lo, hi === lo ? lo + 1 : hi] as [number, number];
  }, [cur]);
  const diverging = robust[0] < 0 && robust[1] > 0;
  const cm = diverging ? "coolwarm" : cmap;
  // ---------------- scene
  const cam = useMemo(() => { const k = new OrbitCamera(); k.frame([0.032, 0, FS.span / 2], 0.045); k.setView(0.25, 0.5); return k; }, []);
  const S = useRef({ data: null as Float32Array | null, dirty: false, range: [0, 1] as [number, number], cm: "viridis", run, upMs: 0, vaneDirty: true });
  const scene: Scene = useMemo(() => {
    let r: Renderer, vol: WebGLTexture, vane: GpuMesh | null = null, walls: GpuMesh;
    const lb = new LineBatch();
    return {
      init(rr) { r = rr; vol = r.createVolume(DIMS, new Float32Array(DIMS[0] * DIMS[1] * DIMS[2])); walls = r.createMesh(endwalls()); S.current.dirty = true; S.current.vaneDirty = true; },
      draw(ctx: ViewCtx) {
        if (ctx.picking) return;
        const s = S.current;
        if (s.dirty && s.data) { const t0 = performance.now(); r.updateVolume(vol, DIMS, s.data); r.gl.finish(); s.upMs = performance.now() - t0; s.dirty = false; }
        if (s.vaneDirty) { if (vane) r.deleteMesh(vane); vane = r.createMesh(vaneMesh(s.run, "Tw")); s.vaneDirty = false; }
        if (vane) r.drawSurface(vane, { color: [0.6, 0.62, 0.66, 1] });
        if (s.data) for (const z of [FS.span * 0.5, FS.span * 0.06]) r.drawSlice({ ...sliceAtZ(z), va: vol, dims: DIMS, bmin: BMIN, bmax: BMAX, mode: 0, lut: lut(r, s.cm), range: s.range, iso: 14, alpha: z < FS.span * 0.1 ? 0.85 : 1 });
        lb.clear();
        lb.box([BMIN[0], BMIN[1], 0], [BMAX[0], BMAX[1], FS.span], [0.5, 0.6, 0.8, 0.3]);
        lb.poly(Array.from({ length: s.run.geo.x.length }, (_, i) => [s.run.geo.x[i] * FS.c, s.run.geo.y[i] * FS.c, FS.span * 0.5 + 1e-5] as V3), [1, 1, 1, 0.7]);
        r.drawLines(lb.data, 1.5);
        r.drawSurface(walls, { color: [0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 1], alpha: 0.08, grid: 0.005 });
      },
      frame: () => ({ c: [0.032, 0, FS.span / 2], r: 0.045 }),
      hud: () => `GPU upload ${S.current.upMs.toFixed(2)} ms`,
    };
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { Object.assign(S.current, { data: cur?.data ?? null, dirty: true, range: robust, cm }); }, [cur, robust, cm]);
  useEffect(() => { S.current.run = run; S.current.vaneDirty = true; }, [run]);
  // ---------------- Rhai console
  const print = (text: string, kind: Line["kind"] = "out") => setLines((l) => [...l.slice(-200), { text, kind }]);
  const host: RhaiHost = {
    help: () => { print("fields() units(name) check(expr) derive(name, expr) stats(name) probe(name, [x,y,z], [x,y,z], n) mean(a) max(a) min(a) len(a) show(name) colormap(name) run(\"A\"|\"B\") print(x)", "info"); return null; },
    print: (...a) => { print(a.map(fmtR).join(" ")); return null; },
    fields: () => [...Object.keys(VARS).filter((k) => !VARS[k].value), ...Object.keys(derived)],
    units: (n) => { const v = VARS[String(n)] ?? (derived[String(n)] ? { dim: derived[String(n)].dim } : null); return v ? describeDim(v.dim) : `unknown '${n}'`; },
    check: (e) => { try { const st = compileStmt(String(e)); return `OK · ${st.ty.kind === "s" ? "scalar" : st.ty.kind === "v" ? "vector" : "tensor"} · ${describeDim(st.ty.dim)}`; } catch (x) { return "✕ " + (x as Error).message; } },
    derive: (name, e) => { const st = compileStmt(`${name} = ${e}`); const d = derive(st); setDerived((m) => ({ ...m, [d.name]: d })); setShown(d.name); return `uploaded '${d.name}' → CFDFLD03 (${(d.buf.byteLength / 1024).toFixed(0)} KiB) · ${describeDim(d.dim)}`; },
    stats: (n) => { const d = derived[String(n)]; if (!d) throw new Error(`no derived field '${n}' (derive it first)`); return [d.min, d.max, d.mean]; },
    probe: (n, a, b, cnt) => { const f = derived[String(n)]?.data ?? (run.fields as any)[String(n)]; if (!f) throw new Error(`unknown field '${n}'`); const A = a as number[], B = b as number[], k = Number(cnt) || 16; return Array.from({ length: k }, (_, i) => { const t = i / Math.max(1, k - 1); return sampleTri(f, A.map((v, j) => v + (B[j] - v) * t)); }); },
    mean: (a) => (a as number[]).filter(isFinite).reduce((s, v) => s + v, 0) / (a as number[]).filter(isFinite).length,
    max: (a) => Math.max(...(a as number[]).filter(isFinite)),
    min: (a) => Math.min(...(a as number[]).filter(isFinite)),
    len: (a) => (Array.isArray(a) ? a.length : String(a).length),
    show: (n) => { setShown(String(n)); return null; },
    colormap: (n) => { setCmap(String(n)); return null; },
    run: (w) => { setWhich(String(w) === "B" ? "B" : "A"); return null; },
  };
  const exec = () => {
    if (!inp.trim()) return;
    print("» " + inp, "in");
    setHist((h) => [...h, inp]);
    try { const v = runRhai(inp, host, scope.current); if (v !== null) print(fmtR(v)); } catch (e) { print("✕ " + (e as Error).message, "err"); }
    setInp("");
  };
  const consoleEnd = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => consoleEnd.current?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" }), [lines]);
  const selC = compiled[sel];
  const hex = cur ? Array.from(new Uint8Array(cur.buf, 0, 64)) : [];
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0">
      <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
        <div className="relative min-h-0 flex-1">
          <Viewport scene={scene} camera={cam} active={active} />
          <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 flex gap-2">
            <Seg value={which} onChange={setWhich} options={[{ v: "A", label: "Run A" }, { v: "B", label: "Run B" }]} />
            <Seg value={shown} onChange={setShown} small options={Object.keys(derived).slice(0, 7).map((k) => ({ v: k, label: k }))} />
          </div>
          {cur && (
            <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-3">
              <Colorbar css={lutCss(cm)} min={robust[0].toPrecision(3)} max={robust[1].toPrecision(3)} center={diverging ? "0" : undefined} label={`${cur.name} [${fmtDim(cur.dim)}] · ${qname(cur.dim) ?? "derived"} · CFDFLD03 → R32F 3D texture`} />
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="flex h-[230px] flex-col border-t border-[#26262d] bg-[#0f0f13]">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-[#24242b] px-3 py-1 text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500"><span className="font-semibold text-zinc-300">Rhai console</span><span>engine: rhai (native) · subset interpreter (preview)</span><span className="ml-auto">↑ history · Enter run</span></div>
          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-1.5 font-mono text-[11px] leading-[1.55]">
            {lines.map((l, i) => <div key={i} className={l.kind === "in" ? "text-[#9ec1ff]" : l.kind === "err" ? "text-[#ff6b7c]" : l.kind === "info" ? "text-zinc-500" : "text-zinc-200"}>{l.text}</div>)}
            <div ref={consoleEnd} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-[#24242b] px-3 py-1.5">
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-[#004AFD]">rhai»</span>
            <input value={inp} onChange={(e) => setInp(e.target.value)} spellCheck={false}
              onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === "Enter") exec(); if (e.key === "ArrowUp" && hist.length) setInp(hist[hist.length - 1]); }}
              className="flex-1 bg-transparent font-mono text-[11.5px] text-zinc-100 outline-none" placeholder='for f in ["p_total", "mach"] { print(f, stats(f)) }' />
            <Btn variant="primary" onClick={exec}>Run</Btn>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <aside className="w-[460px] shrink-0 overflow-y-auto border-l border-[#26262d] bg-[#18181C]">
        <Section title="SI-checked field expressions" accent={C.blue} right={<Badge color={compiled.some((c) => c.err) ? C.red : C.green}>{compiled.filter((c) => c.err).length} rejected</Badge>}>
          <textarea value={script} onChange={(e) => setScript(e.target.value)} rows={7} spellCheck={false}
            onSelect={(e) => { const pos = (e.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).selectionStart; setSel(script.slice(0, pos).split("\n").length - 1); }}
            className="w-full resize-y rounded border border-[#2c2c34] bg-[#0e0e12] p-2 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-[1.6] text-[#d6e4ff] outline-none focus:border-[#004AFD]" />
          <div className="space-y-1">
            {compiled.map((c) => c.stmt || c.err ? (
              <button key={c.i} onClick={() => setSel(c.i)} className={"block w-full rounded px-2 py-1 text-left " + (sel === c.i ? "bg-[#23232b]" : "hover:bg-[#1d1d24]")}>
                {c.err ? (
                  <div className="font-mono text-[10.5px]">
                    <div className="text-zinc-400">{c.text.slice(0, c.err.s)}<span className="rounded-sm bg-[#EF233C]/30 text-[#ffd0d5] underline decoration-[#EF233C] decoration-wavy">{c.text.slice(c.err.s, c.err.e) || " "}</span>{c.text.slice(c.err.e)}</div>
                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-[#ff8a96]">✕ {c.err.message}</div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[10.5px]">
                    <span className="text-[#22c55e]">✓</span><span className="text-zinc-200">{c.stmt!.target}</span>
                    <span className="text-zinc-500">{c.stmt!.ty.kind === "s" ? "scalar" : c.stmt!.ty.kind === "v" ? "vector" : "tensor"}</span>
                    <span className="ml-auto text-zinc-300">{describeDim(c.stmt!.ty.dim)}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                {c.stmt?.diags.map((d, k) => <div key={k} className="mt-0.5 text-[10px] text-[#7dd3fc]">ℹ {d.msg}</div>)}
              </button>
            ) : null)}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
            {PRESETS.map(([l, s]) => <Btn key={l} variant={l.startsWith("✗") ? "danger" : "outline"} onClick={() => setScript((x) => x + "\n" + s)}>{l}</Btn>)}
          </div>
        </Section>
        <Section title="AST · dimension propagation [kg, m, s, K, mol, A, cd]">
          {selC?.stmt ? <div className="max-h-72 overflow-auto rounded bg-[#121216] p-2"><div className="mb-1 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-500">{selC.stmt.target} =</div><AstTree n={selC.stmt.ast} src={selC.stmt.src} /></div> : <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">{selC?.err ? "Rejected at parse time — no AST is executed or uploaded." : "Select a line in the editor."}</div>}
        </Section>
        <Section title="CFDFLD03 upload">
          {cur ? (
            <>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-4 font-mono text-[10.5px] text-zinc-300">
                <span className="text-zinc-500">name</span><span>{cur.name}</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-500">n_cells × comp</span><span>{(cur.data.length).toLocaleString()} × 1 ({cur.kind === "s" ? "scalar" : "|" + cur.kind + "|"})</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-500">dims (i,j,k)</span><span>{DIMS.join(" × ")}</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-500">SI dim (i8 ×2)</span><span>{fmtVec(cur.dim)}</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-500">min / max / mean</span><span>{cur.min.toPrecision(4)} / {cur.max.toPrecision(4)} / {cur.mean.toPrecision(4)}</span>
                <span className="text-zinc-500">payload</span><span>{(cur.buf.byteLength / 1024).toFixed(1)} KiB · eval {cur.evalMs.toFixed(1)} ms</span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 rounded bg-[#0e0e12] p-2 font-mono text-[10px] leading-[1.5] text-zinc-400">
                {[0, 16, 32, 48].map((o) => (
                  <div key={o}><span className="text-zinc-600">{o.toString(16).padStart(4, "0")} </span>{hex.slice(o, o + 16).map((b) => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join(" ")} <span className="text-[#9ec1ff]">{hex.slice(o, o + 16).map((b) => (b >= 32 && b < 127 ? String.fromCharCode(b) : "·")).join("")}</span></div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </>
          ) : <div className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">No derived field yet.</div>}
        </Section>
        <Section title="Reference" defaultOpen={false}>
          <div className="font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-relaxed text-zinc-400">
            {Object.entries(VARS).map(([k, v]) => <div key={k}><span className="text-[#9ec1ff]">{k}</span> {v.kind === "v" ? "vec" : "scl"} [{fmtDim(v.dim)}] <span className="text-zinc-600">{v.desc}{v.value !== undefined ? ` = ${v.value}` : ""}</span></div>)}
            <div className="mt-2 text-zinc-300">{FUNC_DOCS.join(" · ")}</div>
            <div className="mt-2">Literals are dimensionless unless annotated: <span className="text-[#fbbf24]">287.05[J/(kg*K)]</span>. Well-known constants (R_air, R_u, g, σ, p_atm) are auto-recognised with an ℹ note.</div>
          </div>
        </Section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

All seven workspaces are written. Now the native Rust reference crate, starting with batch 1: Cargo manifest, crate root, rule DSL, synthesizer, wgpu pick target and the WGSL shaders.

//! # CFD Cockpit — Stage 2
//!
//! Builds on Stage 1 (Reverse-Z wgpu viewport, bytemuck CFDGEO01/CFDMSH02/CFDFLD03 readers,
//! Oklab LUTs, Surface-LIC, async NDJSON IPC) and adds:
//!
//! * **W1/W3** [`rules`] — click-to-author *spatial* boundary rules synthesised from the picked
//!   face group's normal cone / centroid / bbox / cylindrical radius / STEP tag, with live
//!   overlap detection, plus [`gizmo`] manipulators (farfield box, MRF zone, DPM injector,
//!   FWH microphones, anisotropic porous axes).
//! * **W4/W5** [`probe`] + [`plots`] — 3D line probe / chord slicer with trilinear (structured)
//!   and barycentric (tet) sampling feeding egui_plot engineering charts.
//! * **W6** [`compare`] — synchronised dual / wipe-split viewports and a GPU Δφ = φB − φA pass.
//! * **W7** [`calc`] — SI-dimension-checked expression language + Rhai console, results written
//!   straight into a new CFDFLD03 GPU buffer.
//!
//! Frame orchestration (one `egui::Context`, one wgpu device; viewports are egui-wgpu callbacks):
//!
//! ```text
//!  pointer ─▶ PickTarget (R32Uint face_group_id, 1px scissor) ─▶ map_async ─▶ face id
//!        └──▶ PatchDescriptor::analyze ─▶ Synthesizer::propose ─▶ RuleBook::evaluate
//!                                                            └─▶ FaceStyle SSBO (blue / red)
//! ```
pub mod rules { pub mod dsl; pub mod pick; pub mod synth; }
pub mod gizmo { pub mod actors; pub mod domain; }
pub mod probe { pub mod sampler; }
pub mod plots { pub mod engineering; }
pub mod compare { pub mod ab; }
pub mod calc { pub mod eval; pub mod expr; pub mod rhai_console; pub mod units; }
use egui::Color32;
pub const OBSIDIAN: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(0x11, 0x12, 0x17);
pub const CARBON: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(0x18, 0x18, 0x1C);
pub const ELECTRIC_BLUE: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(0x00, 0x4A, 0xFD);
pub const WARN_RED: Color32 = Color32::from_rgb(0xEF, 0x23, 0x3C);
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Workspace { BoundaryRules = 1, DomainActors = 3, ProbePlots = 4, Compare = 6, Calculator = 7 }
/// Everything Stage 2 owns. Stage-1 handles (device, queue, mesh/field stores) are borrowed per frame.
pub struct Stage2 {
    pub workspace: Workspace,
    pub picker: rules::pick::PickTarget,
    pub rulebook: rules::synth::RuleBook,
    pub draft: rules::synth::Draft,
    pub farfield: gizmo::domain::FarfieldBox,
    pub mrf: gizmo::domain::MrfZone,
    pub actors: gizmo::actors::Actors,
    pub probe: probe::sampler::LineProbe,
    pub ab: compare::ab::AbCompare,
    pub console: calc::rhai_console::Console,
}
/// Borrowed Stage-1 context for one frame.
pub struct FrameCtx<'a> {
    pub device: &'a wgpu::Device,
    pub queue: &'a wgpu::Queue,
    pub patches: &'a [rules::synth::PatchSamples],
    pub fields: &'a probe::sampler::StructuredBlock,
    pub pointer_px: Option<(u32, u32)>,
    pub clicked: bool,
}
impl Stage2 {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, size: (u32, u32), cad_len: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            workspace: Workspace::BoundaryRules,
            picker: rules::pick::PickTarget::new(device, size),
            rulebook: rules::synth::RuleBook::default(),
            draft: rules::synth::Draft::default(),
            farfield: gizmo::domain::FarfieldBox::new(cad_len),
            mrf: gizmo::domain::MrfZone::default(),
            actors: gizmo::actors::Actors::default(),
            probe: probe::sampler::LineProbe::default(),
            ab: compare::ab::AbCompare::default(),
            console: calc::rhai_console::Console::new(),
        }
    }
    /// Called once per frame after the Stage-1 viewport has rendered its pick pass.
    pub fn update(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, f: &FrameCtx) {
        if self.workspace == Workspace::BoundaryRules {
            if let (Some(px), true) = (f.pointer_px, f.clicked) {
                self.picker.request(px);
            }
            // async readback completes a frame later (no pipeline stall)
            if let Some(face) = self.picker.poll(f.device) {
                if let Some(p) = f.patches.iter().find(|p| p.id == face) {
                    let desc = rules::synth::PatchDescriptor::analyze(p, f.patches);
                    let scope = rules::synth::Scope::from_patches(f.patches);
                    self.draft.candidates = rules::synth::Synthesizer::propose(&desc, &scope, f.patches);
                    if let Some(best) = self.draft.candidates.iter().find(|c| c.unique) {
                        self.draft.set_text(best.rule.clone());
                    }
                }
            }
            let styles = self.rulebook.face_styles(&self.draft, f.patches);
            self.picker.upload_styles(f.queue, &styles);
            self.draft.ui(ui, &mut self.rulebook, f.patches);
        }
        if self.workspace == Workspace::DomainActors {
            self.farfield.ui(ui);
            self.mrf.ui(ui);
            self.actors.ui(ui);
        }
        if self.workspace == Workspace::ProbePlots {
            let samples = self.probe.sample(f.fields);
            plots::engineering::line_probe_plot(ui, &samples, self.probe.field_label());
        }
        if self.workspace == Workspace::Calculator {
            self.console.ui(ui, f.fields, f.device, f.queue);
        }
    }
}

Created rust/cfd_cockpit_stage2/src/rules/dsl.rs
Created rust/cfd_cockpit_stage2/src/rules/dsl.rs

//! Spatial boundary-rule DSL: `normal.x < -0.995 and abs(x - bbox.x_min) < 1e-4`,
//! `cyl_radius_yz(0, 0) in [0.018, 0.022]`, `step_tag ~ "ROTOR_BLADE_*"`.
//! Rules are compiled once into an AST and evaluated per sample vertex (fluid-outward normals).
use glam::Vec3;
use thiserror::Error;
#[derive(Debug, Error)]
#[error("{msg} (at byte {pos})")]
pub struct RuleError { pub msg: String, pub pos: usize }
fn err<T>(msg: impl Into<String>, pos: usize) -> Result<T, RuleError> { Err(RuleError { msg: msg.into(), pos }) }
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Aabb { pub min: Vec3, pub max: Vec3 }
/// One evaluation point of a face group.
pub struct Sample<'a> { pub p: Vec3, pub n: Vec3, pub tag: &'a str, pub area: f32, pub bbox: &'a Aabb, pub cad: &'a Aabb }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Val { Num(f32), Bool(bool), Str(String), Vec(Vec3) }
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum Ast {
    Num(f32), Str(String), Var(String), Box { which: BoxRef, axis: usize, max: bool },
    Member(Box<Ast>, usize), Vec3(Box<[Ast; 3]>), Neg(Box<Ast>), Not(Box<Ast>),
    Bin(Op, Box<Ast>, Box<Ast>), In(Box<Ast>, Box<Ast>, Box<Ast>), Glob(Box<Ast>, String), Call(String, Vec<Ast>),
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)] pub enum BoxRef { Scope, Cad }
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Op { Or, And, Lt, Le, Gt, Ge, Eq, Ne, Add, Sub, Mul, Div }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
enum Tok { Num(f32), Id(String), Str(String), Op(&'static str) }
fn lex(s: &str) -> Result<Vec<(Tok, usize)>, RuleError> {
    const OPS: [&str; 18] = ["<=", ">=", "==", "!=", "&&", "||", "<", ">", "+", "-", "*", "/", "(", ")", "[", "]", ",", "."];
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    let (mut i, mut out) = (0, Vec::new());
    while i < b.len() {
        let c = b[i] as char;
        if c.is_whitespace() { i += 1; continue; }
        if c.is_ascii_digit() || (c == '.' && b.get(i + 1).map_or(false, |d| d.is_ascii_digit())) {
            let st = i;
            while i < b.len() && (b[i].is_ascii_digit() || b[i] == b'.' || b[i] == b'e' || b[i] == b'E'
                || ((b[i] == b'-' || b[i] == b'+') && (b[i - 1] == b'e' || b[i - 1] == b'E'))) { i += 1; }
            out.push((Tok::Num(s[st..i].parse().map_err(|_| RuleError { msg: "bad number".into(), pos: st })?), st));
        } else if c.is_ascii_alphabetic() || c == '_' {
            let st = i;
            while i < b.len() && (b[i].is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b[i] == b'_') { i += 1; }
            out.push((Tok::Id(s[st..i].to_string()), st));
        } else if c == '"' {
            let end = s[i + 1..].find('"').ok_or(RuleError { msg: "unterminated string".into(), pos: i })? + i + 1;
            out.push((Tok::Str(s[i + 1..end].to_string()), i));
            i = end + 1;
        } else if c == '~' { out.push((Tok::Op("~"), i)); i += 1; }
        else if let Some(op) = OPS.iter().find(|o| s[i..].starts_with(**o)) { out.push((Tok::Op(op), i)); i += op.len(); }
        else { return err(format!("unexpected character '{c}'"), i); }
    }
    Ok(out)
}
pub struct Rule { pub src: String, ast: Ast }
impl Rule {
    pub fn compile(src: &str) -> Result<Self, RuleError> {
        let toks = lex(src)?;
        let mut p = Parser { t: &toks, i: 0, len: src.len() };
        let ast = p.or()?;
        if p.i < toks.len() { return err("unexpected trailing token", toks[p.i].1); }
        Ok(Self { src: src.to_string(), ast })
    }
    /// A face group matches when ≥ 99.5 % of its samples satisfy the predicate.
    pub fn matches<'a>(&self, samples: impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = Sample<'a>>) -> Result<bool, RuleError> {
        let n = samples.len();
        let allowed = n / 200;
        let mut fail = 0;
        for s in samples {
            match eval(&self.ast, &s)? { Val::Bool(true) => {}, Val::Bool(false) => { fail += 1; if fail > allowed { return Ok(false) } }
                _ => return err("rule must evaluate to a boolean", 0) }
        }
        Ok(true)
    }
}
struct Parser<'t> { t: &'t [(Tok, usize)], i: usize, len: usize }
impl Parser<'_> {
    fn pos(&self) -> usize { self.t.get(self.i).map_or(self.len, |t| t.1) }
    fn kw(&mut self, k: &str) -> bool { if matches!(self.t.get(self.i), Some((Tok::Id(s), _)) if s == k) { self.i += 1; true } else { false } }
    fn op(&mut self, o: &str) -> bool { if matches!(self.t.get(self.i), Some((Tok::Op(s), _)) if *s == o) { self.i += 1; true } else { false } }
    fn expect(&mut self, o: &str) -> Result<(), RuleError> { if self.op(o) { Ok(()) } else { err(format!("expected '{o}'"), self.pos()) } }
    fn or(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let mut a = self.and()?;
        while self.kw("or") || self.op("||") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::Or, a.into(), self.and()?.into()); }
        Ok(a)
    }
    fn and(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let mut a = self.not()?;
        while self.kw("and") || self.op("&&") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::And, a.into(), self.not()?.into()); }
        Ok(a)
    }
    fn not(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> { if self.kw("not") { Ok(Ast::Not(self.not()?.into())) } else { self.cmp() } }
    fn cmp(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let a = self.sum()?;
        for (o, op) in [("<=", Op::Le), (">=", Op::Ge), ("==", Op::Eq), ("!=", Op::Ne), ("<", Op::Lt), (">", Op::Gt)] {
            if self.op(o) { return Ok(Ast::Bin(op, a.into(), self.sum()?.into())); }
        }
        if self.kw("in") { self.expect("[")?; let lo = self.sum()?; self.expect(",")?; let hi = self.sum()?; self.expect("]")?; return Ok(Ast::In(a.into(), lo.into(), hi.into())); }
        if self.op("~") {
            return match self.t.get(self.i) { Some((Tok::Str(s), _)) => { self.i += 1; Ok(Ast::Glob(a.into(), s.clone())) }
                _ => err("'~' expects a quoted glob pattern", self.pos()) };
        }
        Ok(a)
    }
    fn sum(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let mut a = self.prod()?;
        loop { if self.op("+") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::Add, a.into(), self.prod()?.into()) } else if self.op("-") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::Sub, a.into(), self.prod()?.into()) } else { return Ok(a) } }
    }
    fn prod(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let mut a = self.unary()?;
        loop { if self.op("*") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::Mul, a.into(), self.unary()?.into()) } else if self.op("/") { a = Ast::Bin(Op::Div, a.into(), self.unary()?.into()) } else { return Ok(a) } }
    }
    fn unary(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> { if self.op("-") { Ok(Ast::Neg(self.unary()?.into())) } else { self.postfix() } }
    fn postfix(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let mut a = self.primary()?;
        while self.op(".") {
            let pos = self.pos();
            let Some((Tok::Id(f), _)) = self.t.get(self.i).cloned() else { return err("expected member", pos) };
            self.i += 1;
            a = match (&a, f.as_str()) {
                (Ast::Var(b), m) if b == "bbox" || b == "cad" => {
                    let axis = "xyz".find(&m[..1]).ok_or(RuleError { msg: format!("unknown bbox field {m}"), pos })?;
                    Ast::Box { which: if b == "cad" { BoxRef::Cad } else { BoxRef::Scope }, axis, max: m.ends_with("max") }
                }
                (_, "x") => Ast::Member(a.into(), 0), (_, "y") => Ast::Member(a.into(), 1), (_, "z") => Ast::Member(a.into(), 2),
                _ => return err(format!("unknown member .{f}"), pos),
            };
        }
        Ok(a)
    }
    fn primary(&mut self) -> Result<Ast, RuleError> {
        let pos = self.pos();
        let Some((t, _)) = self.t.get(self.i).cloned() else { return err("unexpected end of rule", pos) };
        self.i += 1;
        match t {
            Tok::Num(v) => Ok(Ast::Num(v)),
            Tok::Str(s) => Ok(Ast::Str(s)),
            Tok::Op("(") => {
                let a = self.or()?;
                if self.op(",") { let b = self.or()?; self.expect(",")?; let c = self.or()?; self.expect(")")?; return Ok(Ast::Vec3(Box::new([a, b, c]))); }
                self.expect(")")?; Ok(a)
            }
            Tok::Id(id) if self.op("(") => {
                let mut args = vec![];
                if !self.op(")") { loop { args.push(self.or()?); if self.op(")") { break; } self.expect(",")?; } }
                Ok(Ast::Call(id, args))
            }
            Tok::Id(id) => Ok(Ast::Var(id)),
            Tok::Op(o) => err(format!("unexpected '{o}'"), pos),
        }
    }
}
fn num(v: Val) -> Result<f32, RuleError> { if let Val::Num(x) = v { Ok(x) } else { err("expected number", 0) } }
fn vec3(v: Val) -> Result<Vec3, RuleError> { if let Val::Vec(x) = v { Ok(x) } else { err("expected vector", 0) } }
fn glob(s: &str, pat: &str) -> bool {
    match pat.split_once('*') { None => s == pat, Some((pre, rest)) => s.starts_with(pre) && (rest.is_empty() || (0..=s.len() - pre.len()).any(|k| glob(&s[pre.len() + k..], rest))) }
}
fn eval(a: &Ast, s: &Sample) -> Result<Val, RuleError> {
    use Val::*;
    Ok(match a {
        Ast::Num(v) => Num(*v),
        Ast::Str(v) => Str(v.clone()),
        Ast::Var(v) => match v.as_str() {
            "x" => Num(s.p.x), "y" => Num(s.p.y), "z" => Num(s.p.z), "nx" => Num(s.n.x), "ny" => Num(s.n.y), "nz" => Num(s.n.z),
            "pos" => Vec(s.p), "normal" => Vec(s.n), "r_yz" => Num(s.p.y.hypot(s.p.z)), "r_xz" => Num(s.p.x.hypot(s.p.z)), "r_xy" => Num(s.p.x.hypot(s.p.y)),
            "theta_yz" => Num(s.p.z.atan2(s.p.y).to_degrees().rem_euclid(360.0)), "step_tag" => Str(s.tag.to_string()), "area" => Num(s.area),
            _ => return err(format!("unknown identifier '{v}'"), 0),
        },
        Ast::Box { which, axis, max } => { let b = match which { BoxRef::Scope => s.bbox, BoxRef::Cad => s.cad }; Num(if *max { b.max[*axis] } else { b.min[*axis] }) }
        Ast::Member(v, k) => Num(vec3(eval(v, s)?)?[*k]),
        Ast::Vec3(c) => Vec(glam::vec3(num(eval(&c[0], s)?)?, num(eval(&c[1], s)?)?, num(eval(&c[2], s)?)?)),
        Ast::Neg(v) => match eval(v, s)? { Num(x) => Num(-x), Vec(x) => Vec(-x), _ => return err("cannot negate", 0) },
        Ast::Not(v) => Bool(!matches!(eval(v, s)?, Bool(true))),
        Ast::In(v, lo, hi) => { let x = num(eval(v, s)?)?; Bool(x >= num(eval(lo, s)?)? && x <= num(eval(hi, s)?)?) }
        Ast::Glob(v, p) => match eval(v, s)? { Str(t) => Bool(glob(&t, p)), _ => return err("'~' needs a string", 0) },
        Ast::Bin(Op::And, l, r) => Bool(matches!(eval(l, s)?, Bool(true)) && matches!(eval(r, s)?, Bool(true))),
        Ast::Bin(Op::Or, l, r) => Bool(matches!(eval(l, s)?, Bool(true)) || matches!(eval(r, s)?, Bool(true))),
        Ast::Bin(op, l, r) => {
            let (x, y) = (eval(l, s)?, eval(r, s)?);
            match op {
                Op::Eq => Bool(x == y), Op::Ne => Bool(x != y),
                _ => { let (x, y) = (num(x)?, num(y)?); match op {
                    Op::Lt => Bool(x < y), Op::Le => Bool(x <= y), Op::Gt => Bool(x > y), Op::Ge => Bool(x >= y),
                    Op::Add => Num(x + y), Op::Sub => Num(x - y), Op::Mul => Num(x * y), Op::Div => Num(x / y), _ => unreachable!() } }
            }
        }
        Ast::Call(f, args) => {
            let v: Vec<Val> = args.iter().map(|x| eval(x, s)).collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
            let n = |i: usize| num(v[i].clone());
            match (f.as_str(), v.len()) {
                ("abs", 1) => Num(n(0)?.abs()), ("sqrt", 1) => Num(n(0)?.sqrt()), ("min", 2) => Num(n(0)?.min(n(1)?)), ("max", 2) => Num(n(0)?.max(n(1)?)),
                ("dot", 2) => Num(vec3(v[0].clone())?.dot(vec3(v[1].clone())?)), ("mag", 1) => Num(vec3(v[0].clone())?.length()),
                ("cyl_radius_yz", 2) => Num((s.p.y - n(0)?).hypot(s.p.z - n(1)?)),
                ("cyl_radius_xz", 2) => Num((s.p.x - n(0)?).hypot(s.p.z - n(1)?)),
                ("cyl_radius_xy", 2) => Num((s.p.x - n(0)?).hypot(s.p.y - n(1)?)),
                ("ang_dist_yz", 1) => Num(((s.p.z.atan2(s.p.y).to_degrees() - n(0)? + 540.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0).abs()),
                ("inside_box", 6) => { let b: Vec<f32> = (0..6).map(n).collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
                    Bool(s.p.cmpge(glam::vec3(b[0], b[1], b[2])).all() && s.p.cmple(glam::vec3(b[3], b[4], b[5])).all()) }
                _ => return err(format!("unknown function {f}/{}", v.len()), 0),
            }
        }
    })
}

Created rust/cfd_cockpit_stage2/src/rules/synth.rs
Created rust/cfd_cockpit_stage2/src/rules/synth.rs

//! Smart Spatial Rule Synthesizer + RuleBook (overlap detection & face highlight styles).
use super::dsl::{Aabb, Rule, Sample};
use glam::{Mat3, Vec3};
/// Sample vertices of one face group (fluid-outward normals), produced from CFDGEO01 on load.
pub struct PatchSamples { pub id: u32, pub name: String, pub step_tag: String, pub area: f32, pub centroid: Vec3, pub p: Vec<Vec3>, pub n: Vec<Vec3> }
#[derive(Clone, Copy)] pub struct Scope { pub bbox: Aabb, pub cad: Aabb }
impl Scope {
    pub fn from_patches(ps: &[PatchSamples]) -> Self {
        let mut b = Aabb { min: Vec3::splat(f32::MAX), max: Vec3::splat(f32::MIN) };
        let mut c = b;
        for p in ps { for q in &p.p { b.min = b.min.min(*q); b.max = b.max.max(*q); if !p.step_tag.starts_with("FARFIELD") { c.min = c.min.min(*q); c.max = c.max.max(*q); } } }
        Self { bbox: b, cad: c }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)] pub enum Kind { Planar, Cylinder, Cone, Revolved, Freeform }
pub struct PatchDescriptor {
    pub id: u32, pub tag: String, pub kind: Kind, pub mean_n: Vec3, pub cone_deg: f32, pub centroid: Vec3, pub bbox: Aabb,
    pub axis: Option<usize>, pub center: [f32; 2], pub r_min: f32, pub r_max: f32, pub theta_c: f32, pub theta_half: f32, pub siblings: Vec<u32>,
}
fn tag_prefix(t: &str) -> &str { t.trim_end_matches(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()).trim_end_matches('_') }
impl PatchDescriptor {
    pub fn analyze(p: &PatchSamples, all: &[PatchSamples]) -> Self {
        let n = p.p.len() as f32;
        let mut bbox = Aabb { min: Vec3::splat(f32::MAX), max: Vec3::splat(f32::MIN) };
        p.p.iter().for_each(|q| { bbox.min = bbox.min.min(*q); bbox.max = bbox.max.max(*q); });
        let mean: Vec3 = p.n.iter().copied().sum::<Vec3>() / n;
        let mean_n = mean.normalize_or_zero();
        let cone_deg = p.n.iter().map(|v| v.dot(mean_n).clamp(-1.0, 1.0).acos().to_degrees()).fold(0.0, f32::max);
        // centred normal covariance → smallest eigenvector is the revolution axis
        let mut cov = Mat3::ZERO;
        for v in &p.n { let d = *v - mean; cov += Mat3::from_cols(d * d.x, d * d.y, d * d.z) / n; }
        let (evals, evecs) = jacobi_eigen(cov);
        let pre = tag_prefix(&p.step_tag);
        let siblings = all.iter().filter(|q| tag_prefix(&q.step_tag) == pre && pre != p.step_tag && ((q.area - p.area) / p.area).abs() < 0.03).map(|q| q.id).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let mut d = PatchDescriptor { id: p.id, tag: p.step_tag.clone(), kind: Kind::Freeform, mean_n, cone_deg, centroid: p.centroid, bbox, axis: None,
            center: [0.0; 2], r_min: 0.0, r_max: 0.0, theta_c: 0.0, theta_half: 0.0, siblings: if siblings.is_empty() { vec![p.id] } else { siblings } };
        if cone_deg < 0.6 { d.kind = Kind::Planar; return d; }
        let axis = evecs[0];
        let snapped = (0..3).find(|&k| axis[k].abs() > 0.995);
        let k = snapped.unwrap_or(0);
        let (u, w) = ((k + 1) % 3, (k + 2) % 3);
        let [cu, cw] = if snapped.is_some() { kasa_circle(p.p.iter().map(|q| (q[u], q[w]))) } else { [0.0, 0.0] };
        let radii: Vec<f32> = p.p.iter().map(|q| (q[u] - cu).hypot(q[w] - cw)).collect();
        d.r_min = radii.iter().copied().fold(f32::MAX, f32::min);
        d.r_max = radii.iter().copied().fold(0.0, f32::max);
        d.center = [cu, cw];
        d.theta_c = (p.centroid[w] - cw).atan2(p.centroid[u] - cu).to_degrees().rem_euclid(360.0);
        d.theta_half = p.p.iter().map(|q| (((q[w] - cw).atan2(q[u] - cu).to_degrees() - d.theta_c + 540.0).rem_euclid(360.0) - 180.0).abs()).fold(0.0, f32::max);
        if snapped.is_some() && evals[0] / (evals[0] + evals[1] + evals[2]).max(1e-12) < 0.01 {
            d.axis = Some(k);
            let r = 0.5 * (d.r_min + d.r_max);
            let n_std = (p.n.iter().map(|v| (v[k] - mean[k]).powi(2)).sum::<f32>() / n).sqrt();
            d.kind = if (d.r_max - d.r_min) / r < 0.01 && mean[k].abs() < 0.05 { Kind::Cylinder } else if n_std < 0.02 { Kind::Cone } else { Kind::Revolved };
        }
        d
    }
}
pub struct Candidate { pub label: &'static str, pub rule: String, pub matches: Vec<u32>, pub unique: bool, pub pattern: bool, pub geometric: bool }
pub struct Synthesizer;
impl Synthesizer {
    pub fn propose(d: &PatchDescriptor, scope: &Scope, all: &[PatchSamples]) -> Vec<Candidate> {
        let ax = ["x", "y", "z"];
        let eval = |r: &str| -> Vec<u32> { Rule::compile(r).map(|rule| RuleBook::matches(&rule, scope, all)).unwrap_or_default() };
        let unique = |r: &str| { let m = eval(r); m.len() == 1 && m[0] == d.id };
        let pad = |k: usize| ((d.bbox.max[k] - d.bbox.min[k]) * 0.02).max(1e-4);
        let range = |k: usize| format!("{} in [{:.4}, {:.4}]", ax[k], d.bbox.min[k] - pad(k), d.bbox.max[k] + pad(k));
        let mut out: Vec<(&'static str, String, bool)> = vec![];
        match d.kind {
            Kind::Planar => {
                if let Some(k) = (0..3).find(|&k| d.mean_n[k].abs() > 0.995) {
                    let c1 = if d.mean_n[k] < 0.0 { format!("normal.{} < -0.995", ax[k]) } else { format!("normal.{} > 0.995", ax[k]) };
                    let v = d.centroid[k];
                    let c2 = if (v - scope.bbox.min[k]).abs() < 1e-6 { format!("abs({0} - bbox.{0}_min) < 1e-4", ax[k]) }
                        else if (v - scope.bbox.max[k]).abs() < 1e-6 { format!("abs({0} - bbox.{0}_max) < 1e-4", ax[k]) }
                        else if (v - scope.cad.max[k]).abs() < 1e-6 { format!("abs({0} - cad.{0}_max) < 1e-4", ax[k]) }
                        else { format!("abs({} - {:.5}) < 1e-4", ax[k], v) };
                    let mut r = format!("{c1} and {c2}");
                    if !unique(&r) { r = format!("{r} and {} and {}", range((k + 1) % 3), range((k + 2) % 3)); }
                    out.push(("Planar · axis-aligned", r, true));
                } else {
                    let n = d.mean_n;
                    out.push(("Planar · oblique", format!("dot(normal, ({:.4}, {:.4}, {:.4})) > 0.995 and abs(dot(pos, ({:.4}, {:.4}, {:.4})) - {:.5}) < 1e-4", n.x, n.y, n.z, n.x, n.y, n.z, d.centroid.dot(n)), true));
                }
            }
            Kind::Cylinder | Kind::Cone | Kind::Revolved => {
                let k = d.axis.unwrap();
                let f = format!("cyl_radius_{}{}({:.5}, {:.5})", ax[(k + 1) % 3], ax[(k + 2) % 3], d.center[0], d.center[1]);
                let r = 0.5 * (d.r_min + d.r_max);
                let mut rule = String::new();
                for tol in [0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01] {
                    rule = if d.kind == Kind::Cylinder { format!("{f} in [{:.4}, {:.4}]", r * (1.0 - tol), r * (1.0 + tol)) }
                        else { format!("abs(normal.{} - {:.3}) < 0.03 and {f} in [{:.4}, {:.4}]", ax[k], d.mean_n[k], d.r_min * (1.0 - tol), d.r_max * (1.0 + tol)) };
                    if unique(&rule) { break; }
                }
                if !unique(&rule) { rule = format!("{rule} and {}", range(k)); }
                out.push((if d.kind == Kind::Cylinder { "Cylinder · radius band" } else { "Revolution · normal angle + radius" }, rule, true));
            }
            Kind::Freeform => {
                let region = format!("r_yz in [{:.4}, {:.4}] and {}", d.r_min * 0.98, d.r_max * 1.02, range(0));
                if d.siblings.len() > 1 {
                    out.push(("Pattern · annular region", region.clone(), true));
                    out.push(("Instance · angular sector", format!("{region} and ang_dist_yz({:.2}) < {:.2}", d.theta_c, (d.theta_half + 1.5).min(179.0)), true));
                } else { out.push(("Freeform · bounding region", format!("{} and {} and {}", range(0), range(1), range(2)), true)); }
            }
        }
        if d.siblings.len() > 1 { out.push(("STEP tag pattern", format!("step_tag ~ \"{}_*\"", tag_prefix(&d.tag)), false)); }
        out.push(("STEP tag (exact)", format!("step_tag ~ \"{}\"", d.tag), false));
        out.into_iter().map(|(label, rule, geometric)| {
            let m = eval(&rule);
            let unique = m.len() == 1 && m[0] == d.id;
            let pattern = d.siblings.len() > 1 && m.len() == d.siblings.len() && m.iter().all(|x| d.siblings.contains(x));
            Candidate { label, rule, matches: m, unique, pattern, geometric }
        }).collect()
    }
}
#[derive(Clone)] pub struct Bc { pub name: String, pub kind: BcKind, pub rule: String }
#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq)] pub enum BcKind { VelocityInlet, PressureOutlet, Wall, MrfWall, Symmetry, Interface }
#[derive(Default)] pub struct RuleBook { pub bcs: Vec<Bc> }
#[derive(Default)] pub struct Draft { pub text: String, pub candidates: Vec<Candidate>, compiled: Option<Rule>, pub error: Option<String> }
/// Packed per-face style uploaded to the `FaceStyle` SSBO read by `pick.wgsl`.
#[repr(C)] #[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
pub struct FaceStyle { pub rgba: u32, pub flags: u32 }
pub const STYLE_DRAFT: u32 = 1; pub const STYLE_OVERLAP: u32 = 2;
impl Draft {
    pub fn set_text(&mut self, t: String) { self.compiled = None; self.error = None; match Rule::compile(&t) { Ok(r) => self.compiled = Some(r), Err(e) => self.error = Some(e.to_string()) } self.text = t; }
    pub fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui, book: &mut RuleBook, _ps: &[PatchSamples]) {
        let mut t = self.text.clone();
        if ui.add(egui::TextEdit::multiline(&mut t).code_editor().desired_rows(3)).changed() { self.set_text(t); }
        if let Some(e) = &self.error { ui.colored_label(crate::WARN_RED, e); }
        for c in &self.candidates {
            if ui.selectable_label(self.text == c.rule, format!("{} · {} faces{}", c.label, c.matches.len(), if c.unique { " · unique" } else { "" })).clicked() { self.set_text(c.rule.clone()); }
        }
        if ui.add_enabled(self.compiled.is_some(), egui::Button::new("Commit as BC")).clicked() { book.bcs.push(Bc { name: format!("bc_{}", book.bcs.len()), kind: BcKind::Wall, rule: self.text.clone() }); }
    }
}
impl RuleBook {
    pub fn matches(rule: &Rule, scope: &Scope, ps: &[PatchSamples]) -> Vec<u32> {
        ps.iter().filter(|p| rule.matches(p.p.iter().zip(&p.n).map(|(q, n)| Sample { p: *q, n: *n, tag: &p.step_tag, area: p.area, bbox: &scope.bbox, cad: &scope.cad }).collect::<Vec<_>>().into_iter()).unwrap_or(false)).map(|p| p.id).collect()
    }
    /// Electric Blue (#004AFD) for draft matches, Red (#EF233C) where the draft overlaps another BC.
    pub fn face_styles(&self, draft: &Draft, ps: &[PatchSamples]) -> Vec<FaceStyle> {
        let scope = Scope::from_patches(ps);
        let n = ps.iter().map(|p| p.id as usize + 1).max().unwrap_or(0);
        let mut owners = vec![0u32; n];
        for bc in &self.bcs { if let Ok(r) = Rule::compile(&bc.rule) { for id in Self::matches(&r, &scope, ps) { owners[id as usize] += 1; } } }
        let draft_ids = draft.compiled.as_ref().map(|r| Self::matches(r, &scope, ps)).unwrap_or_default();
        (0..n).map(|i| {
            let in_draft = draft_ids.contains(&(i as u32));
            match (in_draft, owners[i]) {
                (true, 0) => FaceStyle { rgba: 0xFF_FD_4A_00, flags: STYLE_DRAFT },
                (true, _) | (false, 2..) => FaceStyle { rgba: 0xFF_3C_23_EF, flags: STYLE_OVERLAP },
                (false, 1) => FaceStyle { rgba: 0xFF_AD_A0_9A, flags: 0 },
                _ => FaceStyle { rgba: 0xFF_9C_91_8D, flags: 0 },
            }
        }).collect()
    }
}
fn kasa_circle(pts: impl Iterator<Item = (f32, f32)>) -> [f32; 2] {
    let (mut m, mut b) = (Mat3::ZERO, Vec3::ZERO);
    for (x, y) in pts { let r = Vec3::new(x, y, 1.0); m += Mat3::from_cols(r * x, r * y, r); b += r * (x * x + y * y); }
    let s = m.inverse() * b;
    [if (s.x * 0.5).abs() < 2e-5 { 0.0 } else { s.x * 0.5 }, if (s.y * 0.5).abs() < 2e-5 { 0.0 } else { s.y * 0.5 }]
}
/// Cyclic Jacobi; returns ascending eigenvalues and matching eigenvectors.
pub fn jacobi_eigen(m: Mat3) -> ([f32; 3], [Vec3; 3]) {
    let mut a = m.to_cols_array_2d();
    let mut v = Mat3::IDENTITY.to_cols_array_2d();
    for _ in 0..32 {
        for (p, q) in [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)] {
            if a[q][p].abs() < 1e-14 { continue; }
            let th = (a[q][q] - a[p][p]) / (2.0 * a[q][p]);
            let t = th.signum() / (th.abs() + (th * th + 1.0).sqrt());
            let (c, s) = (1.0 / (t * t + 1.0).sqrt(), t / (t * t + 1.0).sqrt());
            for k in 0..3 { let (akp, akq) = (a[p][k], a[q][k]); a[p][k] = c * akp - s * akq; a[q][k] = s * akp + c * akq; }
            for k in 0..3 { let (apk, aqk) = (a[k][p], a[k][q]); a[k][p] = c * apk - s * aqk; a[k][q] = s * apk + c * aqk; }
            for k in 0..3 { let (vp, vq) = (v[p][k], v[q][k]); v[p][k] = c * vp - s * vq; v[q][k] = s * vp + c * vq; }
        }
    }
    let mut idx = [0, 1, 2];
    idx.sort_by(|&i, &j| a[i][i].total_cmp(&a[j][j]));
    (idx.map(|i| a[i][i]), idx.map(|i| Vec3::from(v[i])))
}

//! R32Uint `face_group_id` pick target with asynchronous 1-pixel readback and the FaceStyle SSBO.
use super::synth::FaceStyle;
use std::sync::{atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering}, Arc};
pub const PICK_FORMAT: wgpu::TextureFormat = wgpu::TextureFormat::R32Uint;
pub const DEPTH_FORMAT: wgpu::TextureFormat = wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float; // Reverse-Z: clear 0.0, GreaterEqual
pub const HANDLE_BASE: u32 = 0x0010_0000;
pub struct PickTarget {
    pub color: wgpu::Texture, pub color_view: wgpu::TextureView, pub depth_view: wgpu::TextureView,
    readback: wgpu::Buffer, pub styles: wgpu::Buffer,
    pending: Option<(u32, u32)>, in_flight: Option<Arc<AtomicBool>>, size: (u32, u32),
}
impl PickTarget {
    pub fn new(device: &wgpu::Device, size: (u32, u32)) -> Self {
        let ext = wgpu::Extent3d { width: size.0, height: size.1, depth_or_array_layers: 1 };
        let mk = |format, usage, label| device.create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor { label: Some(label), size: ext, mip_level_count: 1, sample_count: 1,
            dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2, format, usage, view_formats: &[] });
        let color = mk(PICK_FORMAT, wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::COPY_SRC, "pick.face_group_id");
        let depth = mk(DEPTH_FORMAT, wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT, "pick.depth");
        Self {
            color_view: color.create_view(&Default::default()), depth_view: depth.create_view(&Default::default()), color,
            // copy_texture_to_buffer requires bytes_per_row % 256 == 0 even for a single texel
            readback: device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor { label: Some("pick.readback"), size: 256, usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ, mapped_at_creation: false }),
            styles: device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor { label: Some("face_styles"), size: 8 * 4096, usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST, mapped_at_creation: false }),
            pending: None, in_flight: None, size,
        }
    }
    /// Render-pass descriptor for the pick pass; the caller scissors to the requested pixel.
    pub fn pass_desc(&self) -> (wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment<'_>, wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment<'_>) {
        (wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment { view: &self.color_view, resolve_target: None, ops: wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store } },
         wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment { view: &self.depth_view, depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations { load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(0.0), store: wgpu::StoreOp::Discard }), stencil_ops: None })
    }
    pub fn request(&mut self, px: (u32, u32)) { if px.0 < self.size.0 && px.1 < self.size.1 { self.pending = Some(px); } }
    pub fn scissor(&self) -> Option<(u32, u32)> { self.pending }
    /// Encode the 1-texel copy after the pick pass and start mapping.
    pub fn encode_copy(&mut self, enc: &mut wgpu::CommandEncoder) {
        let Some((x, y)) = self.pending.take() else { return };
        enc.copy_texture_to_buffer(
            wgpu::ImageCopyTexture { texture: &self.color, mip_level: 0, origin: wgpu::Origin3d { x, y, z: 0 }, aspect: wgpu::TextureAspect::All },
            wgpu::ImageCopyBuffer { buffer: &self.readback, layout: wgpu::ImageDataLayout { offset: 0, bytes_per_row: Some(256), rows_per_image: Some(1) } },
            wgpu::Extent3d { width: 1, height: 1, depth_or_array_layers: 1 },
        );
        let flag = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
        let f2 = flag.clone();
        // map after submit: the closure fires once the GPU copy has completed
        self.readback.slice(..4).map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |r| if r.is_ok() { f2.store(true, Ordering::Release) });
        self.in_flight = Some(flag);
    }
    /// Non-blocking poll; returns the picked face_group_id (None for background / handles).
    pub fn poll(&mut self, device: &wgpu::Device) -> Option<u32> {
        device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Poll);
        let ready = self.in_flight.as_ref()?.load(Ordering::Acquire);
        if !ready { return None; }
        self.in_flight = None;
        let id = { let view = self.readback.slice(..4).get_mapped_range(); u32::from_le_bytes([view[0], view[1], view[2], view[3]]) };
        self.readback.unmap();
        match id { 0 => None, v if v >= HANDLE_BASE => None, v => Some(v - 1) }
    }
    pub fn upload_styles(&self, queue: &wgpu::Queue, s: &[FaceStyle]) { queue.write_buffer(&self.styles, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(s)); }
}

// Surface pass + pick pass sharing one vertex layout.
// Reverse-Z infinite projection: depth cleared to 0.0, compare = GreaterEqual.
struct Camera { view_proj: mat4x4<f32>, view: mat4x4<f32>, eye: vec4<f32> };
struct FaceStyle { rgba: u32, flags: u32 };
@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> cam: Camera;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage, read> styles: array<FaceStyle>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> hover: vec4<u32>;   // x = hovered face, y = selected face
struct VsIn { @location(0) pos: vec3<f32>, @location(1) nrm: vec3<f32>, @location(2) face: u32 };
struct VsOut { @builtin(position) clip: vec4<f32>, @location(0) vn: vec3<f32>, @location(1) @interpolate(flat) face: u32 };
@vertex fn vs(v: VsIn) -> VsOut {
    var o: VsOut;
    o.clip = cam.view_proj * vec4(v.pos, 1.0);
    o.vn = (cam.view * vec4(v.nrm, 0.0)).xyz;
    o.face = v.face;
    return o;
}
fn unpack(c: u32) -> vec4<f32> { return vec4(f32(c & 0xffu), f32((c >> 8u) & 0xffu), f32((c >> 16u) & 0xffu), f32(c >> 24u)) / 255.0; }
@fragment fn fs_shade(i: VsOut) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    var n = normalize(i.vn);
    if (n.z < 0.0) { n = -n; }                          // two-sided studio lighting in view space
    let s = styles[i.face];
    var base = unpack(s.rgba).rgb;
    let key = max(dot(n, normalize(vec3(0.35, 0.55, 0.75))), 0.0);
    let fill = max(dot(n, normalize(vec3(-0.7, -0.15, 0.45))), 0.0);
    let rim = pow(1.0 - max(n.z, 0.0), 3.0);
    var c = base * (0.26 + 0.58 * key + 0.16 * fill + 0.06 * (0.5 + 0.5 * n.y)) + rim * 0.07;
    if ((s.flags & 2u) != 0u) { c = mix(c, vec3(0.937, 0.137, 0.235), 0.35); }   // #EF233C overlap pulse
    if (i.face == hover.x) { c = mix(c, vec3(1.0, 0.82, 0.42), 0.38); }
    return vec4(c, 1.0);
}
// R32Uint target: 0 = background, face_group_id + 1 otherwise. Gizmo handles write HANDLE_BASE + id.
@fragment fn fs_pick(i: VsOut) -> @location(0) u32 { return i.face + 1u; }
struct HandleU { id: u32 };
@group(1) @binding(0) var<uniform> handle: HandleU;
@fragment fn fs_pick_handle() -> @location(0) u32 { return handle.id; }

// Differential field Δφ(x) = φ_B(x) − φ_A(x) on a slice plane, evaluated per fragment.
// Both runs are CFDFLD03 structured blocks uploaded as R32Float 3D textures on the SAME grid
// (non-matching meshes are first resampled by `compare::ab::Resampler`).
// The diverging Moreland cool-warm LUT (Oklab-interpolated, 256 texels) is centred strictly at 0.
struct Slice { origin: vec4<f32>, eu: vec4<f32>, ev: vec4<f32>, bmin: vec4<f32>, bmax: vec4<f32>, dims: vec4<i32>, mode: u32, delta_max: f32, iso: f32, _pad: f32 };
struct Camera { view_proj: mat4x4<f32>, view: mat4x4<f32>, eye: vec4<f32> };
@group(0) @binding(0) var<uniform> cam: Camera;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> sl: Slice;
@group(0) @binding(2) var phi_a: texture_3d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var phi_b: texture_3d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var lut_tex: texture_1d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(5) var lut_smp: sampler;
const MASKED: f32 = 1e29;
struct VsOut { @builtin(position) clip: vec4<f32>, @location(0) w: vec3<f32> };
@vertex fn vs(@builtin(vertex_index) vi: u32) -> VsOut {
    let uv = array(vec2(0.0, 0.0), vec2(1.0, 0.0), vec2(1.0, 1.0), vec2(0.0, 0.0), vec2(1.0, 1.0), vec2(0.0, 1.0))[vi];
    let w = sl.origin.xyz + sl.eu.xyz * uv.x + sl.ev.xyz * uv.y;
    return VsOut(cam.view_proj * vec4(w, 1.0), w);
}
// Manual trilinear (R32Float is not filterable on all adapters); any masked corner → masked.
fn tri(t: texture_3d<f32>, g: vec3<f32>) -> f32 {
    let i0 = clamp(vec3<i32>(floor(g)), vec3(0), sl.dims.xyz - 2);
    let f = clamp(g - vec3<f32>(i0), vec3(0.0), vec3(1.0));
    var c: array<f32, 8>;
    var mx = -1e38;
    for (var k = 0; k < 8; k++) {
        let o = vec3<i32>(k & 1, (k >> 1) & 1, (k >> 2) & 1);
        c[k] = textureLoad(t, i0 + o, 0).r;
        mx = max(mx, c[k]);
    }
    if (mx > MASKED) { return 1e30; }
    let x00 = mix(c[0], c[1], f.x); let x10 = mix(c[2], c[3], f.x);
    let x01 = mix(c[4], c[5], f.x); let x11 = mix(c[6], c[7], f.x);
    return mix(mix(x00, x10, f.y), mix(x01, x11, f.y), f.z);
}
@fragment fn fs(i: VsOut) -> @location(0) vec4<f32> {
    let g = (i.w - sl.bmin.xyz) / (sl.bmax.xyz - sl.bmin.xyz) * vec3<f32>(sl.dims.xyz - 1);
    let a = tri(phi_a, g);
    let b = tri(phi_b, g);
    if (a > MASKED || b > MASKED) {
        let h = step(0.5, fract((i.clip.x + i.clip.y) / 7.0));
        return vec4(mix(vec3(0.16), vec3(0.24), h), 1.0);          // solid / masked hatch
    }
    var v: f32;
    var t: f32;
    switch sl.mode {
        case 0u: { v = a; t = v; }
        case 1u: { v = b; t = v; }
        default: { v = b - a; t = 0.5 + 0.5 * clamp(v / sl.delta_max, -1.0, 1.0); }  // Δφ = 0 ↔ t = 0.5 exactly
    }
    var col = textureSampleLevel(lut_tex, lut_smp, t, 0.0).rgb;
    if (sl.iso > 0.0) {
        let q = t * sl.iso;
        let line = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.0, fwidth(q) * 1.3, abs(fract(q - 0.5) - 0.5));
        col = mix(col, col * 0.3, line * 0.55);
    }
    return vec4(col, 1.0);
}
// Optional compute variant: writes |Δφ| statistics (max, L2) for the KPI panel / auto-range.
@group(1) @binding(0) var<storage, read_write> stats: array<atomic<u32>, 2>;
@compute @workgroup_size(8, 8, 4) fn reduce_delta(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id: vec3<u32>) {
    let d = vec3<i32>(id);
    if (any(d >= sl.dims.xyz)) { return; }
    let a = textureLoad(phi_a, d, 0).r;
    let b = textureLoad(phi_b, d, 0).r;
    if (a > MASKED || b > MASKED) { return; }
    atomicMax(&stats[0], bitcast<u32>(abs(b - a)));                // IEEE754 positive floats sort as u32
    atomicAdd(&stats[1], u32(min(abs(b - a) * 1e3, 4.0e6)));
}

//! Farfield wind-tunnel box (in multiples of L) and cylindrical MRF / sliding-mesh zone gizmos.
use crate::rules::dsl::Aabb;
use glam::Vec3;
pub const A_REF: f32 = 340.3; // speed of sound at ISA SL [m/s]
/// Closest-point parameter t on axis (o + t·d) to a pick ray — the core of every axis-constrained drag.
pub fn ray_axis_t(ray_o: Vec3, ray_d: Vec3, o: Vec3, d: Vec3) -> f32 {
    let w0 = o - ray_o;
    let (a, b, c) = (d.dot(d), d.dot(ray_d), ray_d.dot(ray_d));
    let (dd, e) = (d.dot(w0), ray_d.dot(w0));
    let den = a * c - b * b;
    if den.abs() < 1e-12 { 0.0 } else { (b * e - c * dd) / den }
}
/// Camera-facing plane drag (free 2D translation of actors).
pub fn ray_plane(ray_o: Vec3, ray_d: Vec3, p: Vec3, n: Vec3) -> Option<Vec3> {
    let den = ray_d.dot(n);
    (den.abs() > 1e-9).then(|| ray_o + ray_d * ((p - ray_o).dot(n) / den)).filter(|q| (*q - ray_o).dot(ray_d) > 0.0)
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct FarfieldBox { pub l: f32, pub upstream: f32, pub downstream: f32, pub lateral: f32, pub vertical: f32, pub cad: Aabb }
impl FarfieldBox {
    pub fn new(l: f32) -> Self { Self { l, upstream: 5.0, downstream: 15.0, lateral: 5.0, vertical: 5.0, cad: Aabb { min: Vec3::ZERO, max: Vec3::X * l } } }
    pub fn aabb(&self) -> Aabb {
        Aabb { min: Vec3::new(self.cad.min.x - self.upstream * self.l, -self.vertical * self.l, -self.lateral * self.l),
               max: Vec3::new(self.cad.max.x + self.downstream * self.l, self.vertical * self.l, self.lateral * self.l) }
    }
    pub fn blockage(&self, frontal_area: f32) -> f32 { let b = self.aabb(); frontal_area / ((b.max.y - b.min.y) * (b.max.z - b.min.z)) }
    /// Face handle k (0:x−,1:x+,2:y−,3:y+,4:z−,5:z+) dragged to world coordinate v → snap to ¼ L.
    pub fn drag_face(&mut self, k: usize, v: f32) {
        let snap = |x: f32| ((x * 4.0).round() / 4.0).max(1.0);
        match k { 0 => self.upstream = snap((self.cad.min.x - v) / self.l), 1 => self.downstream = snap((v - self.cad.max.x) / self.l),
                  2 | 3 => self.vertical = snap(v.abs() / self.l), _ => self.lateral = snap(v.abs() / self.l) }
    }
    /// The six faces become FARFIELD_* patches so W1 rules like `abs(x - bbox.x_min) < 1e-4` resolve.
    pub fn patches(&self) -> [(&'static str, Vec3); 6] {
        [("FARFIELD_XMIN", -Vec3::X), ("FARFIELD_XMAX", Vec3::X), ("FARFIELD_SIDE_1", -Vec3::Y), ("FARFIELD_SIDE_2", Vec3::Y), ("FARFIELD_SIDE_3", -Vec3::Z), ("FARFIELD_SIDE_4", Vec3::Z)]
    }
    pub fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        ui.heading("Farfield box");
        ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut self.upstream, 1.0..=20.0).step_by(0.25).suffix(" L").text("upstream"));
        ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut self.downstream, 2.0..=40.0).step_by(0.25).suffix(" L").text("downstream"));
        ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut self.lateral, 1.0..=20.0).step_by(0.25).suffix(" L").text("lateral"));
        let b = self.aabb();
        ui.monospace(format!("{:.2} × {:.2} × {:.2} m", b.max.x - b.min.x, b.max.y - b.min.y, b.max.z - b.min.z));
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct MrfZone { pub axis_origin: Vec3, pub axis: Vec3, pub radius: f32, pub x0: f32, pub x1: f32, pub rpm: f32, pub v_inf: f32, pub r_tip: f32, pub r_shroud: f32, pub blades: u32 }
impl Default for MrfZone { fn default() -> Self { Self { axis_origin: Vec3::ZERO, axis: Vec3::X, radius: 0.0448, x0: 0.012, x1: 0.040, rpm: 32_000.0, v_inf: 30.0, r_tip: 0.0445, r_shroud: 0.045, blades: 7 } } }
#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)] pub enum Severity { Ok, Warn, Error }
impl MrfZone {
    pub fn omega(&self) -> f32 { self.rpm / 60.0 * std::f32::consts::TAU }
    pub fn tip_speed(&self) -> f32 { self.omega() * self.r_tip }
    pub fn tip_mach(&self) -> f32 { self.tip_speed() / A_REF }
    pub fn helical_mach(&self) -> f32 { self.tip_speed().hypot(self.v_inf) / A_REF }
    pub fn bpf(&self) -> f32 { self.blades as f32 * self.rpm / 60.0 }
    pub fn validate(&self, blade_x: (f32, f32), stator_x0: f32) -> Vec<(Severity, String)> {
        let mut v = vec![];
        if self.radius <= self.r_tip { v.push((Severity::Error, format!("interface r={:.2} mm cuts the blades", self.radius * 1e3))); }
        else if self.radius < self.r_shroud { v.push((Severity::Ok, "interface in tip gap".into())); }
        else { v.push((Severity::Warn, "zone encloses shroud: mark DUCT_SHROUD as absolute-frame wall".into())); }
        if self.x0 > blade_x.0 || self.x1 < blade_x.1 { v.push((Severity::Error, "zone does not enclose blades axially".into())); }
        if self.x1 > stator_x0 { v.push((Severity::Warn, "zone intersects stator row".into())); }
        match self.tip_mach() { m if m > 0.85 => v.push((Severity::Error, format!("M_tip {m:.2}: transonic"))), m if m > 0.7 => v.push((Severity::Warn, format!("M_tip {m:.2}: compressible"))), _ => {} }
        v
    }
    pub fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        ui.heading("MRF zone");
        ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut self.rpm, 2000.0..=60_000.0).text("RPM"));
        ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut self.radius, 0.025..=0.09).text("interface r [m]"));
        let m = self.tip_mach();
        let col = if m > 0.85 { crate::WARN_RED } else if m > 0.7 { egui::Color32::from_rgb(245, 165, 36) } else { egui::Color32::from_rgb(34, 197, 94) };
        ui.colored_label(col, format!("U_tip {:.1} m/s · M_tip {:.3} · M_hel {:.3} · BPF {:.0} Hz", self.tip_speed(), m, self.helical_mach(), self.bpf()));
    }
}

//! Physics actors: DPM spray injector (cone gizmo + Rosin–Rammler), FWH microphones (distance sphere,
//! Gutin tonal estimate) and anisotropic porous media with local principal axes (e1, e2, e3).
use glam::{Mat3, Vec3};
pub fn gamma(z: f64) -> f64 {
    if z < 0.5 { return std::f64::consts::PI / ((std::f64::consts::PI * z).sin() * gamma(1.0 - z)); }
    const G: [f64; 8] = [676.5203681218851, -1259.1392167224028, 771.32342877765313, -176.61502916214059, 12.507343278686905, -0.13857109526572012, 9.9843695780195716e-6, 1.5056327351493116e-7];
    let z = z - 1.0;
    let x = G.iter().enumerate().fold(0.99999999999980993, |a, (i, c)| a + c / (z + i as f64 + 1.0));
    let t = z + 7.5;
    (2.0 * std::f64::consts::PI).sqrt() * t.powf(z + 0.5) * (-t).exp() * x
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct SprayInjector { pub pos: Vec3, pub axis: Vec3, pub half_angle_deg: f32, pub length: f32, pub d32_um: f32, pub spread_n: f32, pub mdot_gs: f32, pub u_inj: f32 }
impl SprayInjector {
    /// Characteristic diameter X̄ of the volume-based RR distribution: d32 = X̄ / Γ(1 − 1/n).
    pub fn x_bar(&self) -> f32 { self.d32_um * gamma(1.0 - 1.0 / self.spread_n as f64) as f32 }
    pub fn pdf(&self, d: f32) -> f32 { let (x, n) = (self.x_bar(), self.spread_n); n / x * (d / x).powf(n - 1.0) * (-(d / x).powf(n)).exp() }
    /// Inverse-CDF sampling used when seeding parcels: d = X̄ (−ln(1−u))^(1/n).
    pub fn sample_diameter(&self, u: f32) -> f32 { self.x_bar() * (-(1.0 - u.min(0.9999)).ln()).powf(1.0 / self.spread_n) }
    /// Uniform direction inside the cone (u, v ∈ [0,1)).
    pub fn sample_dir(&self, u: f32, v: f32) -> Vec3 {
        let a = self.axis.normalize();
        let (e1, e2) = a.any_orthonormal_pair();
        let cos_t = 1.0 - u * (1.0 - self.half_angle_deg.to_radians().cos());
        let sin_t = (1.0 - cos_t * cos_t).sqrt();
        let phi = v * std::f32::consts::TAU;
        a * cos_t + (e1 * phi.cos() + e2 * phi.sin()) * sin_t
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct FwhMic { pub name: String, pub pos: Vec3 }
impl FwhMic {
    pub fn distance(&self, src: Vec3) -> f32 { (self.pos - src).length() }
    /// Polar angle from the upstream rotor axis (−x).
    pub fn theta_deg(&self, src: Vec3) -> f32 { let d = (self.pos - src).normalize(); (-d.x).clamp(-1.0, 1.0).acos().to_degrees() }
}
fn bessel_j(n: u32, x: f64) -> f64 {
    let mut term = (x / 2.0).powi(n as i32) / gamma(n as f64 + 1.0);
    let mut s = term;
    for k in 1..40 { term *= -(x * x) / (4.0 * k as f64 * (k + n) as f64); s += term; if term.abs() < 1e-16 * s.abs() { break; } }
    s
}
/// Gutin far-field loading noise, harmonics m = 1..3, plus broadband floor → OASPL [dB re 20 µPa].
pub fn gutin_oaspl(rpm: f64, blades: u32, r_tip: f64, thrust: f64, torque: f64, s: f64, theta_deg: f64) -> f64 {
    let (a0, om, re, th) = (340.3, rpm / 60.0 * std::f64::consts::TAU, 0.8 * r_tip, theta_deg.to_radians());
    let mut e = 0.0;
    for m in 1..=3u32 {
        let mb = (m * blades) as f64;
        let pm = mb * om / (2.0 * 2f64.sqrt() * std::f64::consts::PI * a0 * s) * (-thrust * th.cos() + torque * a0 / (om * re * re)).abs() * bessel_j(m * blades, mb * om * re * th.sin() / a0).abs();
        e += (pm / 2e-5).powi(2);
    }
    let mt = om * r_tip / a0;
    let bb = 58.0 + 50.0 * (mt / 0.4).log10() - 20.0 * s.log10() + 10.0 * (0.3 + th.sin().powi(2)).log10();
    10.0 * (e + 10f64.powf(bb / 10.0)).log10()
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct PorousZone { pub center: Vec3, pub half_extent: Vec3, pub yaw: f32, pub pitch: f32, pub roll: f32, pub permeability: Vec3, pub c2: Vec3 }
impl PorousZone {
    /// Intrinsic yaw(Y) · pitch(Z) · roll(X); columns are the principal axes e1, e2, e3.
    pub fn frame(&self) -> Mat3 { Mat3::from_rotation_y(self.yaw.to_radians()) * Mat3::from_rotation_z(self.pitch.to_radians()) * Mat3::from_rotation_x(self.roll.to_radians()) }
    /// Darcy viscous resistance tensor in the global frame: D = R diag(1/K) Rᵀ  [m⁻²].
    pub fn viscous_tensor(&self) -> Mat3 { let r = self.frame(); r * Mat3::from_diagonal(self.permeability.recip()) * r.transpose() }
    pub fn inertial_tensor(&self) -> Mat3 { let r = self.frame(); r * Mat3::from_diagonal(self.c2) * r.transpose() }
    /// Dragging the e1 tip re-derives yaw/pitch so that R·x̂ points at the cursor.
    pub fn aim_e1(&mut self, dir: Vec3) { let d = dir.normalize(); self.pitch = d.y.clamp(-1.0, 1.0).asin().to_degrees(); self.yaw = (-d.z).atan2(d.x).to_degrees(); }
    /// Momentum sink S = −(μ D·u + ½ ρ |u| C·u)
    pub fn source(&self, u: Vec3, mu: f32, rho: f32) -> Vec3 { -(self.viscous_tensor() * u * mu + self.inertial_tensor() * u * (0.5 * rho * u.length())) }
}
pub struct Actors { pub injectors: Vec<SprayInjector>, pub mics: Vec<FwhMic>, pub porous: Vec<PorousZone>, pub rotor_center: Vec3 }
impl Default for Actors {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            injectors: vec![SprayInjector { pos: Vec3::new(-0.12, 0.03, 0.0), axis: Vec3::new(0.988, -0.156, 0.0), half_angle_deg: 14.0, length: 0.09, d32_um: 40.0, spread_n: 2.6, mdot_gs: 2.0, u_inj: 25.0 }],
            mics: (0..3).map(|i| { let th = (45.0 + 45.0 * i as f32).to_radians(); FwhMic { name: format!("MIC-{}", i + 1), pos: Vec3::new(0.025 - th.cos(), th.sin(), 0.0) } }).collect(),
            porous: vec![PorousZone { center: Vec3::new(0.06, -0.095, 0.0), half_extent: Vec3::new(0.02, 0.0125, 0.03), yaw: 0.0, pitch: 0.0, roll: 0.0, permeability: Vec3::new(1e-7, 1e-10, 1e-10), c2: Vec3::new(20.0, 2000.0, 2000.0) }],
            rotor_center: Vec3::new(0.025, 0.0, 0.0),
        }
    }
}
impl Actors {
    pub fn ui(&mut self, ui: &mut egui::Ui) {
        for inj in &mut self.injectors {
            ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut inj.half_angle_deg, 2.0..=45.0).text("θ_cone [°]"));
            ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut inj.d32_um, 5.0..=200.0).text("d32 [µm]"));
            ui.monospace(format!("X̄ = {:.1} µm", inj.x_bar()));
        }
        for m in &self.mics {
            let (s, th) = (m.distance(self.rotor_center), m.theta_deg(self.rotor_center));
            ui.monospace(format!("{} r={s:.2} m θ={th:.0}° {:.1} dB", m.name, gutin_oaspl(32_000.0, 7, 0.0445, 29.3, 0.48, s as f64, th as f64)));
        }
        for p in &mut self.porous {
            ui.add(egui::Slider::new(&mut p.yaw, -180.0..=180.0).text("yaw"));
            let d = p.viscous_tensor();
            ui.monospace(format!("D = {:.2e} {:.2e} {:.2e}\n    {:.2e} {:.2e} {:.2e}\n    {:.2e} {:.2e} {:.2e}", d.x_axis.x, d.y_axis.x, d.z_axis.x, d.x_axis.y, d.y_axis.y, d.z_axis.y, d.x_axis.z, d.y_axis.z, d.z_axis.z));
        }
    }
}
